# Code review: the Entity boss (procedural tentacles + 3D presence)

You are reviewing C++ for **Glyphbound** (Unreal Engine 5.9.0), in `Glyphbound/`. This is a read-only review: do not edit files.

## What to review
Two implementer runs on the final boss, the Entity:
1. Commit 1517e72 (Muse Spark), brief `Prompts/feedback13_entity_procedural.md`: procedural tentacle animation, the `AGBEntity` enemy class, and a smoke step.
2. Uncommitted changes in the working tree (Sonnet), brief `Prompts/feedback14_entity_3d.md`: weighted turning, 3D aim, flight with banking and strafe, a shell hit sphere, energy bolts, and an eye light.

See it all with:
`git diff 1517e72~1 -- Glyphbound/Source Glyphbound/Content/Data`

These files are in scope:
- `AI/GBEntity.*`
- `AI/GBTentacleRigComponent.*`
- `AI/GBTentacleMath.*`
- `AI/GBEnemyCharacter.*` (only the hooks)
- `Combat/GBProjectile.*`
- `Core/GBDevCommands.cpp` (the Entity parts only)
- `Tests/GBEntityTests.cpp`
- `Content/Data/Game.json` and `Enemies.json`

Ignore other chats' changes in `Tools/Blender`, `Devlog` and so on.

## Look for, in order of priority
1. **Correctness bugs:**
   - crashes and null dereferences;
   - wrong maths (FABRIK, springs, quaternion/rotator composition on the bone poses, spaces mixed: component vs world vs bone-local);
   - frame-rate dependence or instability at low FPS;
   - state not reset on death, respawn or era switch;
   - timers or lights left running after `Destroy`.
2. **Regressions for other enemies and projectiles.** `GBProjectile` changes must be inert unless `bEnergyBolt` is set. Check the cost of the two new dormant components on every projectile: is the light registered or ticking when unused?
3. **Collision:**
   - The capsule now ignores `ECC_Camera` and `ECC_WorldDynamic`, and a shell `USphereComponent` was added. Can the player's hitscan, projectiles, melee or spells still damage it?
   - Are there double hits (sphere plus capsule)?
   - Does anything else rely on the capsule being hit? Look at AI perception, encounter logic, pickups and nav.
4. **Performance per tick:** 80 bones, poseable mesh updates, lights. Note anything proportional to the number of loaded objects.
5. **Test quality.** Could each of the three tests in `GBEntityTests.cpp` pass with a wrong implementation?
6. Anything in the smoke code that could make the smoke flaky.

## Output
A numbered list of findings, most severe first. For each one:
- file:line;
- what is wrong;
- a concrete failure scenario;
- the fix in one or two sentences.

Say "no findings" for a category you checked and found clean. Keep it under about 60 lines.

# Task: the Entity's tentacles, animated procedurally (and the Entity as a real enemy class)

Project: **Glyphbound** (Unreal Engine 5.9.0, C++), in `Glyphbound/`. Read `Design/ARCHITECTURE.md` and `Prompts/implementer_rules.md` first. In this task you DO build and run things, and the rest of "Hard rules" still applies:
- One build or run at a time.
- Do not open the Unreal Editor and do not launch PIE.
- Do not commit.
- Do not run `Tools/Jam/Import-*.ps1`, and do not touch `Content/` except `Content/Data/*.json`.

Owner (Spanish, translated): "Let's start the rigging work. Since it has tentacles, it would be good to use a procedural animation system like the ones used for creatures such as snakes or octopuses."

The Entity is the final boss (GDD Â§4 and Â§11). Its phase 1, "the machine", is a floating metal shell with a big central eye and about ten tentacles made of green hologram light. The architect already did the art and the rig. Your job is the code that brings it to life.

## The assets (already in `/Game/Glyphbound/Enemies/Entity/`)
All share one frame: origin at the centre of the shell, the eye looking to +X, up +Z, 1 unit = 1 cm. The model is about 290 cm tall: the shell spans roughly z -89..83 cm, and the tentacles hang down to z -224 cm.

| Asset | What | Material |
|---|---|---|
| `SM_Entity_Body` | the metal shell and the central machinery, rigid | `M_EntityBody`: `Glow`, `GlowColor`, `Flash` (0..1), `FlashGlow` |
| `SM_Entity_Eye` | the big central eye, rigid: **the weak point** | `MI_EntityEye` (same parameters) |
| `SK_Entity_Tentacles` | skeletal mesh, skeleton `SK_Entity_Tentacles_Skeleton`. 81 bones: `root` at the origin, then 10 chains `T00_00`..`T00_07` ... `T09_00`..`T09_07`, each of 8 bones from the root of a tentacle (`_00`, at the shell) to its tip (`_07`), every `_00` parented to `root`. Two-bone linear skinning along each tentacle. | `MI_EntityTentacles` (translucent hologram). Its parameter `SwayAmount` (cm) is a material sway done with world position offset: **set it to 0 on a dynamic instance**, because the bones now do the motion. |

Rig facts (from `Glyphbound/SourceArt/Enemies/Entity/export_report.json`; read the reference pose at runtime, do not hardcode them):
- The chain roots are on the lower half of the shell.
- Chain lengths are about 60..230 cm. Chain `T04` is a short stub of about 60 cm under the body.
- The weak point is a sphere: centre (66.4, 0.1, -13.5) cm, radius 27 cm, in the actor's visual frame. Provisional constants, named.

## 1. Pure math: `AI/GBTentacleMath.h/.cpp` (new, you own them)
Small, testable functions in a namespace `GBTentacle`, with no UObjects. Points are `FVector` in component space.
- `void SolveFabrik(TArray<FVector>& Joints, const FVector& Target, int32 Iterations, float Tolerance)`.
  - Joints[0] is the fixed root, and segment lengths are kept.
  - The usual forward/backward passes.
  - If the target is out of reach, the chain points straight at it.
- `float SpringStep(float& Value, float& Velocity, float Target, float Stiffness, float Damping, float Dt)`: a semi-implicit damped spring, stable for Dt up to 0.1 s (sub-step if needed). It returns the new value.
- `float WaveAngle(float Time, float Frequency, float Phase, float BoneIndex, float WaveLength, float Amplitude, int32 BoneCount)`: a travelling wave, `Amplitude * ((BoneIndex + 1) / BoneCount) * sin(2*pi*(Frequency*Time + Phase) - BoneIndex / WaveLength)`. The angle grows towards the tip.

## 2. The rig driver: `AI/GBTentacleRigComponent.h/.cpp` (new, you own them)
A `UActorComponent` that drives a `UPoseableMeshComponent` showing `SK_Entity_Tentacles`. A poseable mesh is enough here: no AnimBP and no Control Rig assets.
- On setup it reads the reference pose and finds the chains by bone name (`T<cc>_<bb>`), so it does not depend on 10 x 8.
- Every tick it composes four layers into one pose per chain, as rotations on top of each bone's reference local rotation. Keep it cheap: 80 bones per tick.
  1. **Idle wave:** two perpendicular `WaveAngle` bends per bone (pitch and yaw in the bone's frame), with a different random phase per chain, so the tentacles undulate out of step like an octopus floating.
  2. **Inertia / lag:** the actor's velocity and yaw rate, expressed in the actor frame, push a per-chain bend that follows `SpringStep`. When the Entity moves or turns, the tentacles trail behind and then settle. The tips bend most.
  3. **Reach / aim:** `SetReachTarget(FVector WorldTarget, float Weight)` and `ClearReach()`. The chain whose tip is closest to the target, or a chosen one, solves `SolveFabrik` on its joint positions towards the target. The other layers are blended out of that chain by `Weight`, and FABRIK positions are turned back into bone rotations. Used for the attack (below).
  4. **Hit ripple:** `AddRipple(float Strength)` starts a pulse that runs from root to tip along every chain over about 0.5 s and decays.
- `SetLimp(bool)`: on death the waves fade out over about 1 s and the chains droop towards -Z, as if the hologram loses power.
- **Do not let a chain pass through the shell:** the bend of bones `_00` and `_01` is limited. Pick a limit from looking at the result (provisional, named).
- **Every tuning number** goes in `Game.json` "Values" with a default in code, prefixed `EntityTentacle`:
  - wave: frequency, amplitude, wave length;
  - lag: strength, stiffness, damping;
  - root limit;
  - reach: blend in and out times;
  - ripple: strength and duration;
  - limp: droop.
  Start values from looking at the screenshot, and list them in your report.

## 3. The enemy: `AI/GBEntity.h/.cpp` (new, you own them)
`AGBEntity : public AGBEnemyCharacter`. Set `"ActorClass": "GBEntity"` on `Entity_Phase1` in `Enemies.json`, the only data change there. Check how `SpawnEnemy` resolves `ActorClass`, and use the form it expects.
- **InitFromDefinition:** call the base, then build the visual.
  - A scene root offset from the capsule centre, so the tentacle tips clear the floor by about 20 cm.
  - The body and the eye as `UStaticMeshComponent`s (no collision: the capsule is the collision).
  - The tentacles as a `UPoseableMeshComponent`.
  - The rig component.
  - Hide the base class placeholder look.
  - Load the assets by path, as `Weapons/GBGun.cpp` loads the gun meshes. If any is missing, log one warning and keep the placeholder.
- **The Entity floats.** It should hover at its height without gravity: flying movement mode, or whatever is simplest with the existing chase code. It must still reach the player around obstacles as other enemies do. Say in the report what you chose.
- **Body MIDs:** `Flash` 1 on a weak-point hit, fading over 0.3 s. `GlowColor` stays as authored.
- **Weak point:** a hit inside the eye sphere counts as a weak-point hit. `IsHeadshotLocation` is not virtual today. **You may change `AI/GBEnemyCharacter.h/.cpp` only to make `IsHeadshotLocation` virtual, and to give subclasses a protected virtual hook called from `HandleDamaged`** (for example `OnDamagedVisual(const FGBDamageInfo&)`). Nothing else in that file.
- **Attack** (`Entity_Phase1` is ranged):
  - during the windup, the nearest tentacle reaches towards the player (`SetReachTarget` with Weight ramping 0 -> 1);
  - on the shot, it whips back (Weight -> 0 with a short ripple);
  - if the base class lets a subclass choose the projectile's start point cleanly, start the shot at that tentacle's tip; otherwise leave it and say so.
- **Damage:** `AddRipple` on every hit, scaled by the share of max health.
- **Death:** `SetLimp(true)`.

## 4. Dev command and smoke
- `gb.SpawnEntity`, in `Core/GBDevCommands.cpp` (you may edit it for this): spawns `Entity_Phase1` 12 m in front of the player, in the player's current era. This is how the owner will look at it while playing.
- In the smoke run (`gb.Smoke`), add one Entity step at the end, before quitting:
  - spawn it in front of the player and let it idle for 1 s;
  - log `SMOKE entity bones=%d chains=%d tip_moved=%.1f`: the largest movement in cm of any `_07` bone between two moments 0.5 s apart. It must be > 3 (it is alive);
  - set a reach target 60 cm in front of the tip of chain 0, wait 0.6 s, log `SMOKE entity reach error=%.1f` (cm from that tip to the target, which must be < 15), then clear it;
  - log `SMOKE entity weakpoint eye=%d shell=%d`: `IsHeadshotLocation` at the eye centre must be 1, and at the top of the shell 0;
  - screenshot `15a_entity`, framed so the whole Entity is visible;
  - destroy it after the step, so the existing smoke steps are not affected.

## 5. Tests (`Tests/GBEntityTests.cpp`)
Exactly three, pure logic, none of whose names is a prefix of another:
- `Glyphbound.Entity.FabrikReach`: a 5-joint chain with a reachable target. The tip ends within the tolerance, the root does not move, and every segment length is kept within 0.1 cm.
- `Glyphbound.Entity.FabrikTooFar`: an unreachable target. The lengths are kept, and the chain is straight and points at the target (each joint on the root-target line within 0.1 cm).
- `Glyphbound.Entity.SpringSettles`: from 0 to a target of 10 with critical-ish damping, at dt 1/60. Within 1 s it is within 0.1 of the target and never overshoots by more than 5%; with dt 0.1 it stays finite and settles too.

# Task: the Entity must read as a 3D AAA cyberpunk boss, not a sprite

Project: **Glyphbound** (Unreal Engine 5.9.0, C++), in `Glyphbound/`. Read `Design/ARCHITECTURE.md` and `Prompts/implementer_rules.md` first. Hard rules for this task:
- One build or run at a time.
- Do not open the Unreal Editor and do not launch PIE.
- Do not commit.
- Do not run `Tools/Jam/Import-*.ps1`, and do not touch `Content/` except `Content/Data/*.json`.
- **If a problem's cause is outside the files you may edit, stop and report it.** Do not loop on it. The previous run on this enemy spent over an hour on such a problem.
- Other chats share this working tree. If the build fails in a file you did not touch, wait a minute and retry. Do not edit their files.

## Context
The Entity (`AI/GBEntity.h/.cpp`, `AI/GBTentacleRigComponent.*`, `AI/GBTentacleMath.*`, commit 1517e72) is the final boss: a floating metal shell with a big glowing eye and ten green hologram tentacles, animated procedurally. It is a cyberpunk machine from the future.

The owner played it (`gb.SpawnEntity`) and said, translated: "It works like a 2D entity, not at all like 3D. It has to work like a typical triple-A made in Unreal Engine... zero sprite, zero 2D." They confirmed three faults:
1. **Flat shots.** The bolts are small glowing spheres, and they look like 2D sprites.
2. **It always faces you.** It snaps its yaw to the player every frame, like a sprite (`AGBEnemyCharacter::FaceTowards` calls `SetActorRotation`). It never pitches.
3. **It moves flat, with no flight.** It slides at a fixed height and never tilts. Today the capsule stays on the ground and the visual is raised, with a 4 cm bob.

## What to build (all in the Entity's files unless stated)

### A. Turning with weight, and a 3D aim
- **No yaw snapping.** Turn the actor (or the visual root) towards the player at a limited rate with easing: a spring, or a max rate of about 90 deg/s.
  - You may make `FaceTowards` virtual in `GBEnemyCharacter.h` and override it. That one-word change is the only edit allowed in that file.
- **The whole body aims in 3D.** Pitch the visual so the eye points at the player's head, clamped to about Â±30 degrees and smoothed.
- **The eye tracks first, the body follows:** the shell lags behind the aim a little.

### B. Real 3D flight
- **Altitude changes.** The shell's height above the floor drifts between about 2.5 m and 4.5 m, using smooth noise or sines, not a constant bob. It also rises before an attack and dips after one. Keep the tentacle tips off the floor.
- **It banks and pitches into its motion,** like a drone or helicopter:
  - it rolls into lateral velocity and turns;
  - it pitches forward when accelerating and back when braking;
  - all of it is spring-smoothed and clamped to about 20 degrees.
- **It moves sideways like a hunter.** When it is in attack range and has line of sight, it strafes or orbits around the player and changes direction now and then, instead of standing still. Reuse the existing movement and pathing; you choose how.
- **Hits knock it back.** Each hit pushes the visual back and tilts it on a spring, scaled by damage. A weak-point hit is a bigger stagger.
- **Collision must follow the visual.** If the shell floats above the capsule, shots must still hit the shell and the eye where they are drawn. Today the capsule is the only collision, and the weak point is an eye sphere tested on hit location. Make sure:
  - a shot at the drawn eye is a weak-point hit;
  - a shot at the drawn shell is a body hit;
  - shots through empty space under it miss.
  Pick the simplest correct way, for example a sphere on the shell that blocks the weapon trace channel. Say in the report what you chose.

### C. 3D energy bolts
- The Entity's shots must read as **3D energy bolts**, not dots:
  - an elongated glowing core oriented along the velocity, about 60â€“90 cm long;
  - a real `UPointLightComponent` on the bolt, in the projectile colour, lighting the walls and floor as it flies;
  - a short fading trail, or a stretched second mesh.
- Each shot also gets a muzzle flash at the tentacle tip: a brief point light and a flash on the tentacle.
- `Combat/GBProjectile.h/.cpp` may get **additive** options for this, for example a config flag for an energy bolt with stretch and light. Every existing projectile must look and behave exactly as before.

### D. The eye is a real light
- Put a `UPointLightComponent` at the eye, in the eye's colour, with shadows off for cost. It lights the environment in front of the Entity and pulses with the body glow.
- It flares on the windup and on weak-point hits, and fades out on death.

### Numbers
Every tuning number goes in `Game.json` "Values", with a default in code and the prefix `Entity`, as the tentacle values already are. List them in the report.

## Smoke (extend the existing Entity step in `gb.Smoke`)
- Log `SMOKE entity turn max_rate=%.0f`. Put the player behind the Entity, then measure the largest yaw change per second over 0.5 s. It must be â‰¤ 120.
- Log `SMOKE entity flight z_range=%.0f roll_max=%.1f`. Over 2 s, the shell's height range must be > 20 cm. While moving the Entity sideways (set a velocity or a move target), the largest roll must be > 3 degrees.
- Log `SMOKE entity bolt light=%d length=%.0f` for a fired bolt: light 1, length â‰¥ 50.
- Log `SMOKE entity hit eye=%d shell=%d below=%d`, from line traces on the weapon channel:
  - at the drawn eye, 1 (weak point);
  - at the drawn shell top, 1 (body hit, not weak point);
  - 1.5 m below the shell, between the tentacles, 0.
- Take two screenshots:
  - `15a_entity`: front three-quarter view, the whole Entity in frame;
  - `15b_entity_side`: from the side, during a strafe, so the bank is visible, with a bolt in flight if possible.
- Keep the existing `SMOKE entity` lines passing. The step still destroys the Entity at the end.

## Tests
No new tests are required. If you add a pure function (for example a bank or flight helper), add at most one test for it, named under `Glyphbound.Entity.` and not a prefix of an existing name.

## Verification (all must be true when you finish)
1. Build the Editor target: `powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File Tools/Jam/Build-Jam.ps1` gives `Result: Succeeded`.
2. Build the game target: `... Build-Jam.ps1 -Target Glyphbound` gives `Result: Succeeded`.

   - no `missing usage flag` warning.
5. Open both screenshots and look at them. Does it read as a heavy 3D machine, tilted, lit, and casting light? Say what you saw.

  67: 
  68: void FGBEntitySpring::Step(float Target, float Dt, float Omega, float Zeta)
  69: {
  70: 	float Remaining = FMath::Max(Dt, 0.f);
  71: 	while (Remaining > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
  72: 	{
  73: 		const float H = FMath::Min(Remaining, GBEntityLocal::MaxSpringStep);
  74: 		V += (Omega * Omega * (Target - X) - 2.f * Zeta * Omega * V) * H;
  75: 		X += V * H;
  76: 		Remaining -= H;
  77: 	}
  78: }

 225: 	{
 226: 		Move->MaxAcceleration = Tuning.Acceleration;
 227: 		Move->BrakingDecelerationWalking = Tuning.Braking;
 228: 		Move->GroundFriction = Tuning.GroundFriction;
 229: 	}
 230: 	if (ShellHit)
 231: 	{
 232: 		ShellHit->SetSphereRadius(Tuning.ShellRadius);
 233: 	}
 234: 	EyeLightColor = UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(Definition.Strings.FindRef(GBEnemyKey::ProjectileColor), EyeLightColor);
 235: 	BuildVisual();
 236: }
 237: 
 238: void AGBEntity::BuildVisual()
 239: {
 240: 	if (bVisualBuilt)
 241: 	{
 242: 		return;
 243: 	}
 244: 	bVisualBuilt = true;
 245: 
 246: 	UCapsuleComponent* Capsule = GetCapsuleComponent();
 247: 	const float HalfHeight = Capsule ? Capsule->GetUnscaledCapsuleHalfHeight() : 135.f;
 248: 	CapsuleHalfHeightCached = HalfHeight;
 249: 	VisualBaseZ = GBEntityLocal::TentacleTipDepthCm + GBEntityLocal::TipClearanceCm - HalfHeight;
 250: 	VisualRoot->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, 0.f, VisualBaseZ));
 251: 
 252: 	UStaticMesh* BodySM = LoadObject<UStaticMesh>(nullptr, GBEntityLocal::BodyPath, nullptr, LOAD_NoWarn | LOAD_Quiet);
 253: 	UStaticMesh* EyeSM = LoadObject<UStaticMesh>(nullptr, GBEntityLocal::EyePath, nullptr, LOAD_NoWarn | LOAD_Quiet);
 254: 	USkeletalMesh* TentacleSK = LoadObject<USkeletalMesh>(nullptr, GBEntityLocal::TentaclesPath, nullptr, LOAD_NoWarn | LOAD_Quiet);
 255: 	UMaterialInterface* TentacleMI = LoadObject<UMaterialInterface>(nullptr, GBEntityLocal::TentaclesMaterialPath, nullptr, LOAD_NoWarn | LOAD_Quiet);
 256: 	if (!BodySM || !EyeSM || !TentacleSK || !TentacleMI)
 257: 	{
 258: 		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Entity art missing (body=%d eye=%d tentacles=%d material=%d), keeping the placeholder look"),
 259: 			BodySM ? 1 : 0, EyeSM ? 1 : 0, TentacleSK ? 1 : 0, TentacleMI ? 1 : 0);
 260: 		return;
 261: 	}
 262: 
 263: 	EntityBody->SetStaticMesh(BodySM);
 264: 	EntityEye->SetStaticMesh(EyeSM);
 265: 	EntityTentacles->SetSkinnedAssetAndUpdate(TentacleSK, true);
 266: 	// The skeletal mesh ships without a material on its slot: use the authored hologram instance.
 267: 	for (int32 Slot = 0; Slot < EntityTentacles->GetNumMaterials(); ++Slot)
 268: 	{
 269: 		if (EntityTentacles->GetMaterial(Slot) == nullptr)
 270: 		{
 271: 			EntityTentacles->SetMaterial(Slot, TentacleMI);
 272: 		}
 273: 	}
 274: 
 275: 	// Dynamic instances: Flash starts at 0, GlowColor stays as authored. SwayAmount is 0:
 276: 	// the bones now do the motion, the material sway would fight them.
 277: 	for (int32 Slot = 0; Slot < EntityBody->GetNumMaterials(); ++Slot)
 278: 	{
 279: 		if (UMaterialInstanceDynamic* MID = EntityBody->CreateDynamicMaterialInstance(Slot))
 280: 		{
 281: 			MID->SetScalarParameterValue(GBEntityLocal::FlashParam, 0.f);
 282: 			BodyMIDs.Add(MID);
 283: 		}
 284: 	}
 285: 	for (int32 Slot = 0; Slot < EntityEye->GetNumMaterials(); ++Slot)
 286: 	{
 287: 		if (UMaterialInstanceDynamic* MID = EntityEye->CreateDynamicMaterialInstance(Slot))
 288: 		{
 289: 			MID->SetScalarParameterValue(GBEntityLocal::FlashParam, 0.f);
 290: 			EyeMIDs.Add(MID);
 291: 		}
 292: 	}
 293: 	for (int32 Slot = 0; Slot < EntityTentacles->GetNumMaterials(); ++Slot)
 294: 	{
 295: 		if (UMaterialInstanceDynamic* MID = EntityTentacles->CreateDynamicMaterialInstance(Slot))
 296: 		{
 297: 			MID->SetScalarParameterValue(GBEntityLocal::SwayParam, 0.f);
 298: 			if (!TentacleMID)
 299: 			{
 300: 				TentacleMID = MID;
 301: 			}
 302: 		}
 303: 	}
 304: 
 305: 	// The eye light takes the eye material's glow colour when it has one (else the projectile colour).
 306: 	for (UMaterialInstanceDynamic* MID : EyeMIDs)
 307: 	{
 308: 		if (MID)
 309: 		{
 310: 			const FLinearColor Glow = MID->K2_GetVectorParameterValue(FName(TEXT("GlowColor")));
 311: 			if (Glow.R + Glow.G + Glow.B > 0.05f)
 312: 			{
 313: 				EyeLightColor = FLinearColor(Glow.R, Glow.G, Glow.B);
 314: 				break;
 315: 			}
 316: 		}
 317: 	}
 318: 	EyeLight->SetRelativeLocation(GBEntityLocal::EyeCentreCm + FVector(GBEntityLocal::EyeLightForwardCm, 0.f, 0.f));
 319: 	EyeLight->SetLightColor(EyeLightColor);
 320: 	EyeLight->SetAttenuationRadius(Tuning.EyeLightRadius);
 321: 	MuzzleLight->SetLightColor(EyeLightColor);
 322: 	MuzzleLight->SetAttenuationRadius(Tuning.MuzzleRadius);
 323: 
 324: 	// The real look is up: hide the base class placeholder.
 325: 	Sprite->SetVisibility(false);
 326: 	BodyMesh->SetVisibility(false);
 327: 
 328: 	TentacleRig->Setup(EntityTentacles);
 329: }
 330: 
 331: FVector AGBEntity::GetEyeCentreWorld() const
 332: {
 333: 	if (EntityEye)
 334: 	{
 335: 		return EntityEye->GetComponentTransform().TransformPosition(GBEntityLocal::EyeCentreCm);
 336: 	}
 337: 	return GetActorLocation();
 338: }
 339: 
 340: FVector AGBEntity::GetShellTopWorld() const
 341: {
 342: 	if (VisualRoot)
 343: 	{
 344: 		return VisualRoot->GetComponentTransform().TransformPosition(FVector(0.f, 0.f, GBEntityLocal::ShellTopCm));
 345: 	}
 346: 	return GetActorLocation();
 347: }
 348: 
 349: bool AGBEntity::IsHeadshotLocation(const FVector& HitLocation) const
 350: {
 351: 	// The weak point is the drawn eye. The hit sphere stands a little proud of the lens, so the radius has some slack.
 352: 	return FVector::Dist(HitLocation, GetEyeCentreWorld()) <= FMath::Max(Tuning.EyeWeakRadius, GBEntityLocal::EyeRadiusCm);
 353: }
 354: 
 355: FVector AGBEntity::GetAttackOrigin(AActor* Goal) const
 356: {
 357: 	if (TentacleRig && TentacleRig->IsReady() && Goal)
 358: 	{
 359: 		const FVector GoalPos = Goal->GetActorLocation();
 360: 		int32 Best = INDEX_NONE;
 361: 		float BestDist = FLT_MAX;
 362: 		for (int32 Chain = 0; Chain < TentacleRig->GetChainCount(); ++Chain)
 363: 		{
 364: 			const float Dist = FVector::Dist(TentacleRig->GetChainTipWorldPosition(Chain), GoalPos);
 365: 			if (Dist < BestDist)
 366: 			{
 367: 				BestDist = Dist;
 368: 				Best = Chain;
 369: 			}
 370: 		}
 371: 		if (Best != INDEX_NONE)
 372: 		{
 373: 			return TentacleRig->GetChainTipWorldPosition(Best);
 374: 		}
 375: 	}
 376: 	return GetChestLocation();
 377: }
 378: 
 379: FVector AGBEntity::GetGoalHeadLocation(const AActor* Goal) const
 380: {
 381: 	if (const APawn* GoalPawn = Cast<APawn>(Goal))
 382: 	{
 383: 		return GoalPawn->GetPawnViewLocation();
 384: 	}
 385: 	return Goal ? Goal->GetActorLocation() : GetActorLocation();
 386: }
 387: 
 388: AGBProjectile* AGBEntity::FireBolt(AActor* Goal, const FVector& Origin, const FVector& Direction, float SpeedOverride)
 389: {
 390: 	const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
 391: 	if (!Def || !GetWorld())
 392: 	{
 393: 		return nullptr;
 394: 	}
 395: 
 396: 	// Same shot as the base ranged attack, but a 3D energy bolt from a tentacle tip.
 397: 	FGBDamageInfo Info;
 398: 	Info.Amount = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackDamage, 0.f);
 399: 	Info.Element = Def->Element;
 400: 	Info.Instigator = this;
 401: 	Info.Causer = this;
 402: 	Info.SourceId = EnemyId;
 403: 	Info.OnHitEffect = FName(*Def->Strings.FindRef(GBString::OnHitEffect));
 404: 
 405: 	FGBProjectileConfig Config;
 406: 	Config.Damage = Info;
 407: 	Config.Damage.Causer = nullptr;
 408: 	Config.Speed = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileSpeed, Config.Speed);
 409: 	if (SpeedOverride > 0.f)
 410: 	{
 411: 		Config.Speed = SpeedOverride;
 412: 	}
 413: 	Config.Radius = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileRadius, Config.Radius);
 414: 	Config.GravityScale = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileGravity, 0.f);
 415: 	Config.MaxRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AggroRange, 0.f) * GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::ProjectileRangeMult, 1.5f);
 416: 	Config.MaxLifetime = GetGameValue(GBProjectileKey::ProjectileMaxLifetime, Config.MaxLifetime);
 417: 	Config.bPixelArt = false;
 418: 	Config.Color = UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(Def->Strings.FindRef(GBEnemyKey::ProjectileColor),
 419: 		GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(Def->Element)));
 420: 	Config.bEnergyBolt = true;
 421: 	Config.BoltLength = Tuning.BoltLength;
 422: 	Config.BoltThickness = Tuning.BoltThickness;
 423: 	Config.BoltTrailLength = Tuning.BoltTrail;
 424: 	Config.BoltLightIntensity = Tuning.BoltLightIntensity;
 425: 	Config.BoltLightRadius = Tuning.BoltLightRadius;
 426: 
 427: 	AGBProjectile* Bolt = AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(GetWorld(), Config, Origin, Direction, this);
 428: 	LastBolt = Bolt;
 429: 
 430: 	// Muzzle flash: a brief light at the tip that fired.
 431: 	if (MuzzleLight && bVisualBuilt)
 432: 	{
 433: 		MuzzleLight->SetWorldLocation(Origin);
 434: 		MuzzleLight->SetLightColor(Config.Color);
 435: 		MuzzleLight->SetIntensity(Tuning.MuzzleIntensity);
 436: 		MuzzleLight->SetVisibility(true);
 437: 		MuzzleTimer = Tuning.MuzzleTime;
 438: 	}
 439: 
 440: 	// The tentacle whips back: release the reach and ripple it.
 441: 	if (TentacleRig)
 442: 	{
 443: 		TentacleRig->ClearReach();
 444: 		const float Strength = GetGameValue(GBEntityTentacle::RippleStrength, GBEntityTentacle::RippleStrengthDefault);
 445: 		TentacleRig->AddRipple(Strength * GBEntityLocal::ShotRippleShare);
 446: 	}
 447: 	return Bolt;
 448: }
 449: 
 450: AGBProjectile* AGBEntity::FireDebugBolt(const FVector& Direction, float Speed)
 451: {
 452: 	AActor* Goal = FindTarget();
 453: 	if (!Goal)
 454: 	{
 455: 		return nullptr;
 456: 	}
 457: 	return FireBolt(Goal, GetAttackOrigin(Goal), Direction, Speed);
 458: }
 459: 
 460: AGBProjectile* AGBEntity::GetLastBolt() const
 461: {
 462: 	return LastBolt.Get();
 463: }
 464: 
 465: void AGBEntity::PerformAttack()
 466: {
 467: 	AActor* Goal = FindTarget();
 468: 	const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
 469: 	if (!Goal || !Def || !GetWorld())
 470: 	{
 471: 		return;
 472: 	}
 473: 	// After every shot (suppressed ones too, for the smoke run) the shell dips.
 474: 	AltDipTimer = Tuning.AltDipTime;
 475: 	if (bAttackSuppressed)
 476: 	{
 477: 		return;
 478: 	}
 479: 	if (!IsRanged())
 480: 	{
 481: 		Super::PerformAttack();
 482: 		return;
 483: 	}
 484: 
 485: 	const FVector Origin = GetAttackOrigin(Goal);
 486: 	FVector Direction = Goal->GetActorLocation() - Origin;
 487: 	if (Direction.IsNearlyZero())
 488: 	{
 489: 		Direction = GetActorForwardVector();
 490: 	}
 491: 	FireBolt(Goal, Origin, Direction, 0.f);
 492: }
 493: 
 494: void AGBEntity::OnDamagedVisual(const FGBDamageInfo& Info, float ActualDamage)
 495: {
 496: 	const bool bWeak = Info.bHeadshot || IsHeadshotLocation(Info.HitLocation);
 497: 	if (bWeak)
 498: 	{
 499: 		FlashValue = 1.f;
 500: 	}
 501: 	if (State != EGBEnemyState::Dead && bVisualBuilt)
 502: 	{
 503: 		// Knockback: the shell is pushed along the shot and tilts on a spring; a weak-point hit staggers more.
 504: 		const float Share = FMath::Clamp(ActualDamage / Tuning.HitRefDamage, Tuning.HitMinShare, 1.f) * (bWeak ? Tuning.HitWeakMult : 1.f);
 505: 		FVector Dir = Info.HitDirection.GetSafeNormal();
 506: 		if (Dir.IsNearlyZero())
 507: 		{
 508: 			Dir = -GetActorForwardVector();
 509: 		}
 510: 		const FVector Local = GetActorQuat().UnrotateVector(Dir);
 511: 		HitPitchSpring.V += -Local.X * Tuning.HitTiltKick * Share;
 512: 		HitRollSpring.V += Local.Y * Tuning.HitTiltKick * Share;
 513: 		PushX.V += Local.X * Tuning.HitPushKick * Share;
 514: 		PushY.V += Local.Y * Tuning.HitPushKick * Share;
 515: 		PushZ.V += Local.Z * Tuning.HitPushKick * Share;
 516: 	}
 517: 	if (TentacleRig && TentacleRig->IsReady())
 518: 	{
 519: 		const float MaxHealth = Attributes ? Attributes->GetMaxHealth() : 0.f;
 520: 		const float Share = MaxHealth > 0.f
 521: 			? FMath::Clamp(ActualDamage / MaxHealth, GBEntityLocal::MinRippleShare, 1.f)
 522: 			: GBEntityLocal::MinRippleShare;
 523: 		const float Strength = GetGameValue(GBEntityTentacle::RippleStrength, GBEntityTentacle::RippleStrengthDefault);
 524: 		TentacleRig->AddRipple(Strength * Share);
 525: 	}
 526: }
 527: 
 528: void AGBEntity::HandleDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow)
 529: {
 530: 	Super::HandleDeath(KillingBlow);
 531: 	bDying = true;
 532: 	HitPitchSpring.V += GBEntityLocal::DeathPitchKick;
 533: 	HitRollSpring.V += GBEntityLocal::DeathRollKick;
 534: 	if (TentacleRig)
 535: 	{
 536: 		TentacleRig->ClearReach();
 537: 		TentacleRig->SetLimp(true);
 538: 	}
 539: }
 540: 
 541: FString AGBEntity::DescribeMaterials(UMeshComponent* InMesh) const
 542: {
 543: 	FString Out;
 544: 	if (!InMesh)
 545: 	{
 546: 		return Out;
 547: 	}
 548: 	for (int32 Slot = 0; Slot < InMesh->GetNumMaterials(); ++Slot)
 549: 	{
 550: 		if (Slot > 0)
 551: 		{
 552: 			Out += TEXT("|");
 553: 		}
 554: 		if (UMaterialInterface* Material = InMesh->GetMaterial(Slot))
 555: 		{
 556: 			Out += Material->GetName();
 557: 		}
 558: 		else
 559: 		{
 560: 			Out += TEXT("null");
 561: 		}
 562: 	}
 563: 	return Out;
 564: }
 565: 
 566: FString AGBEntity::DescribeVisual() const
 567: {
 568: 	const FString BodyState = EntityBody
 569: 		? FString::Printf(TEXT("mesh=%s vis=%d"), EntityBody->GetStaticMesh() ? *EntityBody->GetStaticMesh()->GetName() : TEXT("null"), EntityBody->IsVisible() ? 1 : 0)
 570: 		: TEXT("null");
 571: 	const FString EyeState = EntityEye
 572: 		? FString::Printf(TEXT("mesh=%s vis=%d"), EntityEye->GetStaticMesh() ? *EntityEye->GetStaticMesh()->GetName() : TEXT("null"), EntityEye->IsVisible() ? 1 : 0)
 573: 		: TEXT("null");
 574: 	const FString TentState = EntityTentacles
 575: 		? FString::Printf(TEXT("mesh=%s vis=%d slots=%d mats=[%s] bounds=%s"), EntityTentacles->GetSkinnedAsset() ? *EntityTentacles->GetSkinnedAsset()->GetName() : TEXT("null"),
 576: 			EntityTentacles->IsVisible() ? 1 : 0, EntityTentacles->GetNumMaterials(), *DescribeMaterials(EntityTentacles),
 577: 			*EntityTentacles->Bounds.BoxExtent.ToCompactString())
 578: 		: TEXT("null");
 579: 	const FString RootState = VisualRoot
 580: 		? FString::Printf(TEXT("rel=%s"), *VisualRoot->GetRelativeLocation().ToCompactString())
 581: 		: TEXT("null");
 582: 	const FString RigState = TentacleRig && TentacleRig->IsReady()
 583: 		? FString::Printf(TEXT("chains=%d tip0=%s"), TentacleRig->GetChainCount(), *TentacleRig->GetChainTipWorldPosition(0).ToCompactString())
 584: 		: TEXT("notready");
 585: 	return FString::Printf(TEXT("built=%d loc=%s %s body=[%s] eye=[%s] tent=[%s] rig=[%s]"),
 586: 		bVisualBuilt ? 1 : 0, *GetActorLocation().ToCompactString(), *RootState, *BodyState, *EyeState, *TentState, *RigState);
 587: }
 588: 
 589: void AGBEntity::ApplyFlash()
 590: {
 591: 	for (UMaterialInstanceDynamic* MID : BodyMIDs)
 592: 	{
 593: 		if (MID)
 594: 		{
 595: 			MID->SetScalarParameterValue(GBEntityLocal::FlashParam, FlashValue);
 596: 		}
 597: 	}
 598: 	for (UMaterialInstanceDynamic* MID : EyeMIDs)
 599: 	{
 600: 		if (MID)
 601: 		{
 602: 			MID->SetScalarParameterValue(GBEntityLocal::FlashParam, FlashValue);
 603: 		}
 604: 	}
 605: }
 606: 
 607: void AGBEntity::ResetMotionStats()
 608: {
 609: 	PeakYawRate = 0.f;
 610: 	ShellZMin = 1e9f;
 611: 	ShellZMax = -1e9f;
 612: 	PeakRoll = 0.f;
 613: }
 614: 
 615: void AGBEntity::UpdateTurn(float Dt, const AActor* Goal)
 616: {
 617: 	const FVector ToGoal = (Goal->GetActorLocation() - GetActorLocation()).GetSafeNormal2D();
 618: 	if (ToGoal.IsNearlyZero())
 619: 	{
 620: 		YawRateDeg = FMath::FInterpTo(YawRateDeg, 0.f, Dt, Tuning.TurnAccel);
 621: 		return;
 622: 	}
 623: 	const float Yaw = GetActorRotation().Yaw;
 624: 	const float Error = FMath::FindDeltaAngleDegrees(Yaw, ToGoal.Rotation().Yaw);
 625: 	// Aim for a rate proportional to the error (it slows into the target), capped, and reached with inertia.
 626: 	const float Desired = FMath::Clamp(Error * Tuning.TurnGain, -Tuning.TurnMaxRate, Tuning.TurnMaxRate);
 627: 	YawRateDeg = FMath::Clamp(FMath::FInterpTo(YawRateDeg, Desired, Dt, Tuning.TurnAccel), -Tuning.TurnMaxRate, Tuning.TurnMaxRate);
 628: 	float Step = YawRateDeg * Dt;
 629: 	if (FMath::Abs(Step) > FMath::Abs(Error) && FMath::Sign(Step) == FMath::Sign(Error))
 630: 	{
 631: 		Step = Error;
 632: 	}
 633: 	SetActorRotation(FRotator(0.f, Yaw + Step, 0.f));
 634: }
 635: 
 636: void AGBEntity::UpdateStrafe(float Dt, const AActor* Goal)
 637: {
 638: 	StrafeTimer -= Dt;
 639: 	if (StrafeTimer <= 0.f)
 640: 	{
 641: 		StrafeSign = -StrafeSign;
 642: 		StrafeTimer = FMath::FRandRange(Tuning.StrafeFlipMin, Tuning.StrafeFlipMax);
 643: 	}
 644: 
 645: 	FVector ToGoal = Goal->GetActorLocation() - GetActorLocation();
 646: 	ToGoal.Z = 0.f;
 647: 	const float Distance = ToGoal.Size();
 648: 	if (Distance < 1.f)
 649: 	{
 650: 		return;
 651: 	}
 652: 	ToGoal /= Distance;
 653: 	// Orbit the target, drifting towards a preferred range (a share of the attack range) while doing it.
 654: 	const float AttackRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackRange, 0.f);
 655: 	const float Keep = FMath::Max(AttackRange * Tuning.StrafeKeep, 100.f);
 656: 	const float Radial = FMath::Clamp((Distance - Keep) / (Keep * 0.5f), -1.f, 1.f);
 657: 	const FVector Side(-ToGoal.Y * StrafeSign, ToGoal.X * StrafeSign, 0.f);
 658: 	const FVector Dir = (Side + ToGoal * Radial * 0.6f).GetSafeNormal();
 659: 	if (IsDirectionBlocked(Dir, Tuning.StrafeProbe, Goal))
 660: 	{
 661: 		// A wall or a ledge that way: go the other way round for a while.
 662: 		StrafeSign = -StrafeSign;
 663: 		StrafeTimer = FMath::FRandRange(Tuning.StrafeFlipMin, Tuning.StrafeFlipMax);
 664: 		return;
 665: 	}
 666: 	AddMovementInput(Dir, Tuning.StrafeSpeed);
 667: 	bStrafingLastTick = true;
 668: }
 669: 
 670: void AGBEntity::UpdateFlight(float Dt, const AActor* Goal)
 671: {
 672: 	const UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement();
 673: 	const float Speed = Move ? FMath::Max(Move->MaxWalkSpeed, 1.f) : 1.f;
 674: 	const float AccelRef = Move ? FMath::Max(Move->MaxAcceleration, 1.f) : 1.f;
 675: 	const FVector LocalVel = GetActorQuat().UnrotateVector(GetVelocity());
 676: 
 677: 	// Altitude of the shell centre above the floor: a slow drift, a rise in the windup, a dip after a shot.
 678: 	const float Mid = 0.5f * (Tuning.AltMin + Tuning.AltMax);
 679: 	const float W = 2.f * PI * Tuning.AltDriftHz * BobTime;
 680: 	const float Drift = Tuning.AltDriftAmp * (0.65f * FMath::Sin(W + 0.4f) + 0.35f * FMath::Sin(2.1f * W + 1.9f));
 681: 	AltDipTimer = FMath::Max(0.f, AltDipTimer - Dt);
 682: 	const bool bWindup = GetState() == EGBEnemyState::Windup;
 683: 	WindupAlpha = FMath::FInterpTo(WindupAlpha, (bWindup && !bDying) ? 1.f : 0.f, Dt, GBEntityLocal::WindupAlphaRate);
 684: 	float AltTarget = FMath::Clamp(Mid + Drift + Tuning.AltRise * WindupAlpha - Tuning.AltDip * (AltDipTimer / Tuning.AltDipTime),
 685: 		Tuning.AltMin, Tuning.AltMax);
 686: 	float AltOmega = Tuning.AltOmega;
 687: 	if (bDying)
 688: 	{
 689: 		AltTarget = GBEntityLocal::DeathAltitudeCm;
 690: 		AltOmega = GBEntityLocal::DeathAltOmega;
 691: 	}
 692: 	if (!bAltInitialised)
 693: 	{
 694: 		AltSpring.X = AltTarget;
 695: 		bAltInitialised = true;
 696: 	}
 697: 	AltSpring.Step(AltTarget, Dt, AltOmega, 0.9f);
 698: 
 699: 	// Aim: the whole body pitches at the target's head (clamped, smoothed); the eye turns a little ahead of it.
 700: 	const FVector ShellCentre = VisualRoot->GetComponentLocation();
 701: 	float RawPitch = 0.f;
 702: 	float YawError = 0.f;
 703: 	if (Goal && !bDying)
 704: 	{
 705: 		const FVector ToHead = GetGoalHeadLocation(Goal) - ShellCentre;
 706: 		RawPitch = FMath::RadiansToDegrees(FMath::Atan2(ToHead.Z, FMath::Max(ToHead.Size2D(), 1.f)));
 707: 		YawError = FMath::FindDeltaAngleDegrees(GetActorRotation().Yaw, ToHead.Rotation().Yaw);
 708: 	}
 709: 	AimPitchSpring.Step(FMath::Clamp(RawPitch, -Tuning.PitchMax, Tuning.PitchMax), Dt, Tuning.AimOmega, 0.9f);
 710: 	EyePitch = FMath::FInterpTo(EyePitch, FMath::Clamp(RawPitch - AimPitchSpring.X, -Tuning.EyeTrackMax, Tuning.EyeTrackMax), Dt, Tuning.EyeTrackSpeed);
 711: 	EyeYaw = FMath::FInterpTo(EyeYaw, FMath::Clamp(YawError, -Tuning.EyeTrackMax, Tuning.EyeTrackMax), Dt, Tuning.EyeTrackSpeed);
 712: 
 713: 	// Banking like a drone: roll into sideways speed and turns, pitch forward when speeding up, back when braking.
 714: 	const float FwdAccel = Dt > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER ? (LocalVel.X - PrevLocalFwd) / Dt : 0.f;
 715: 	PrevLocalFwd = LocalVel.X;
 716: 	FwdAccelSmoothed = FMath::FInterpTo(FwdAccelSmoothed, FwdAccel, Dt, 6.f);
 717: 	const float RollTarget = FMath::Clamp(LocalVel.Y / Speed * Tuning.BankMax + YawRateDeg * Tuning.BankPerYawRate, -Tuning.BankMax, Tuning.BankMax);
 718: 	const float PitchTarget = FMath::Clamp(-(LocalVel.X / Speed) * Tuning.LeanDeg - (FwdAccelSmoothed / AccelRef) * Tuning.AccelPitchDeg,
 719: 		-Tuning.BankMax, Tuning.BankMax);
 720: 	BankRollSpring.Step(RollTarget, Dt, Tuning.TiltOmega, Tuning.TiltZeta);
 721: 	MotionPitchSpring.Step(PitchTarget, Dt, Tuning.TiltOmega, Tuning.TiltZeta);
 722: 
 723: 	// Knockback springs (underdamped, so a hit wobbles).
 724: 	HitPitchSpring.Step(0.f, Dt, Tuning.HitOmega, Tuning.HitZeta);
 725: 	HitRollSpring.Step(0.f, Dt, Tuning.HitOmega, Tuning.HitZeta);
 726: 	PushX.Step(0.f, Dt, Tuning.HitOmega, Tuning.HitZeta);
 727: 	PushY.Step(0.f, Dt, Tuning.HitOmega, Tuning.HitZeta);
 728: 	PushZ.Step(0.f, Dt, Tuning.HitOmega, Tuning.HitZeta);
 729: 
 730: 	const float Pitch = AimPitchSpring.X + MotionPitchSpring.X + HitPitchSpring.X;
 731: 	const float Roll = BankRollSpring.X + HitRollSpring.X;
 732: 	VisualRoot->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(PushX.X, PushY.X, AltSpring.X - CapsuleHalfHeightCached + PushZ.X));
 733: 	VisualRoot->SetRelativeRotation(FRotator(Pitch, 0.f, Roll));
 734: 	EntityEye->SetRelativeRotation(FRotator(EyePitch, EyeYaw, 0.f));
 735: 
 736: 	const float ShellZ = VisualRoot->GetComponentLocation().Z;
 737: 	ShellZMin = FMath::Min(ShellZMin, ShellZ);
 738: 	ShellZMax = FMath::Max(ShellZMax, ShellZ);
 739: 	PeakRoll = FMath::Max(PeakRoll, FMath::Abs(Roll));
 740: }
 741: 
 742: void AGBEntity::UpdateLights(float Dt)
 743: {
 744: 	if (bDying)
 745: 	{
 746: 		DeathAlpha = FMath::Max(0.f, DeathAlpha - Dt / Tuning.EyeLightFade);
 747: 	}
 748: 	const float Pulse = 1.f + Tuning.EyeLightPulse * FMath::Sin(2.f * PI * Tuning.EyeLightPulseHz * BobTime);
 749: 	const float Flare = 1.f + Tuning.EyeLightWindupFlare * WindupAlpha + Tuning.EyeLightHitFlare * FlashValue;
 750: 	const float Intensity = Tuning.EyeLightIntensity * Pulse * Flare * DeathAlpha;
 751: 	EyeLight->SetIntensity(Intensity);
 752: 	EyeLight->SetVisibility(Intensity > 1.f);
 753: 
 754: 	if (MuzzleTimer > 0.f)
 755: 	{
 756: 		MuzzleTimer = FMath::Max(0.f, MuzzleTimer - Dt);
 757: 		MuzzleLight->SetIntensity(Tuning.MuzzleIntensity * (MuzzleTimer / Tuning.MuzzleTime));
 758: 		if (MuzzleTimer <= 0.f)
 759: 		{
 760: 			MuzzleLight->SetVisibility(false);
 761: 		}
 762: 	}
 763: }
 764: 
 765: void AGBEntity::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
 766: {
 767: 	ReadTuning();
 768: 	UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement();
 769: 	const FVector VelocityBefore = Move ? Move->Velocity : FVector::ZeroVector;
 770: 	const bool bWasStrafing = bStrafingLastTick;
 771: 
 772: 	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
 773: 
 774: 	// The base state machine stops the body at each windup; a strafe keeps its momentum through that.
 775: 	if (bWasStrafing && Move && !IsDead() && Move->Velocity.IsNearlyZero())
 776: 	{
 777: 		Move->Velocity = VelocityBefore;
 778: 	}
 779: 
 780: 	const float Dt = FMath::Min(DeltaSeconds, 0.05f);
 781: 	BobTime += Dt;
 782: 
 783: 	if (FlashValue > 0.f)
 784: 	{
 785: 		FlashValue = FMath::Max(0.f, FlashValue - DeltaSeconds / GBEntityLocal::FlashFadeTime);
 786: 		ApplyFlash();
 787: 	}
 788: 
 789: 	AActor* Goal = FindTarget();
 790: 	const bool bPresent = !GetEraComponent() || GetEraComponent()->IsPresent();
 791: 	const bool bFrozen = GetControlLockTime() > 0.f;
 792: 	const bool bAlive = !IsDead();
 793: 	const bool bActive = bVisualBuilt && bAlive && Goal && bPresent && !bFrozen;
 794: 
 795: 	if (bActive)
 796: 	{
 797: 		UpdateTurn(Dt, Goal);
 798: 	}
 799: 	else
 800: 	{
 801: 		YawRateDeg = FMath::FInterpTo(YawRateDeg, 0.f, Dt, Tuning.TurnAccel);
 802: 	}
 803: 	PeakYawRate = FMath::Max(PeakYawRate, FMath::Abs(YawRateDeg));
 804: 
 805: 	// Strafe: in range and in sight the Entity circles the player instead of standing still.
 806: 	bStrafingLastTick = false;
 807: 	if (ForcedStrafeTime > 0.f)
 808: 	{
 809: 		ForcedStrafeTime -= DeltaSeconds;
 810: 	}
 811: 	if (bActive)
 812: 	{
 813: 		const float AttackRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackRange, 0.f);
 814: 		const bool bFighting = !bAttackSuppressed && AttackRange > 0.f
 815: 			&& (GetState() == EGBEnemyState::Windup || GetState() == EGBEnemyState::Recover)
 816: 			&& FVector::Dist(GetActorLocation(), Goal->GetActorLocation()) <= AttackRange && HasLineOfSight(Goal);
 817: 		if (ForcedStrafeTime > 0.f || bFighting)
 818: 		{
 819: 			UpdateStrafe(DeltaSeconds, Goal);
 820: 		}
 821: 	}
 822: 
 823: 	if (bVisualBuilt)
 824: 	{
 825: 		UpdateFlight(Dt, bAlive ? Goal : nullptr);
 826: 		UpdateLights(Dt);
 827: 	}
 828: 
 829: 	if (bAttackSuppressed)
 830: 	{
 831: 		if (ForcedStrafeTime <= 0.f)
 832: 		{
 833: 			StopMoving();
 834: 		}
 835: 		return;
 836: 	}
 837: 
 838: 	// During the windup the nearest tentacle reaches towards the player.
 839: 	if (GetState() == EGBEnemyState::Windup && TentacleRig && TentacleRig->IsReady() && Goal)
 840: 	{
 841: 		TentacleRig->SetReachTarget(Goal->GetActorLocation(), 1.f);
 842: 	}
 843: }
 844: 
 845: FVector AGBEntity::GetShellCentreWorld() const
 846: {
 847: 	return VisualRoot ? VisualRoot->GetComponentLocation() : GetActorLocation();
 848: }

  29: // Named (not anonymous) so unity builds cannot collide with another file's helpers.
  30: namespace GBEntityLocal
  31: {
  32: 	// Visual frame (export_report.json, SourceArt/Enemies/Entity): origin at the shell centre,
  33: 	// eye looking to +X, up +Z, 1 unit = 1 cm. Provisional, from the art drop.
  34: 	const FVector EyeCentreCm(66.4f, 0.1f, -13.5f);
  35: 	constexpr float EyeRadiusCm = 27.f;
  36: 	constexpr float ShellTopCm = 83.f;          // visual-frame Z of the top of the shell
  37: 	constexpr float TentacleTipDepthCm = 224.f; // how far the tentacles hang below the origin
  38: 	constexpr float TipClearanceCm = 20.f;      // floor clearance of the lowest tip
  39: 
  40: 	const TCHAR* BodyPath = TEXT("/Game/Glyphbound/Enemies/Entity/SM_Entity_Body.SM_Entity_Body");
  41: 	const TCHAR* EyePath = TEXT("/Game/Glyphbound/Enemies/Entity/SM_Entity_Eye.SM_Entity_Eye");
  42: 	const TCHAR* TentaclesPath = TEXT("/Game/Glyphbound/Enemies/Entity/SK_Entity_Tentacles.SK_Entity_Tentacles");
  43: 	const TCHAR* TentaclesMaterialPath = TEXT("/Game/Glyphbound/Enemies/Entity/MI_EntityTentacles.MI_EntityTentacles");
  44: 
  45: 	const FName FlashParam(TEXT("Flash"));
  46: 	const FName SwayParam(TEXT("SwayAmount"));
  47: 
  48: 	// Weak-point flash: full for an instant, then a linear fade over this long. Visual timing.
  49: 	constexpr float FlashFadeTime = 0.3f;
  50: 	// Whip-back ripple after the shot, as a share of the tuning ripple strength.
  51: 	constexpr float ShotRippleShare = 0.5f;
  52: 	// Smallest ripple on any hit, as a share of max health, so chip damage still shivers the hologram.
  53: 	constexpr float MinRippleShare = 0.05f;
  54: 
  55: 	// Longest spring sub-step, s (a long frame cannot blow a spring up).
  56: 	constexpr float MaxSpringStep = 1.f / 60.f;
  57: 	// How fast the windup flag eases in and out (1/s); it drives the rise and the eye flare.
  58: 	constexpr float WindupAlphaRate = 6.f;
  59: 	// On death the shell tips forward and rolls as it drops to this height (cm above the floor).
  60: 	constexpr float DeathPitchKick = -140.f;
  61: 	constexpr float DeathRollKick = 70.f;
  62: 	constexpr float DeathAltitudeCm = 150.f;
  63: 	constexpr float DeathAltOmega = 2.f;
  64: 	// The eye light sits this far in front of the lens, cm.
  65: 	constexpr float EyeLightForwardCm = 110.f;
  66: }
  67: 
  68: void FGBEntitySpring::Step(float Target, float Dt, float Omega, float Zeta)
  69: {
  70: 	float Remaining = FMath::Max(Dt, 0.f);
  71: 	while (Remaining > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
  72: 	{
  73: 		const float H = FMath::Min(Remaining, GBEntityLocal::MaxSpringStep);
  74: 		V += (Omega * Omega * (Target - X) - 2.f * Zeta * Omega * V) * H;
  75: 		X += V * H;
  76: 		Remaining -= H;
  77: 	}
  78: }

  80: AGBEntity::AGBEntity()
  81: {
  82: 	VisualRoot = CreateDefaultSubobject<USceneComponent>(TEXT("EntityVisual"));
  83: 	VisualRoot->SetupAttachment(GetCapsuleComponent());
  84: 
  85: 	EntityBody = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("EntityBody"));
  86: 	EntityBody->SetupAttachment(VisualRoot);
  87: 
  88: 	EntityEye = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("EntityEye"));
  89: 	EntityEye->SetupAttachment(VisualRoot);
  90: 
  91: 	EntityTentacles = CreateDefaultSubobject<UPoseableMeshComponent>(TEXT("EntityTentacles"));
  92: 	EntityTentacles->SetupAttachment(VisualRoot);
  93: 
  94: 	TentacleRig = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBTentacleRigComponent>(TEXT("EntityTentacleRig"));
  95: 
  96: 	// The shell is what weapons hit: guns trace the camera channel, projectiles overlap pawns.
  97: 	ShellHit = CreateDefaultSubobject<USphereComponent>(TEXT("EntityShellHit"));
  98: 	ShellHit->SetupAttachment(VisualRoot);
  99: 	ShellHit->InitSphereRadius(FGBEntityTuning().ShellRadius);
 100: 	ShellHit->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::QueryOnly);
 101: 	ShellHit->SetCollisionObjectType(ECC_Pawn);
 102: 	ShellHit->SetCollisionResponseToAllChannels(ECR_Ignore);
 103: 	ShellHit->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Camera, ECR_Block);
 104: 	ShellHit->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Visibility, ECR_Block);
 105: 	ShellHit->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_WorldDynamic, ECR_Overlap);
 106: 	ShellHit->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(true);
 107: 	ShellHit->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
 108: 
 109: 	EyeLight = CreateDefaultSubobject<UPointLightComponent>(TEXT("EntityEyeLight"));
 110: 	EyeLight->SetupAttachment(EntityEye);
 111: 	EyeLight->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
 112: 	EyeLight->CastShadows = false;
 113: 	EyeLight->IntensityUnits = ELightUnits::Candelas;
 114: 	EyeLight->Intensity = 0.f;
 115: 
 116: 	MuzzleLight = CreateDefaultSubobject<UPointLightComponent>(TEXT("EntityMuzzleLight"));
 117: 	MuzzleLight->SetupAttachment(VisualRoot);
 118: 	MuzzleLight->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
 119: 	MuzzleLight->CastShadows = false;
 120: 	MuzzleLight->IntensityUnits = ELightUnits::Candelas;
 121: 	MuzzleLight->Intensity = 0.f;
 122: 	MuzzleLight->SetVisibility(false);
 123: 
 124: 	for (UStaticMeshComponent* Part : { EntityBody.Get(), EntityEye.Get() })
 125: 	{
 126: 		Part->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
 127: 		Part->SetCollisionProfileName(UCollisionProfile::NoCollision_ProfileName);
 128: 		Part->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
 129: 		Part->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
 130: 	}
 131: 	EntityTentacles->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
 132: 	EntityTentacles->SetCollisionProfileName(UCollisionProfile::NoCollision_ProfileName);
 133: 	EntityTentacles->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
 134: 	EntityTentacles->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
 135: 
 136: 	// The capsule only walks and blocks pawns; it must not catch shots meant for the empty space under the shell.
 137: 	if (UCapsuleComponent* Capsule = GetCapsuleComponent())
 138: 	{
 139: 		Capsule->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Camera, ECR_Ignore);
 140: 		Capsule->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_WorldDynamic, ECR_Ignore);
 141: 	}
 142: 	// The turn is ours (rate-limited in Tick), not the movement component's.
 143: 	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement())
 144: 	{
 145: 		Move->bOrientRotationToMovement = false;
 146: 	}
 147: }

 149: void AGBEntity::BeginPlay()
 150: {
 151: 	Super::BeginPlay();
 152: 	StrafeSign = FMath::RandBool() ? 1.f : -1.f;
 153: 	StrafeTimer = 1.f;
 154: }

 156: void AGBEntity::ReadTuning()
 157: {
 158: 	FGBEntityTuning& T = Tuning;
 159: 	auto V = [this](const TCHAR* Name, float Default) { return GetGameValue(FName(Name), Default); };
 160: 	T.TurnMaxRate = V(TEXT("EntityTurnMaxRate"), 90.f);
 161: 	T.TurnGain = V(TEXT("EntityTurnGain"), 3.f);
 162: 	T.TurnAccel = V(TEXT("EntityTurnAccel"), 5.f);
 163: 	T.PitchMax = V(TEXT("EntityPitchMax"), 30.f);
 164: 	T.AimOmega = V(TEXT("EntityAimOmega"), 6.f);
 165: 	T.EyeTrackMax = V(TEXT("EntityEyeTrackMax"), 12.f);
 166: 	T.EyeTrackSpeed = V(TEXT("EntityEyeTrackSpeed"), 14.f);
 167: 	T.AltMin = V(TEXT("EntityAltMin"), 250.f);
 168: 	T.AltMax = FMath::Max(T.AltMin, V(TEXT("EntityAltMax"), 450.f));
 169: 	T.AltDriftAmp = V(TEXT("EntityAltDriftAmp"), 70.f);
 170: 	T.AltDriftHz = V(TEXT("EntityAltDriftHz"), 0.22f);
 171: 	T.AltRise = V(TEXT("EntityAltRise"), 60.f);
 172: 	T.AltDip = V(TEXT("EntityAltDip"), 45.f);
 173: 	T.AltDipTime = FMath::Max(V(TEXT("EntityAltDipTime"), 0.7f), 0.05f);
 174: 	T.AltOmega = V(TEXT("EntityAltOmega"), 3.f);
 175: 	T.BankMax = V(TEXT("EntityBankMax"), 20.f);
 176: 	T.BankPerYawRate = V(TEXT("EntityBankPerYawRate"), 0.07f);
 177: 	T.LeanDeg = V(TEXT("EntityLeanDeg"), 10.f);
 178: 	T.AccelPitchDeg = V(TEXT("EntityAccelPitchDeg"), 8.f);
 179: 	T.TiltOmega = V(TEXT("EntityTiltOmega"), 7.f);
 180: 	T.TiltZeta = V(TEXT("EntityTiltZeta"), 0.75f);
 181: 	T.Acceleration = V(TEXT("EntityAcceleration"), 700.f);
 182: 	T.Braking = V(TEXT("EntityBraking"), 600.f);
 183: 	T.GroundFriction = V(TEXT("EntityGroundFriction"), 2.f);
 184: 	T.StrafeSpeed = V(TEXT("EntityStrafeSpeed"), 0.7f);
 185: 	T.StrafeFlipMin = V(TEXT("EntityStrafeFlipMin"), 1.8f);
 186: 	T.StrafeFlipMax = FMath::Max(T.StrafeFlipMin, V(TEXT("EntityStrafeFlipMax"), 4.f));
 187: 	T.StrafeKeep = V(TEXT("EntityStrafeKeep"), 0.5f);
 188: 	T.StrafeProbe = V(TEXT("EntityStrafeProbe"), 220.f);
 189: 	T.HitTiltKick = V(TEXT("EntityHitTiltKick"), 90.f);
 190: 	T.HitPushKick = V(TEXT("EntityHitPushKick"), 220.f);
 191: 	T.HitRefDamage = FMath::Max(V(TEXT("EntityHitRefDamage"), 100.f), 1.f);
 192: 	T.HitMinShare = V(TEXT("EntityHitMinShare"), 0.15f);
 193: 	T.HitWeakMult = V(TEXT("EntityHitWeakMult"), 2.f);
 194: 	T.HitOmega = V(TEXT("EntityHitOmega"), 8.f);
 195: 	T.HitZeta = V(TEXT("EntityHitZeta"), 0.3f);
 196: 	T.ShellRadius = V(TEXT("EntityShellRadius"), 88.f);
 197: 	T.EyeWeakRadius = V(TEXT("EntityEyeWeakRadius"), 40.f);
 198: 	T.EyeLightIntensity = V(TEXT("EntityEyeLightIntensity"), 1500.f);
 199: 	T.EyeLightRadius = V(TEXT("EntityEyeLightRadius"), 1600.f);
 200: 	T.EyeLightPulseHz = V(TEXT("EntityEyeLightPulseHz"), 0.7f);
 201: 	T.EyeLightPulse = V(TEXT("EntityEyeLightPulse"), 0.15f);
 202: 	T.EyeLightWindupFlare = V(TEXT("EntityEyeLightWindupFlare"), 0.8f);
 203: 	T.EyeLightHitFlare = V(TEXT("EntityEyeLightHitFlare"), 2.f);
 204: 	T.EyeLightFade = FMath::Max(V(TEXT("EntityEyeLightFade"), 0.8f), 0.05f);
 205: 	T.BoltLength = V(TEXT("EntityBoltLength"), 80.f);
 206: 	T.BoltThickness = V(TEXT("EntityBoltThickness"), 12.f);
 207: 	T.BoltTrail = V(TEXT("EntityBoltTrail"), 240.f);

 210: 	T.MuzzleIntensity = V(TEXT("EntityMuzzleIntensity"), 15000.f);
 211: 	T.MuzzleRadius = V(TEXT("EntityMuzzleRadius"), 700.f);
 212: 	T.MuzzleTime = FMath::Max(V(TEXT("EntityMuzzleTime"), 0.12f), 0.02f);

 215: void AGBEntity::FaceTowards(const AActor* Goal)

 220: void AGBEntity::InitFromDefinition(const FGBEnemyDefinition& Definition)
 221: {
 222: 	Super::InitFromDefinition(Definition);
 223: 	ReadTuning();
 224: 	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement())
 225: 	{
 226: 		Move->MaxAcceleration = Tuning.Acceleration;
 227: 		Move->BrakingDecelerationWalking = Tuning.Braking;
 228: 		Move->GroundFriction = Tuning.GroundFriction;
 229: 	}
 230: 	if (ShellHit)
 231: 	{
 232: 		ShellHit->SetSphereRadius(Tuning.ShellRadius);
 233: 	}
 234: 	EyeLightColor = UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(Definition.Strings.FindRef(GBEnemyKey::ProjectileColor), EyeLightColor);
 235: 	BuildVisual();
 236: }
 237: 
 238: void AGBEntity::BuildVisual()
 239: {
 240: 	if (bVisualBuilt)
 241: 	{
 242: 		return;
 243: 	}
 244: 	bVisualBuilt = true;
 245: 
 246: 	UCapsuleComponent* Capsule = GetCapsuleComponent();
 247: 	const float HalfHeight = Capsule ? Capsule->GetUnscaledCapsuleHalfHeight() : 135.f;
 248: 	CapsuleHalfHeightCached = HalfHeight;
 249: 	VisualBaseZ = GBEntityLocal::TentacleTipDepthCm + GBEntityLocal::TipClearanceCm - HalfHeight;
 250: 	VisualRoot->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, 0.f, VisualBaseZ));
 251: 
 252: 	UStaticMesh* BodySM = LoadObject<UStaticMesh>(nullptr, GBEntityLocal::BodyPath, nullptr, LOAD_NoWarn | LOAD_Quiet);
 253: 	UStaticMesh* EyeSM = LoadObject<UStaticMesh>(nullptr, GBEntityLocal::EyePath, nullptr, LOAD_NoWarn | LOAD_Quiet);
 254: 	USkeletalMesh* TentacleSK = LoadObject<USkeletalMesh>(nullptr, GBEntityLocal::TentaclesPath, nullptr, LOAD_NoWarn | LOAD_Quiet);
 255: 	UMaterialInterface* TentacleMI = LoadObject<UMaterialInterface>(nullptr, GBEntityLocal::TentaclesMaterialPath, nullptr, LOAD_NoWarn | LOAD_Quiet);
 256: 	if (!BodySM || !EyeSM || !TentacleSK || !TentacleMI)
 257: 	{
 258: 		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Entity art missing (body=%d eye=%d tentacles=%d material=%d), keeping the placeholder look"),
 259: 			BodySM ? 1 : 0, EyeSM ? 1 : 0, TentacleSK ? 1 : 0, TentacleMI ? 1 : 0);
 260: 		return;
 261: 	}
 262: 
 263: 	EntityBody->SetStaticMesh(BodySM);
 264: 	EntityEye->SetStaticMesh(EyeSM);
 265: 	EntityTentacles->SetSkinnedAssetAndUpdate(TentacleSK, true);
 266: 	// The skeletal mesh ships without a material on its slot: use the authored hologram instance.
 267: 	for (int32 Slot = 0; Slot < EntityTentacles->GetNumMaterials(); ++Slot)
 268: 	{
 269: 		if (EntityTentacles->GetMaterial(Slot) == nullptr)
 270: 		{
 271: 			EntityTentacles->SetMaterial(Slot, TentacleMI);
 272: 		}
 273: 	}
 274: 
 275: 	// Dynamic instances: Flash starts at 0, GlowColor stays as authored. SwayAmount is 0:
 276: 	// the bones now do the motion, the material sway would fight them.
 277: 	for (int32 Slot = 0; Slot < EntityBody->GetNumMaterials(); ++Slot)
 278: 	{
 279: 		if (UMaterialInstanceDynamic* MID = EntityBody->CreateDynamicMaterialInstance(Slot))
 280: 		{
 281: 			MID->SetScalarParameterValue(GBEntityLocal::FlashParam, 0.f);
 282: 			BodyMIDs.Add(MID);
 283: 		}
 284: 	}
 285: 	for (int32 Slot = 0; Slot < EntityEye->GetNumMaterials(); ++Slot)
 286: 	{
 287: 		if (UMaterialInstanceDynamic* MID = EntityEye->CreateDynamicMaterialInstance(Slot))
 288: 		{
 289: 			MID->SetScalarParameterValue(GBEntityLocal::FlashParam, 0.f);
 290: 			EyeMIDs.Add(MID);
 291: 		}
 292: 	}
 293: 	for (int32 Slot = 0; Slot < EntityTentacles->GetNumMaterials(); ++Slot)
 294: 	{
 295: 		if (UMaterialInstanceDynamic* MID = EntityTentacles->CreateDynamicMaterialInstance(Slot))
 296: 		{
 297: 			MID->SetScalarParameterValue(GBEntityLocal::SwayParam, 0.f);
 298: 			if (!TentacleMID)
 299: 			{
 300: 				TentacleMID = MID;
 301: 			}
 302: 		}
 303: 	}
 304: 
 305: 	// The eye light takes the eye material's glow colour when it has one (else the projectile colour).
 306: 	for (UMaterialInstanceDynamic* MID : EyeMIDs)
 307: 	{
 308: 		if (MID)
 309: 		{
 310: 			const FLinearColor Glow = MID->K2_GetVectorParameterValue(FName(TEXT("GlowColor")));
 311: 			if (Glow.R + Glow.G + Glow.B > 0.05f)
 312: 			{
 313: 				EyeLightColor = FLinearColor(Glow.R, Glow.G, Glow.B);
 314: 				break;
 315: 			}
 316: 		}
 317: 	}
 318: 	EyeLight->SetRelativeLocation(GBEntityLocal::EyeCentreCm + FVector(GBEntityLocal::EyeLightForwardCm, 0.f, 0.f));
 319: 	EyeLight->SetLightColor(EyeLightColor);
 320: 	EyeLight->SetAttenuationRadius(Tuning.EyeLightRadius);
 321: 	MuzzleLight->SetLightColor(EyeLightColor);
 322: 	MuzzleLight->SetAttenuationRadius(Tuning.MuzzleRadius);
 323: 
 324: 	// The real look is up: hide the base class placeholder.
 325: 	Sprite->SetVisibility(false);
 326: 	BodyMesh->SetVisibility(false);
 327: 
 328: 	TentacleRig->Setup(EntityTentacles);
 329: }
 330: 
 331: FVector AGBEntity::GetEyeCentreWorld() const
 332: {
 333: 	if (EntityEye)
 334: 	{
 335: 		return EntityEye->GetComponentTransform().TransformPosition(GBEntityLocal::EyeCentreCm);
 336: 	}
 337: 	return GetActorLocation();
 338: }
 339: 
 340: FVector AGBEntity::GetShellTopWorld() const
 341: {
 342: 	if (VisualRoot)
 343: 	{
 344: 		return VisualRoot->GetComponentTransform().TransformPosition(FVector(0.f, 0.f, GBEntityLocal::ShellTopCm));
 345: 	}
 346: 	return GetActorLocation();
 347: }
 348: 
 349: bool AGBEntity::IsHeadshotLocation(const FVector& HitLocation) const
 350: {
 351: 	// The weak point is the drawn eye. The hit sphere stands a little proud of the lens, so the radius has some slack.
 352: 	return FVector::Dist(HitLocation, GetEyeCentreWorld()) <= FMath::Max(Tuning.EyeWeakRadius, GBEntityLocal::EyeRadiusCm);
 353: }
 354: 
 355: FVector AGBEntity::GetAttackOrigin(AActor* Goal) const
 356: {
 357: 	if (TentacleRig && TentacleRig->IsReady() && Goal)
 358: 	{
 359: 		const FVector GoalPos = Goal->GetActorLocation();
 360: 		int32 Best = INDEX_NONE;
 361: 		float BestDist = FLT_MAX;
 362: 		for (int32 Chain = 0; Chain < TentacleRig->GetChainCount(); ++Chain)
 363: 		{
 364: 			const float Dist = FVector::Dist(TentacleRig->GetChainTipWorldPosition(Chain), GoalPos);
 365: 			if (Dist < BestDist)
 366: 			{
 367: 				BestDist = Dist;
 368: 				Best = Chain;
 369: 			}
 370: 		}
 371: 		if (Best != INDEX_NONE)
 372: 		{
 373: 			return TentacleRig->GetChainTipWorldPosition(Best);
 374: 		}
 375: 	}
 376: 	return GetChestLocation();
 377: }
 378: 
 379: FVector AGBEntity::GetGoalHeadLocation(const AActor* Goal) const
 380: {
 381: 	if (const APawn* GoalPawn = Cast<APawn>(Goal))
 382: 	{
 383: 		return GoalPawn->GetPawnViewLocation();
 384: 	}
 385: 	return Goal ? Goal->GetActorLocation() : GetActorLocation();
 386: }
 387: 
 388: AGBProjectile* AGBEntity::FireBolt(AActor* Goal, const FVector& Origin, const FVector& Direction, float SpeedOverride)
 389: {
 390: 	const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
FILE AI/GBEntity.h
   1: // The Entity, phase 1 "the machine" (GDD 11): a floating metal shell with a big central eye
   2: // (the weak point) and hologram tentacles driven by UGBTentacleRigComponent.
   3: // The body, the eye and the tentacles are visuals parented to a scene root that floats above the
   4: // ground capsule. The capsule keeps walking on the navmesh like every other enemy (pathing, local
   5: // steering, ledge checks), but it no longer blocks the weapon channel: a sphere on the shell does
   6: // (guns and projectiles hit what is drawn). The visual root flies: altitude drift, banking into
   7: // motion, a 3D aim, a limited turn rate and spring knockback. The base class placeholder look is
   8: // hidden once the real art loads; if any asset is missing the placeholder stays (one warning).
   9: // Every tuning number is a Game.json "Values" entry prefixed Entity, with the default in code.
  10: #pragma once
  11: 
  12: #include "CoreMinimal.h"
  13: #include "AI/GBEnemyCharacter.h"
  14: #include "GBEntity.generated.h"
  15: 
  16: class UGBTentacleRigComponent;
  17: class UPoseableMeshComponent;
  18: class UStaticMeshComponent;
  19: class USceneComponent;
  20: class UMeshComponent;
  21: class UMaterialInstanceDynamic;
  22: class USphereComponent;
  23: class UPointLightComponent;
  24: class AGBProjectile;
  25: 
  26: /** Critically-tunable spring: X follows Target with natural frequency Omega (rad/s) and damping ratio Zeta. */
  27: struct FGBEntitySpring
  28: {
  29: 	float X = 0.f;
  30: 	float V = 0.f;
  31: 	void Step(float Target, float Dt, float Omega, float Zeta);
  32: };
  33: 
  34: /** Entity flight, turn, hit and light numbers; filled from Game.json every tick (defaults in GBEntity.cpp). */
  35: struct FGBEntityTuning
  36: {
  37: 	float TurnMaxRate = 90.f, TurnGain = 3.f, TurnAccel = 5.f;
  38: 	float PitchMax = 30.f, AimOmega = 6.f, EyeTrackMax = 12.f, EyeTrackSpeed = 14.f;
  39: 	float AltMin = 250.f, AltMax = 450.f, AltDriftAmp = 70.f, AltDriftHz = 0.22f, AltRise = 60.f, AltDip = 45.f, AltDipTime = 0.7f, AltOmega = 3.f;
  40: 	float BankMax = 20.f, BankPerYawRate = 0.07f, LeanDeg = 10.f, AccelPitchDeg = 8.f, TiltOmega = 7.f, TiltZeta = 0.75f;
  41: 	float Acceleration = 700.f, Braking = 600.f, GroundFriction = 2.f;
  42: 	float StrafeSpeed = 0.7f, StrafeFlipMin = 1.8f, StrafeFlipMax = 4.f, StrafeKeep = 0.5f, StrafeProbe = 220.f;
  43: 	float HitTiltKick = 90.f, HitPushKick = 220.f, HitRefDamage = 100.f, HitMinShare = 0.15f, HitWeakMult = 2.f, HitOmega = 8.f, HitZeta = 0.3f;
  44: 	float ShellRadius = 88.f, EyeWeakRadius = 40.f;
  45: 	float EyeLightIntensity = 1500.f, EyeLightRadius = 1600.f, EyeLightPulseHz = 0.7f, EyeLightPulse = 0.15f;
  46: 	float EyeLightWindupFlare = 0.8f, EyeLightHitFlare = 2.f, EyeLightFade = 0.8f;
  47: 	float BoltLength = 80.f, BoltThickness = 12.f, BoltTrail = 240.f, BoltLightIntensity = 4000.f, BoltLightRadius = 700.f;
  48: 	float MuzzleIntensity = 15000.f, MuzzleRadius = 700.f, MuzzleTime = 0.12f;
  49: };

FILE AI/GBTentacleRigComponent.cpp
   1: #include "AI/GBTentacleRigComponent.h"
   2: 
   3: #include "Components/PoseableMeshComponent.h"
   4: #include "BoneContainer.h"
   5: #include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
   6: #include "AI/GBTentacleMath.h"
   7: 
   8: namespace GBTentacleRigLocal
   9: {
  10: 	// Technical constants, not tuning: bone name shape, FABRIK budget, lag clamp, ripple shape.
  11: 	constexpr int32 MaxNameLength = 6;          // "T<cc>_<bb>"
  12: 	constexpr int32 ReachIterations = 10;
  13: 	constexpr float ReachToleranceCm = 0.5f;    // well under the 15 cm the smoke run allows
  14: 	constexpr float MaxLagDegrees = 30.f;       // clamp on a lag spring target
  15: 	constexpr float YawLeverCm = 100.f;         // yaw rate acts like sideways speed at this lever
  16: 	constexpr float MaxDeltaTime = 0.1f;        // a hitch never jumps the pose
  17: 	constexpr float RippleWidth = 4.f;          // sharpness of the travelling pulse
  18: 	constexpr float RippleWaves = 2.f;          // oscillations inside the pulse
  19: 	constexpr int32 RootLimitedBones = 2;       // bones _00/_01 keep the root bend limit
  20: 
  21: 	FQuat AxisAngleQuat(const FVector& Axis, float AngleDeg)
  22: 	{
  23: 		FVector SafeAxis = Axis.GetSafeNormal();
  24: 		if (SafeAxis.IsNearlyZero())
  25: 		{
  26: 			return FQuat::Identity;
  27: 		}
  28: 		return FQuat(SafeAxis, FMath::DegreesToRadians(AngleDeg));
  29: 	}
  30: 
  31: 	FVector AxisVector(int32 Axis)
  32: 	{
  33: 		switch (Axis)
  34: 		{
  35: 		case 0: return FVector::XAxisVector;
  36: 		case 1: return FVector::YAxisVector;
  37: 		default: return FVector::ZAxisVector;
  38: 		}
  39: 	}
  40: }

  53: 		bHasPrevYaw = true;
  54: 	}
  55: }

  64: {

  69: 	OutTuning.LagStrength = GetGameValue(LagStrength, LagStrengthDefault);
  70: 	OutTuning.LagStiffness = GetGameValue(LagStiffness, LagStiffnessDefault);

  74: 	OutTuning.ReachBlendOut = GetGameValue(ReachBlendOut, ReachBlendOutDefault);
