  79: 
  80: void UGBTentacleRigComponent::Setup(UPoseableMeshComponent* InMesh)
  81: {
  82: 	Chains.Reset();
  83: 	TentacleMesh = InMesh;
  84: 	ReachWeight = 0.f;
  85: 	ReachDesired = 0.f;
  86: 
  87: 	if (!IsValid(InMesh) || !InMesh->GetSkinnedAsset())
  88: 	{
  89: 		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("TentacleRig: no poseable mesh with a skeletal mesh, tentacles stay still"));
  90: 		return;
  91: 	}
  92: 
  93: 	TArray<FName> BoneNames;
  94: 	InMesh->GetBoneNames(BoneNames);
  95: 
  96: 	// Group T<cc>_<bb> bones into chains sorted by bone index.
  97: 	TMap<int32, TArray<TPair<int32, FName>>> Groups;
  98: 	for (const FName& BoneName : BoneNames)
  99: 	{
 100: 		const FString Name = BoneName.ToString();
 101: 		if (Name.Len() != GBTentacleRigLocal::MaxNameLength || !Name.StartsWith(TEXT("T")) || Name[3] != TCHAR('_'))
 102: 		{
 103: 			continue;
 104: 		}
 105: 		const int32 ChainIndex = FCString::Atoi(*Name.Mid(1, 2));
 106: 		const int32 BoneIndex = FCString::Atoi(*Name.Mid(4, 2));
 107: 		// Guard the shape: only T<digits>_<digits>.
 108: 		if (!FChar::IsDigit(Name[1]) || !FChar::IsDigit(Name[2]) || !FChar::IsDigit(Name[4]) || !FChar::IsDigit(Name[5]))
 109: 		{
 110: 			continue;
 111: 		}
 112: 		Groups.FindOrAdd(ChainIndex).Add(TPair<int32, FName>(BoneIndex, BoneName));
 113: 	}
 114: 
 115: 	TArray<int32> ChainKeys;
 116: 	Groups.GetKeys(ChainKeys);
 117: 	ChainKeys.Sort();
 118: 
 119: 	RootCompQuat = FQuat::Identity;
 120: 	if (BoneNames.Contains(TEXT("root")))
 121: 	{
 122: 		RootCompQuat = InMesh->GetBoneTransformByName(TEXT("root"), EBoneSpaces::ComponentSpace).GetRotation();
 123: 	}
 124: 
 125: 	for (int32 ChainKey : ChainKeys)
 126: 	{
 127: 		TArray<TPair<int32, FName>>& Pairs = Groups.FindChecked(ChainKey);
 128: 		Pairs.Sort([](const TPair<int32, FName>& A, const TPair<int32, FName>& B) { return A.Key < B.Key; });
 129: 
 130: 		FGBTentacleChain Chain;
 131: 		Chain.Phase = FMath::FRand();
 132: 		FQuat ParentComp = RootCompQuat;
 133: 		for (const TPair<int32, FName>& Pair : Pairs)
 134: 		{
 135: 			const FTransform CompT = InMesh->GetBoneTransformByName(Pair.Value, EBoneSpaces::ComponentSpace);
 136: 			Chain.Bones.Add(Pair.Value);
 137: 			Chain.RefComp.Add(CompT.GetRotation());
 138: 			Chain.RefJoints.Add(CompT.GetLocation());
 139: 			Chain.RefLocal.Add(ParentComp.Inverse() * CompT.GetRotation());
 140: 			ParentComp = CompT.GetRotation();
 141: 		}
 142: 		const int32 BoneCount = Chain.Bones.Num();
 143: 		if (BoneCount == 0)
 144: 		{
 145: 			continue;
 146: 		}
 147: 		// The tip joint continues past the last bone along the last segment.
 148: 		const FVector LastDir = (Chain.RefJoints[BoneCount - 1] - Chain.RefJoints[BoneCount - 2 >= 0 ? BoneCount - 2 : 0]).GetSafeNormal();
 149: 		const float LastLen = BoneCount >= 2 ? (Chain.RefJoints[BoneCount - 1] - Chain.RefJoints[BoneCount - 2]).Size() : 10.f;
 150: 		Chain.RefJoints.Add(Chain.RefJoints[BoneCount - 1] + LastDir * LastLen);
 151: 
 152: 		for (int32 Bone = 0; Bone < BoneCount; ++Bone)
 153: 		{
 154: 			const float Segment = (Chain.RefJoints[Bone + 1] - Chain.RefJoints[Bone]).Size();
 155: 			Chain.SegLengths.Add(FMath::Max(Segment, KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER));
 156: 			const FVector RefDir = (Chain.RefJoints[Bone + 1] - Chain.RefJoints[Bone]).GetSafeNormal();
 157: 			const FVector LocalDir = Chain.RefComp[Bone].Inverse() * RefDir;
 158: 			Chain.LocalDirs.Add(LocalDir);
 159: 			// Bend about the two axes perpendicular to the bone's dominant axis, whatever the rig's convention is.
 160: 			const float AX = FMath::Abs(LocalDir.X);
 161: 			const float AY = FMath::Abs(LocalDir.Y);
 162: 			if (AX >= AY && AX >= FMath::Abs(LocalDir.Z))
 163: 			{
 164: 				Chain.BendAxisA.Add(1);
 165: 				Chain.BendAxisB.Add(2);
 166: 			}
 167: 			else if (AY >= AX && AY >= FMath::Abs(LocalDir.Z))
 168: 			{
 169: 				Chain.BendAxisA.Add(0);
 170: 				Chain.BendAxisB.Add(2);
 171: 			}
 172: 			else
 173: 			{
 174: 				Chain.BendAxisA.Add(0);
 175: 				Chain.BendAxisB.Add(1);
 176: 			}
 177: 		}
 178: 		Chain.FKJoints = Chain.RefJoints;
 179: 		Chains.Add(MoveTemp(Chain));
 180: 	}
 181: 
 182: 	UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("TentacleRig: %d chains ready"), Chains.Num());
 183: }
 184: 
 185: int32 UGBTentacleRigComponent::GetBoneCount() const
 186: {
 187: 	int32 Count = 0;
 188: 	for (const FGBTentacleChain& Chain : Chains)
 189: 	{
 190: 		Count += Chain.Bones.Num();
 191: 	}
 192: 	return Count;
 193: }
 194: 
 195: FVector UGBTentacleRigComponent::GetChainTipPosition(int32 ChainIndex) const
 196: {
 197: 	if (!Chains.IsValidIndex(ChainIndex) || Chains[ChainIndex].FKJoints.Num() == 0)
 198: 	{
 199: 		return FVector::ZeroVector;
 200: 	}
 201: 	return Chains[ChainIndex].FKJoints.Last();
 202: }
 203: 
 204: FVector UGBTentacleRigComponent::GetChainTipWorldPosition(int32 ChainIndex) const
 205: {
 206: 	if (!TentacleMesh.IsValid())
 207: 	{
 208: 		return FVector::ZeroVector;
 209: 	}
 210: 	return TentacleMesh->GetComponentTransform().TransformPosition(GetChainTipPosition(ChainIndex));
 211: }
 212: 
 213: void UGBTentacleRigComponent::SetReachTarget(const FVector& WorldTarget, float Weight)
 214: {
 215: 	ReachTargetWorld = WorldTarget;
 216: 	ReachDesired = FMath::Clamp(Weight, 0.f, 1.f);
 217: }
 218: 
 219: void UGBTentacleRigComponent::ClearReach()
 220: {
 221: 	ReachDesired = 0.f;
 222: }
 223: 
 224: void UGBTentacleRigComponent::SetReachChain(int32 ChainIndex)
 225: {
 226: 	ReachChain = (Chains.IsValidIndex(ChainIndex) || ChainIndex < 0) ? ChainIndex : -1;
 227: }
 228: 
 229: void UGBTentacleRigComponent::AddRipple(float Strength)
 230: {
 231: 	RippleElapsed = 0.f;
 232: 	RippleStrengthNow = Strength;
 233: }
 234: 
 235: void UGBTentacleRigComponent::SetLimp(bool bInLimp)
 236: {
 237: 	bLimp = bInLimp;
 238: }
 239: 
 240: int32 UGBTentacleRigComponent::PickReachChain() const
 241: {
 242: 	if (Chains.Num() == 0)
 243: 	{
 244: 		return INDEX_NONE;
 245: 	}
 246: 	if (Chains.IsValidIndex(ReachChain))
 247: 	{
 248: 		return ReachChain;
 249: 	}
 250: 	// The chain whose tip is currently closest to the target reaches.
 251: 	const FVector TargetLocal = TentacleMesh.IsValid()
 252: 		? TentacleMesh->GetComponentTransform().InverseTransformPosition(ReachTargetWorld)
 253: 		: ReachTargetWorld;
 254: 	int32 Best = 0;
 255: 	float BestDist = FLT_MAX;
 256: 	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Chains.Num(); ++Index)
 257: 	{
 258: 		const float Dist = FVector::Dist(GetChainTipPosition(Index), TargetLocal);
 259: 		if (Dist < BestDist)
 260: 		{
 261: 			BestDist = Dist;
 262: 			Best = Index;
 263: 		}
 264: 	}
 265: 	return Best;
 266: }
 267: 
 268: void UGBTentacleRigComponent::TickComponent(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType, FActorComponentTickFunction* ThisTickFunction)
 269: {
 270: 	Super::TickComponent(DeltaTime, TickType, ThisTickFunction);
 271: 	if (!IsReady())
 272: 	{
 273: 		return;
 274: 	}
 275: 
 276: 	FGBTentacleTuning Tuning;
 277: 	ReadTuning(Tuning);
 278: 
 279: 	const float Dt = FMath::Min(FMath::Max(DeltaTime, 0.f), GBTentacleRigLocal::MaxDeltaTime);
 280: 	Time += Dt;
 281: 
 282: 	// Inertia: actor velocity and yaw rate in the actor frame (X forward, Y right, Z up).
 283: 	FVector LocalVel = FVector::ZeroVector;
 284: 	float YawRate = 0.f;
 285: 	if (AActor* Owner = GetOwner())
 286: 	{
 287: 		const FQuat ActorQuat = Owner->GetActorQuat();
 288: 		LocalVel = ActorQuat.Inverse().RotateVector(Owner->GetVelocity());
 289: 		const float Yaw = Owner->GetActorRotation().Yaw;
 290: 		if (bHasPrevYaw && Dt > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
 291: 		{
 292: 			YawRate = FMath::DegreesToRadians(FMath::FindDeltaAngleDegrees(PrevYaw, Yaw) / Dt);
 293: 		}
 294: 		PrevYaw = Yaw;
 295: 		bHasPrevYaw = true;
 296: 	}
 297: 
 298: 	const float FwdTarget = FMath::Clamp(LocalVel.X * Tuning.LagStrength,
 299: 		-GBTentacleRigLocal::MaxLagDegrees, GBTentacleRigLocal::MaxLagDegrees);
 300: 	const float SideTarget = FMath::Clamp(-(LocalVel.Y + YawRate * GBTentacleRigLocal::YawLeverCm) * Tuning.LagStrength,
 301: 		-GBTentacleRigLocal::MaxLagDegrees, GBTentacleRigLocal::MaxLagDegrees);
 302: 	GBTentacle::SpringStep(LagFwd, LagFwdVel, FwdTarget, Tuning.LagStiffness, Tuning.LagDamping, Dt);
 303: 	GBTentacle::SpringStep(LagSide, LagSideVel, SideTarget, Tuning.LagStiffness, Tuning.LagDamping, Dt);
 304: 
 305: 	// Ramps: reach weight and limp.
 306: 	const float BlendIn = FMath::Max(Tuning.ReachBlendIn, KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER);
 307: 	const float BlendOut = FMath::Max(Tuning.ReachBlendOut, KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER);
 308: 	const float ReachStep = (ReachDesired > ReachWeight ? Dt / BlendIn : Dt / BlendOut);
 309: 	ReachWeight = FMath::Clamp(ReachWeight + FMath::Clamp(ReachDesired - ReachWeight, -ReachStep, ReachStep), 0.f, 1.f);
 310: 
 311: 	const float LimpGoal = bLimp ? 1.f : 0.f;
 312: 	LimpWeight = FMath::Clamp(LimpWeight + FMath::Clamp(LimpGoal - LimpWeight, -Dt, Dt), 0.f, 1.f);
 313: 	const float WaveScale = 1.f - LimpWeight;
 314: 
 315: 	RippleElapsed += Dt;
 316: 
 317: 	const int32 SolvingChain = (ReachWeight > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER) ? PickReachChain() : INDEX_NONE;
 318: 	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Chains.Num(); ++Index)
 319: 	{
 320: 		PoseChain(Chains[Index], Tuning, WaveScale, Index == SolvingChain);
 321: 	}
 322: }
 323: 
 324: void UGBTentacleRigComponent::PoseChain(FGBTentacleChain& Chain, const FGBTentacleTuning& Tuning, float WaveScale, bool bSolving)
 325: {
 326: 	UPoseableMeshComponent* Mesh = TentacleMesh.Get();
 327: 	if (!Mesh)
 328: 	{
 329: 		return;
 330: 	}
 331: 	const int32 BoneCount = Chain.Bones.Num();
 332: 	if (BoneCount == 0)
 333: 	{
 334: 		return;
 335: 	}
 336: 
 337: 	// Reach blend of this chain: the other layers fade out by the weight.
 338: 	const float ReachW = bSolving ? ReachWeight : 0.f;
 339: 	const float OtherScale = (1.f - ReachW) * WaveScale;
 340: 
 341: 	// Hit ripple: a pulse running from the root to the tip over RippleDuration.
 342: 	float RippleAmp = 0.f;
 343: 	float RippleFreq = 0.f;
 344: 	const float RippleDuration = FMath::Max(Tuning.RippleDuration, KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER);
 345: 	const float RippleFront = RippleElapsed / RippleDuration;
 346: 	if (RippleFront < 1.5f)
 347: 	{
 348: 		RippleAmp = RippleStrengthNow;
 349: 		RippleFreq = RippleFront;
 350: 	}
 351: 
 352: 	// FABRIK for the solving chain, from the current pose towards the target.
 353: 	TArray<FVector> Solved;
 354: 	if (bSolving)
 355: 	{
 356: 		Solved = Chain.FKJoints;
 357: 		const FVector TargetLocal = Mesh->GetComponentTransform().InverseTransformPosition(ReachTargetWorld);
 358: 		GBTentacle::SolveFabrik(Solved, TargetLocal, GBTentacleRigLocal::ReachIterations, GBTentacleRigLocal::ReachToleranceCm);
 359: 	}
 360: 
 361: 	FQuat ParentComp = RootCompQuat;
 362: 	for (int32 Bone = 0; Bone < BoneCount; ++Bone)
 363: 	{
 364: 		const float Growth = static_cast<float>(Bone + 1) / static_cast<float>(BoneCount);
 365: 		const FVector RefDir = (Chain.RefJoints[Bone + 1] - Chain.RefJoints[Bone]).GetSafeNormal();
 366: 
 367: 		// Layer 1: idle wave, two perpendicular bends, out of step per chain.
 368: 		float BendA = GBTentacle::WaveAngle(Time, Tuning.WaveFreq, Chain.Phase, static_cast<float>(Bone),
 369: 			Tuning.WaveLength, Tuning.WaveAmp, BoneCount);
 370: 		float BendB = GBTentacle::WaveAngle(Time, Tuning.WaveFreq, Chain.Phase + 0.25f, static_cast<float>(Bone),
 371: 			Tuning.WaveLength, Tuning.WaveAmp, BoneCount);
 372: 		// Layer 4: hit ripple on the first bend axis.
 373: 		if (RippleAmp != 0.f)
 374: 		{
 375: 			const float D = (RippleFreq - Growth) * GBTentacleRigLocal::RippleWidth;
 376: 			BendA += RippleAmp * FMath::Exp(-D * D)
 377: 				* FMath::Sin(2.f * PI * (GBTentacleRigLocal::RippleWaves * RippleFreq - Growth));
 378: 		}
 379: 		BendA *= OtherScale;
 380: 		BendB *= OtherScale;
 381: 		if (Bone < GBTentacleRigLocal::RootLimitedBones)
 382: 		{
 383: 			BendA = FMath::Clamp(BendA, -Tuning.RootLimit, Tuning.RootLimit);
 384: 			BendB = FMath::Clamp(BendB, -Tuning.RootLimit, Tuning.RootLimit);
 385: 		}
 386: 		const FQuat Extra = FQuat(GBTentacleRigLocal::AxisVector(Chain.BendAxisA[Bone]), FMath::DegreesToRadians(BendA))
 387: 			* FQuat(GBTentacleRigLocal::AxisVector(Chain.BendAxisB[Bone]), FMath::DegreesToRadians(BendB));
 388: 		const FQuat Local = Chain.RefLocal[Bone] * Extra;
 389: 
 390: 		// Layer 2: inertia lag as a small component-space bend (scaled out on the reach chain).
 391: 		const float LagScale = Growth * (1.f - ReachW);
 392: 		const FQuat LagDelta = FQuat(FVector::YAxisVector, FMath::DegreesToRadians(LagFwd * LagScale))
 393: 			* FQuat(FVector::XAxisVector, FMath::DegreesToRadians(LagSide * LagScale));
 394: 		FQuat Comp = LagDelta * ParentComp * Local;
 395: 
 396: 		// Limp droop: rotate the bone direction towards -Z (frame-independent).
 397: 		const float Droop = Tuning.LimpDroop * LimpWeight * Growth * (1.f - ReachW);
 398: 		if (FMath::Abs(Droop) > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
 399: 		{
 400: 			const FVector Dir = (Comp * Chain.LocalDirs[Bone]).GetSafeNormal();
 401: 			const FVector Axis = FVector::CrossProduct(Dir, FVector::DownVector);
 402: 			if (!Axis.IsNearlyZero())
 403: 			{
 404: 				Comp = GBTentacleRigLocal::AxisAngleQuat(Axis, Droop) * Comp;
 405: 			}
 406: 		}
 407: 
 408: 		if (bSolving && Solved.Num() == Chain.FKJoints.Num())
 409: 		{
 410: 			// Layer 3: turn the solved joint positions back into bone rotations (twist-minimal
 411: 			// against the reference) and blend the composed pose towards them by the weight.
 412: 			// At full weight the FK below reproduces the solved joints exactly.
 413: 			const FVector SolvedDir = (Solved[Bone + 1] - Solved[Bone]).GetSafeNormal();
 414: 			const FQuat SolvedComp = FQuat::FindBetweenNormals(RefDir, SolvedDir) * Chain.RefComp[Bone];
 415: 			Comp = FQuat::Slerp(Comp, SolvedComp, ReachW);
 416: 		}
 417: 		Comp.Normalize();
 418: 
 419: 		Mesh->SetBoneRotationByName(Chain.Bones[Bone], Comp.Rotator(), EBoneSpaces::ComponentSpace);
 420: 
 421: 		// Forward kinematics for the next bone (and the tip queries): exact, from the same rotations.
 422: 		const FVector BoneDir = (Comp * Chain.LocalDirs[Bone]).GetSafeNormal();
 423: 		Chain.FKJoints[Bone + 1] = Chain.FKJoints[Bone] + BoneDir * Chain.SegLengths[Bone];
 424: 		ParentComp = Comp;
 425: 	}
 426: 	// The root joint never moves.
 427: 	Chain.FKJoints[0] = Chain.RefJoints[0];
 428: }
FILE AI/GBTentacleRigComponent.h
   1: // Drives the Entity's hologram tentacles on a UPoseableMeshComponent (GDD 11, phase 1 "the machine").
   2: // No AnimBP, no Control Rig: every tick each chain's pose is composed from four layers as rotations
   3: // on top of the reference pose, root to tip:
   4: //  1. idle wave (two perpendicular bends per bone, out of step per chain),
   5: //  2. inertia lag (the actor's velocity and yaw rate trail the tips through a damped spring),
   6: //  3. reach (FABRIK towards a world target on one chain, blended in/out by weight),
   7: //  4. hit ripple (a pulse running root to tip).
   8: // SetLimp fades the waves out and droops the chains towards -Z. Bones _00/_01 keep a root bend limit
   9: // so a chain cannot fold back through the shell.
  10: // Every tuning number is a Game.json value (GBEntityTentacle::*) with the default in code.
  11: #pragma once
  12: 
  13: #include "CoreMinimal.h"
  14: #include "Components/ActorComponent.h"
  15: #include "GBTentacleRigComponent.generated.h"
  16: 
  17: class UPoseableMeshComponent;
  18: 
  19: /** Game.json "Values" keys of the Entity tentacle rig, with code defaults. */
  20: namespace GBEntityTentacle
  21: {
  22: 	inline const FName WaveFreq(TEXT("EntityTentacleWaveFreq"));             // Hz of the idle wave
  23: 	inline const FName WaveAmp(TEXT("EntityTentacleWaveAmp"));               // degrees at the tip
  24: 	inline const FName WaveLength(TEXT("EntityTentacleWaveLength"));         // wave number, in bones
  25: 	inline const FName LagStrength(TEXT("EntityTentacleLagStrength"));       // lag degrees per cm/s
  26: 	inline const FName LagStiffness(TEXT("EntityTentacleLagStiffness"));     // spring stiffness of the lag
  27: 	inline const FName LagDamping(TEXT("EntityTentacleLagDamping"));        // spring damping of the lag
  28: 	inline const FName RootLimit(TEXT("EntityTentacleRootLimit"));           // degrees: max bend of bones _00/_01
  29: 	inline const FName ReachBlendIn(TEXT("EntityTentacleReachBlendIn"));     // seconds 0 -> 1
  30: 	inline const FName ReachBlendOut(TEXT("EntityTentacleReachBlendOut"));   // seconds 1 -> 0
  31: 	inline const FName RippleStrength(TEXT("EntityTentacleRippleStrength")); // degrees of the hit pulse
  32: 	inline const FName RippleDuration(TEXT("EntityTentacleRippleDuration")); // seconds root -> tip
  33: 	inline const FName LimpDroop(TEXT("EntityTentacleLimpDroop"));           // droop degrees per bone when limp
  34: 
  35: 	constexpr float WaveFreqDefault = 0.6f;
  36: 	constexpr float WaveAmpDefault = 6.f;
  37: 	constexpr float WaveLengthDefault = 2.5f;
  38: 	constexpr float LagStrengthDefault = 0.025f;
  39: 	constexpr float LagStiffnessDefault = 40.f;
  40: 	constexpr float LagDampingDefault = 12.f;
  41: 	constexpr float RootLimitDefault = 10.f;
  42: 	constexpr float ReachBlendInDefault = 0.3f;
  43: 	constexpr float ReachBlendOutDefault = 0.4f;
  44: 	constexpr float RippleStrengthDefault = 10.f;
  45: 	constexpr float RippleDurationDefault = 0.5f;
  46: 	constexpr float LimpDroopDefault = 2.f;
  47: }
  48: 
  49: /** Tuning values read once per tick from Game.json (with the defaults above). */
  50: struct FGBTentacleTuning
  51: {
  52: 	float WaveFreq = GBEntityTentacle::WaveFreqDefault;
  53: 	float WaveAmp = GBEntityTentacle::WaveAmpDefault;
  54: 	float WaveLength = GBEntityTentacle::WaveLengthDefault;
  55: 	float LagStrength = GBEntityTentacle::LagStrengthDefault;
  56: 	float LagStiffness = GBEntityTentacle::LagStiffnessDefault;
  57: 	float LagDamping = GBEntityTentacle::LagDampingDefault;
  58: 	float RootLimit = GBEntityTentacle::RootLimitDefault;
  59: 	float ReachBlendIn = GBEntityTentacle::ReachBlendInDefault;
  60: 	float ReachBlendOut = GBEntityTentacle::ReachBlendOutDefault;
  61: 	float RippleStrength = GBEntityTentacle::RippleStrengthDefault;
  62: 	float RippleDuration = GBEntityTentacle::RippleDurationDefault;
  63: 	float LimpDroop = GBEntityTentacle::LimpDroopDefault;
  64: };
  65: 
  66: /** One tentacle chain: bone names plus everything derived from the reference pose at Setup. */
  67: struct FGBTentacleChain
  68: {
  69: 	/** Bone names from the chain root (_00) to the tip, in order. */
  70: 	TArray<FName> Bones;
  71: 	/** Reference component-space positions: one joint per bone plus the extrapolated tip. */
  72: 	TArray<FVector> RefJoints;
  73: 	/** Reference segment lengths between consecutive joints. */
  74: 	TArray<float> SegLengths;
  75: 	/** Reference local (parent-space) rotations, one per bone. */
  76: 	TArray<FQuat> RefLocal;
  77: 	/** Reference component-space rotations, one per bone. */
  78: 	TArray<FQuat> RefComp;
  79: 	/** Reference bone direction in the bone's own frame (dominant axis), for wave bends. */
  80: 	TArray<FVector> LocalDirs;
  81: 	/** Which two local axes a bone bends about (both perpendicular to its dominant axis). */
  82: 	TArray<int32> BendAxisA;
  83: 	TArray<int32> BendAxisB;
  84: 	/** Current joint positions in component space, updated every tick (for FABRIK and tip queries). */
  85: 	TArray<FVector> FKJoints;
  86: 	/** Random wave phase of this chain, in cycles. */
  87: 	float Phase = 0.f;
  88: };
  89: 
  90: UCLASS(ClassGroup = (Glyphbound), meta = (BlueprintSpawnableComponent))
  91: class GLYPHBOUND_API UGBTentacleRigComponent : public UActorComponent
  92: {
  93: 	GENERATED_BODY()
  94: 
  95: public:
  96: 	UGBTentacleRigComponent();
  97: 
  98: 	/** Reads the reference pose and finds the T<cc>_<bb> chains. Safe to call once the skeletal mesh is set. */
  99: 	void Setup(UPoseableMeshComponent* InMesh);
 100: 
 101: 	bool IsReady() const { return TentacleMesh.IsValid() && Chains.Num() > 0; }
 102: 	int32 GetChainCount() const { return Chains.Num(); }
 103: 	/** Total driven bones over all chains. */
 104: 	int32 GetBoneCount() const;
 105: 	/** Component-space tip (end of the last segment) of a chain, from the last tick. */
 106: 	FVector GetChainTipPosition(int32 ChainIndex) const;
 107: 	FVector GetChainTipWorldPosition(int32 ChainIndex) const;
 108: 	/** World position the reach chain is currently solving towards (or zero when idle). */
 109: 	FVector GetReachTargetWorld() const { return ReachTargetWorld; }
 110: 
 111: 	/**
 112: 	 * Reach with a chain towards a world target. The chain whose tip is closest solves FABRIK,
 113: 	 * unless SetReachChain picked one. Weight 0..1 is the goal; the actual weight ramps at the
 114: 	 * blend in/out rates. The other layers fade out of that chain by the weight.
 115: 	 */
 116: 	void SetReachTarget(const FVector& WorldTarget, float Weight);
 117: 	void ClearReach();
 118: 	/** -1 (default) = the chain whose tip is closest to the target reaches. */
 119: 	void SetReachChain(int32 ChainIndex);
 120: 
 121: 	void AddRipple(float Strength);
 122: 	void SetLimp(bool bLimp);
 123: 	bool IsLimp() const { return bLimp; }
 124: 
 125: 	virtual void TickComponent(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType, FActorComponentTickFunction* ThisTickFunction) override;
 126: 
 127: protected:
 128: 	virtual void BeginPlay() override;
 129: 
 130: 	float GetGameValue(FName Key, float DefaultValue) const;
 131: 
 132: private:
 133: 	void PoseChain(FGBTentacleChain& Chain, const FGBTentacleTuning& Tuning, float WaveScale, bool bSolving);
 134: 	void ReadTuning(FGBTentacleTuning& OutTuning) const;
 135: 	int32 PickReachChain() const;
 136: 
 137: 	TWeakObjectPtr<UPoseableMeshComponent> TentacleMesh;
 138: 	TArray<FGBTentacleChain> Chains;
 139: 	/** Reference component-space rotation of the chains' parent bone (the skeleton root). */
 140: 	FQuat RootCompQuat = FQuat::Identity;
 141: 
 142: 	float Time = 0.f;
 143: 
 144: 	// Inertia lag: fore/aft and side bend angles (degrees) with spring velocities.
 145: 	float LagFwd = 0.f;
 146: 	float LagFwdVel = 0.f;
 147: 	float LagSide = 0.f;
 148: 	float LagSideVel = 0.f;
 149: 	float PrevYaw = 0.f;
 150: 	bool bHasPrevYaw = false;
 151: 
 152: 	// Reach: world target, ramped weight, and which chain solves (-1 = closest tip).
 153: 	FVector ReachTargetWorld = FVector::ZeroVector;
 154: 	float ReachWeight = 0.f;
 155: 	float ReachDesired = 0.f;
 156: 	int32 ReachChain = -1;
 157: 
 158: 	// Hit ripple: time since AddRipple and its strength.
 159: 	float RippleElapsed = 1000.f;
 160: 	float RippleStrengthNow = 0.f;
 161: 
 162: 	bool bLimp = false;
 163: 	float LimpWeight = 0.f;
 164: };
FILE AI/GBTentacleMath.cpp
   1: #include "AI/GBTentacleMath.h"
   2: 
   3: namespace GBTentacle
   4: {
   5: 	void SolveFabrik(TArray<FVector>& Joints, const FVector& Target, int32 Iterations, float Tolerance)
   6: 	{
   7: 		const int32 Count = Joints.Num();
   8: 		if (Count < 2)
   9: 		{
  10: 			return;
  11: 		}
  12: 		if (Tolerance < 0.f)
  13: 		{
  14: 			Tolerance = 0.f;
  15: 		}
  16: 
  17: 		TArray<float> Lengths;
  18: 		Lengths.SetNumUninitialized(Count - 1);
  19: 		float TotalLength = 0.f;
  20: 		for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Count - 1; ++Index)
  21: 		{
  22: 			const float Segment = (Joints[Index + 1] - Joints[Index]).Size();
  23: 			Lengths[Index] = Segment;
  24: 			TotalLength += Segment;
  25: 		}
  26: 
  27: 		const FVector Root = Joints[0];
  28: 		const FVector ToTarget = Target - Root;
  29: 		if (ToTarget.Size() >= TotalLength && TotalLength > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
  30: 		{
  31: 			// Out of reach: point every segment straight at the target.
  32: 			const FVector Direction = ToTarget.GetSafeNormal();
  33: 			for (int32 Index = 1; Index < Count; ++Index)
  34: 			{
  35: 				Joints[Index] = Joints[Index - 1] + Direction * Lengths[Index - 1];
  36: 			}
  37: 			return;
  38: 		}
  39: 
  40: 		const int32 Passes = FMath::Max(Iterations, 0);
  41: 		for (int32 Pass = 0; Pass <= Passes; ++Pass)
  42: 		{
  43: 			if ((Joints[Count - 1] - Target).Size() <= Tolerance)
  44: 			{
  45: 				break;
  46: 			}
  47: 			if (Pass >= Passes)
  48: 			{
  49: 				break;
  50: 			}
  51: 			// Forward: pin the tip to the target, walk back to the root.
  52: 			Joints[Count - 1] = Target;
  53: 			for (int32 Index = Count - 2; Index >= 0; --Index)
  54: 			{
  55: 				const FVector Direction = (Joints[Index] - Joints[Index + 1]).GetSafeNormal();
  56: 				Joints[Index] = Joints[Index + 1] + Direction * Lengths[Index];
  57: 			}
  58: 			// Backward: pin the root, walk out to the tip.
  59: 			Joints[0] = Root;
  60: 			for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Count - 1; ++Index)
  61: 			{
  62: 				const FVector Direction = (Joints[Index + 1] - Joints[Index]).GetSafeNormal();
  63: 				Joints[Index + 1] = Joints[Index] + Direction * Lengths[Index];
  64: 			}
  65: 		}
  66: 
  67: 		// The root never moves, even with zero iterations.
  68: 		Joints[0] = Root;
  69: 	}
  70: 
  71: 	float SpringStep(float& Value, float& Velocity, float Target, float Stiffness, float Damping, float Dt)
  72: 	{
  73: 		if (Dt <= 0.f)
  74: 		{
  75: 			return Value;
  76: 		}
  77: 		// Semi-implicit Euler at a small fixed sub-step: stable for stiff springs at large frame times.
  78: 		const float MaxStep = 1.f / 120.f;
  79: 		float Remaining = Dt;
  80: 		while (Remaining > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
  81: 		{
  82: 			const float Step = FMath::Min(Remaining, MaxStep);
  83: 			Velocity += (Stiffness * (Target - Value) - Damping * Velocity) * Step;
  84: 			Value += Velocity * Step;
  85: 			Remaining -= Step;
  86: 		}
  87: 		return Value;
  88: 	}
  89: 
  90: 	float WaveAngle(float Time, float Frequency, float Phase, float BoneIndex, float WaveLength, float Amplitude, int32 BoneCount)
  91: 	{
  92: 		if (BoneCount <= 0 || Amplitude == 0.f)
  93: 		{
  94: 			return 0.f;
  95: 		}
  96: 		const float SafeLength = FMath::Max(WaveLength, KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER);
  97: 		const float Growth = (BoneIndex + 1.f) / static_cast<float>(BoneCount);
  98: 		return Amplitude * Growth * FMath::Sin(2.f * PI * (Frequency * Time + Phase) - BoneIndex / SafeLength);
  99: 	}
 100: }
FILE Tests/GBEntityTests.cpp
   1: // Pure-logic tests of the Entity tentacle math (AI/GBTentacleMath): FABRIK reach and the lag spring.
   2: #include "CoreMinimal.h"
   3: #include "Misc/AutomationTest.h"
   4: #include "AI/GBTentacleMath.h"
   5: 
   6: #if WITH_DEV_AUTOMATION_TESTS
   7: 
   8: namespace GBEntityTestsLocal
   9: {
  10: 	TArray<FVector> StraightChain(int32 Joints, float Spacing)
  11: 	{
  12: 		TArray<FVector> Chain;
  13: 		for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Joints; ++Index)
  14: 		{
  15: 			Chain.Add(FVector(Index * Spacing, 0.f, 0.f));
  16: 		}
  17: 		return Chain;
  18: 	}
  19: 
  20: 	bool SegmentsKept(const TArray<FVector>& Chain, float Spacing, float Tolerance)
  21: 	{
  22: 		for (int32 Index = 0; Index + 1 < Chain.Num(); ++Index)
  23: 		{
  24: 			if (!FMath::IsNearlyEqual((Chain[Index + 1] - Chain[Index]).Size(), Spacing, Tolerance))
  25: 			{
  26: 				return false;
  27: 			}
  28: 		}
  29: 		return true;
  30: 	}
  31: }
  32: 
  33: IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBEntityFabrikReachTest, "Glyphbound.Entity.FabrikReach",
  34: 	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
  35: 
  36: bool FGBEntityFabrikReachTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
  37: {
  38: 	using namespace GBEntityTestsLocal;
  39: 	TArray<FVector> Joints = StraightChain(5, 40.f);
  40: 	const FVector Target(100.f, 80.f, 0.f); // 128 cm from the root, inside the 160 cm reach
  41: 	GBTentacle::SolveFabrik(Joints, Target, 50, 0.1f);
  42: 	TestTrue(TEXT("Tip ends within the tolerance"), (Joints.Last() - Target).Size() <= 0.1f);
  43: 	TestTrue(TEXT("Root does not move"), Joints[0].Equals(FVector::ZeroVector));
  44: 	TestTrue(TEXT("Every segment length is kept"), SegmentsKept(Joints, 40.f, 0.1f));
  45: 	return true;
  46: }
  47: 
  48: IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBEntityFabrikTooFarTest, "Glyphbound.Entity.FabrikTooFar",
  49: 	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
  50: 
  51: bool FGBEntityFabrikTooFarTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
  52: {
  53: 	using namespace GBEntityTestsLocal;
  54: 	TArray<FVector> Joints = StraightChain(5, 40.f);
  55: 	const FVector Target(500.f, 60.f, 0.f); // far outside the 160 cm reach
  56: 	GBTentacle::SolveFabrik(Joints, Target, 50, 0.1f);
  57: 	TestTrue(TEXT("Every segment length is kept"), SegmentsKept(Joints, 40.f, 0.1f));
  58: 	const FVector Direction = (Target - FVector::ZeroVector).GetSafeNormal();
  59: 	bool bStraight = true;
  60: 	for (const FVector& Joint : Joints)
  61: 	{
  62: 		// Distance of the joint from the root-to-target line.
  63: 		const float LineDist = FVector::CrossProduct(Joint - FVector::ZeroVector, Direction).Size();
  64: 		bStraight = bStraight && LineDist <= 0.1f;
  65: 	}
  66: 	TestTrue(TEXT("The chain is straight and points at the target"), bStraight);
  67: 	return true;
  68: }
  69: 
  70: IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBEntitySpringSettlesTest, "Glyphbound.Entity.SpringSettles",
  71: 	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
  72: 
  73: bool FGBEntitySpringSettlesTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
  74: {
  75: 	constexpr float Stiffness = 60.f;
  76: 	const float Damping = 2.f * FMath::Sqrt(Stiffness); // critical-ish damping
  77: 	bool bOvershot = false;
  78: 	float Value = 0.f;
  79: 	float Velocity = 0.f;
  80: 	for (int32 Step = 0; Step < 60; ++Step) // 1 s at 60 Hz
  81: 	{
  82: 		GBTentacle::SpringStep(Value, Velocity, 10.f, Stiffness, Damping, 1.f / 60.f);
  83: 		bOvershot = bOvershot || Value > 10.5f;
  84: 	}
  85: 	TestTrue(TEXT("Never overshoots by more than 5 percent"), !bOvershot);
  86: 	TestTrue(TEXT("Settled within 0.1 of the target in 1 s"), FMath::Abs(Value - 10.f) <= 0.1f);
  87: 
  88: 	// A long frame (dt 0.1) stays finite and settles too.
  89: 	float BigValue = 0.f;
  90: 	float BigVelocity = 0.f;
  91: 	bool bFinite = true;
  92: 	for (int32 Step = 0; Step < 200; ++Step)
  93: 	{
  94: 		GBTentacle::SpringStep(BigValue, BigVelocity, 10.f, Stiffness, Damping, 0.1f);
  95: 		bFinite = bFinite && FMath::IsFinite(BigValue) && FMath::IsFinite(BigVelocity);
  96: 	}
  97: 	TestTrue(TEXT("Stable at dt 0.1"), bFinite);
  98: 	TestTrue(TEXT("Settled at dt 0.1 too"), FMath::Abs(BigValue - 10.f) <= 0.2f);
  99: 	return true;
 100: }
 101: 
 102: #endif // WITH_DEV_AUTOMATION_TESTS
FILE Combat/GBProjectile.cpp
   1: #include "Combat/GBProjectile.h"

  22: {
  23: 	/** Same lift as the radial damage obstruction trace (GBCombatLibrary.cpp), so a blast on the floor is not blocked by it. */
  24: 	constexpr float KnockbackTraceLiftCm = 5.f;

  26: 
  27: AGBProjectile::AGBProjectile()
  28: {
  29: 	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
  30: 
  31: 	Collision = CreateDefaultSubobject<USphereComponent>(TEXT("Collision"));
  32: 	Collision->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::QueryOnly);
  33: 	Collision->SetCollisionObjectType(ECC_WorldDynamic);
  34: 	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToAllChannels(ECR_Ignore);
  35: 	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Pawn, ECR_Overlap);
  36: 	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_WorldDynamic, ECR_Overlap);
  37: 	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_WorldStatic, ECR_Block);
  38: 	Collision->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(true);
  39: 	Collision->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
  40: 	Collision->OnComponentHit.AddDynamic(this, &AGBProjectile::OnCollisionHit);
  41: 	Collision->OnComponentBeginOverlap.AddDynamic(this, &AGBProjectile::OnCollisionOverlap);
  42: 	RootComponent = Collision;
  43: 
  44: 	Movement = CreateDefaultSubobject<UProjectileMovementComponent>(TEXT("Movement"));
  45: 	Movement->UpdatedComponent = Collision;
  46: 	Movement->bRotationFollowsVelocity = true;
  47: 	// The launch velocity is given in world space by InitProjectile.
  48: 	Movement->bInitialVelocityInLocalSpace = false;
  49: 	Movement->bShouldBounce = false;
  50: 	Movement->ProjectileGravityScale = 0.f;
  51: 
  52: 	Sprite = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBillboardComponent>(TEXT("Sprite"));
  53: 	Sprite->SetupAttachment(Collision);
  54: 
  55: 	Mesh = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("Mesh"));
  56: 	Mesh->SetupAttachment(Collision);
  57: 	Mesh->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
  58: 	Mesh->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
  59: 	Mesh->SetCastShadow(false);
  60: 	Mesh->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
  61: 
  62: 	// Energy bolt parts: empty and dark unless InitProjectile turns them on.
  63: 	BoltTrail = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("BoltTrail"));
  64: 	BoltTrail->SetupAttachment(Collision);
  65: 	BoltTrail->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
  66: 	BoltTrail->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
  67: 	BoltTrail->SetCastShadow(false);
  68: 	BoltTrail->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
  69: 
  70: 	BoltLight = CreateDefaultSubobject<UPointLightComponent>(TEXT("BoltLight"));
  71: 	BoltLight->SetupAttachment(Collision);
  72: 	BoltLight->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
  73: 	BoltLight->CastShadows = false;
  74: 	BoltLight->IntensityUnits = ELightUnits::Candelas;
  75: 	BoltLight->Intensity = 0.f;
  76: 	BoltLight->SetVisibility(false);
  77: }
  78: 
  79: AGBProjectile* AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(UWorld* World, const FGBProjectileConfig& InConfig, const FVector& Origin, const FVector& Direction, AActor* InInstigator)
  80: {
  81: 	if (!World)
  82: 	{
  83: 		return nullptr;
  84: 	}
  85: 
  86: 	const FVector LaunchDirection = Direction.IsNearlyZero() ? FVector::ForwardVector : Direction.GetSafeNormal();
  87: 	const FTransform SpawnTransform(LaunchDirection.Rotation(), Origin);
  88: 
  89: 	AGBProjectile* Projectile = World->SpawnActorDeferred<AGBProjectile>(AGBProjectile::StaticClass(), SpawnTransform, InInstigator,
  90: 		Cast<APawn>(InInstigator), ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn);
  91: 	if (!Projectile)
  92: 	{
  93: 		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Projectile: spawn failed"));
  94: 		return nullptr;
  95: 	}
  96: 
  97: 	Projectile->InitProjectile(InConfig, LaunchDirection, InInstigator);
  98: 	Projectile->FinishSpawning(SpawnTransform);
  99: 
 100: 	if (IsValid(InInstigator) && IsValid(Projectile))
 101: 	{
 102: 		Projectile->Collision->IgnoreActorWhenMoving(InInstigator, true);
 103: 		if (UPrimitiveComponent* InstigatorRoot = Cast<UPrimitiveComponent>(InInstigator->GetRootComponent()))
 104: 		{
 105: 			InstigatorRoot->IgnoreActorWhenMoving(Projectile, true);
 106: 		}
 107: 	}
 108: 
 109: 	return IsValid(Projectile) ? Projectile : nullptr;
 110: }
 111: 
 112: void AGBProjectile::InitProjectile(const FGBProjectileConfig& InConfig, const FVector& Direction, AActor* InInstigator)
 113: {
 114: 	Config = InConfig;
 115: 	if (InInstigator)
 116: 	{
 117: 		Config.Damage.Instigator = InInstigator;
 118: 	}
 119: 	Config.Damage.Causer = this;
 120: 	StartLocation = GetActorLocation();
 121: 
 122: 	const float Radius = FMath::Max(Config.Radius, 1.f);
 123: 	Collision->SetSphereRadius(Radius);
 124: 
 125: 	Movement->InitialSpeed = Config.Speed;
 126: 	Movement->MaxSpeed = Config.Speed;
 127: 	Movement->ProjectileGravityScale = Config.GravityScale;
 128: 	Movement->bShouldBounce = Config.FuseTime > 0.f;
 129: 	Movement->Bounciness = Config.Bounciness;
 130: 	Movement->Friction = Config.Friction;
 131: 	Movement->Velocity = Direction.GetSafeNormal() * Config.Speed;
 132: 
 133: 	const float VisualSize = Radius * 2.f * FMath::Max(Config.VisualScale, 0.01f);
 134: 	if (Config.bPixelArt)
 135: 	{
 136: 		Mesh->SetVisibility(false);
 137: 		if (UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* SpriteLibrary = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(this))
 138: 		{
 139: 			SpriteLibrary->SetupBillboard(Sprite, Config.SpriteId, VisualSize);
 140: 		}
 141: 		else
 142: 		{
 143: 			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Projectile: no pixel sprite library; sprite '%s' not shown"), *Config.SpriteId.ToString());
 144: 		}
 145: 	}
 146: 	else if (Config.bEnergyBolt)
 147: 	{
 148: 		Sprite->SetVisibility(false);
 149: 		Sprite->SetHiddenInGame(true);
 150: 		// Ellipsoids stretched along the local X axis, which the movement keeps pointing along the velocity.
 151: 		const float Length = FMath::Max(Config.BoltLength, VisualSize);
 152: 		const float Thick = FMath::Max(Config.BoltThickness, 1.f);
 153: 		const FLinearColor HotCore = FMath::Lerp(Config.Color, FLinearColor::White, 0.2f);
 154: 		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Mesh, EGBShape::Sphere, FVector(Length, Thick, Thick), HotCore, /*bGlow*/ true);
 155: 		const float TrailLength = FMath::Max(Config.BoltTrailLength, 0.f);
 156: 		if (TrailLength > 1.f)
 157: 		{
 158: 			GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(BoltTrail, EGBShape::Sphere, FVector(TrailLength, Thick * 0.6f, Thick * 0.6f), Config.Color * 0.35f, /*bGlow*/ true);
 159: 			BoltTrail->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(-(Length * 0.5f + TrailLength * 0.35f), 0.f, 0.f));
 160: 		}
 161: 		if (Config.BoltLightIntensity > 0.f)
 162: 		{
 163: 			BoltLight->SetLightColor(Config.Color);
 164: 			BoltLight->SetIntensity(Config.BoltLightIntensity);
 165: 			BoltLight->SetAttenuationRadius(FMath::Max(Config.BoltLightRadius, 1.f));
 166: 			BoltLight->SetVisibility(true);
 167: 		}
 168: 	}
 169: 	else
 170: 	{
 171: 		Sprite->SetVisibility(false);
 172: 		Sprite->SetHiddenInGame(true);
 173: 		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Mesh, EGBShape::Sphere, FVector(VisualSize), Config.Color, /*bGlow*/ true);
 174: 	}
 175: }
 176: 
 177: float AGBProjectile::GetBoltLength() const
 178: {
 179: 	return (Config.bEnergyBolt && !Config.bPixelArt && Mesh) ? Mesh->GetRelativeScale3D().X * 100.f : 0.f;
 180: }
 181: 
 182: bool AGBProjectile::HasBoltLight() const
 183: {
 184: 	return Config.bEnergyBolt && !Config.bPixelArt && BoltLight && BoltLight->IsVisible() && BoltLight->Intensity > 0.f;
 185: }
 186: 
 187: void AGBProjectile::BeginPlay()
 188: {
 189: 	Super::BeginPlay();
 190: }
 191: 
 192: void AGBProjectile::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
 193: {
 194: 	// Undo the instigator's ignore entry so its list does not grow with every shot.
 195: 	AActor* ProjectileInstigator = Config.Damage.Instigator.Get();
 196: 	if (IsValid(ProjectileInstigator))
 197: 	{
 198: 		if (UPrimitiveComponent* InstigatorRoot = Cast<UPrimitiveComponent>(ProjectileInstigator->GetRootComponent()))
 199: 		{
 200: 			InstigatorRoot->IgnoreActorWhenMoving(this, false);
 201: 		}
 202: 	}
 203: 
 204: 	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
 205: }
 206: 
 207: void AGBProjectile::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
 208: {
 209: 	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
 210: 
 211: 	if (bFinished)
 212: 	{
 213: 		return;
 214: 	}
 215: 
 216: 	const FVector Location = GetActorLocation();
 217: 	const float Age = GetGameTimeSinceCreation();
 218: 	const bool bOutOfRange = Config.MaxRange > 0.f && FVector::Dist(StartLocation, Location) >= Config.MaxRange;
 219: 	const bool bTooOld = Config.MaxLifetime > 0.f && Age > Config.MaxLifetime;
 220: 	const bool bFuseDone = Config.FuseTime > 0.f && Age >= Config.FuseTime;
 221: 
 222: 	if (bOutOfRange || bTooOld || bFuseDone)
 223: 	{
 224: 		if (Config.ExplosionRadius > 0.f)
 225: 		{
 226: 			Explode(Location);
 227: 		}
 228: 		Finish(nullptr, Location);
 229: 	}
 230: }
 231: 
 232: bool AGBProjectile::IsValidTarget(const AActor* Other) const
 233: {
 234: 	if (!IsValid(Other) || Other == this || Other->IsA<AGBProjectile>())
 235: 	{
 236: 		return false;
 237: 	}
 238: 
 239: 	const AActor* ProjectileInstigator = Config.Damage.Instigator.Get();
 240: 	if (Other == ProjectileInstigator)
 241: 	{
 242: 		return false;
 243: 	}
 244: 
 245: 	if (!UGBCombatLibrary::CanDamage(ProjectileInstigator, Other))
 246: 	{
 247: 		return false;
 248: 	}
 249: 
 250: 	for (const TWeakObjectPtr<AActor>& Damaged : DamagedActors)
 251: 	{
 252: 		if (Damaged.Get() == Other)
 253: 		{
 254: 			return false;
 255: 		}
 256: 	}
 257: 	return true;
 258: }
 259: 
 260: void AGBProjectile::HitTarget(AActor* Other, const FVector& ImpactPoint)
 261: {
 262: 	DamagedActors.Add(Other);
 263: 
 264: 	if (Config.ExplosionRadius > 0.f)
 265: 	{
 266: 		Explode(ImpactPoint);
 267: 	}
 268: 	else
 269: 	{
 270: 		FGBDamageInfo Info = Config.Damage;
 271: 		Info.Causer = this;
 272: 		Info.HitLocation = ImpactPoint;
 273: 		Info.HitDirection = Movement->Velocity.GetSafeNormal();
 274: 		// Only shots with a head bonus (damage or build-up) look for the weak point: a spell bolt on the
 275: 		// head is a normal hit, so its damage number is not shown as a headshot.
 276: 		if (Config.HeadshotMult > 1.f || Info.WeakPointBuildUpMult > 1.f)
 277: 		{
 278: 			UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyWeakPointBonus(Other, Info, Config.HeadshotMult);
 279: 		}
 280: 		UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Other, Info);
 281: 	}
 282: 
 283: 	// Neutral things with attributes (ice walls, destructibles) stop the shot even when it pierces.
 284: 	const bool bHitNeutral = UGBCombatLibrary::GetTeam(Other) == EGBTeam::Neutral;
 285: 	if (!Config.bPierce || bHitNeutral)
 286: 	{
 287: 		Finish(Other, ImpactPoint);
 288: 	}
 289: }
 290: 
 291: void AGBProjectile::OnCollisionOverlap(UPrimitiveComponent* OverlappedComponent, AActor* OtherActor, UPrimitiveComponent* OtherComp,
 292: 	int32 OtherBodyIndex, bool bFromSweep, const FHitResult& SweepResult)
 293: {
 294: 	if (bFinished || !IsValidTarget(OtherActor))
 295: 	{
 296: 		return;
 297: 	}
 298: 
 299: 	const FVector ImpactPoint = (bFromSweep && !SweepResult.bStartPenetrating) ? FVector(SweepResult.ImpactPoint) : GetActorLocation();
 300: 	HitTarget(OtherActor, ImpactPoint);
 301: }
 302: 
 303: void AGBProjectile::OnCollisionHit(UPrimitiveComponent* HitComponent, AActor* OtherActor, UPrimitiveComponent* OtherComp,
 304: 	FVector NormalImpulse, const FHitResult& Hit)
 305: {
 306: 	if (bFinished)
 307: 	{
 308: 		return;
 309: 	}
 310: 
 311: 	const FVector ImpactPoint = Hit.ImpactPoint;
 312: 
 313: 	if (IsValidTarget(OtherActor))
 314: 	{
 315: 		HitTarget(OtherActor, ImpactPoint);
 316: 		return;
 317: 	}
 318: 
 319: 	// The world: grenades keep bouncing until the fuse ends.
 320: 	if (Config.FuseTime > 0.f)
 321: 	{
 322: 		return;
 323: 	}
 324: 
 325: 	if (Config.ExplosionRadius > 0.f)
 326: 	{
 327: 		Explode(ImpactPoint);
 328: 	}
 329: 	Finish(OtherActor, ImpactPoint);
 330: }
 331: 
 332: void AGBProjectile::Explode(const FVector& Origin)
 333: {
 334: 	FGBDamageInfo Info = Config.Damage;
 335: 	Info.Causer = this;
 336: 	UGBCombatLibrary::DealRadialDamage(this, Origin, Config.ExplosionRadius, Info, TConstArrayView<AActor*>(), Config.bExplosionFalloff);
 337: 
 338: 	// Seen and felt even when the blast hits nothing.
 339: 	AGBExplosionEffect::Spawn(GetWorld(), Origin, Config.ExplosionRadius, Config.Color, Config.ExplosionEffectTime);
 340: 	if (Config.ExplosionKnockback > 0.f)
 341: 	{
 342: 		ApplyExplosionKnockback(Origin);
 343: 	}
 344: }
 345: 
 346: void AGBProjectile::ApplyExplosionKnockback(const FVector& Origin)
 347: {
 348: 	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
 349: 	const float Radius = Config.ExplosionRadius;
 350: 	if (!World || Radius <= 0.f)
 351: 	{
 352: 		return;
 353: 	}
 354: 
 355: 	// Smallest upward part of the push direction: a walking character pushed only sideways is stopped at once by
 356: 	// ground friction; a little lift makes it fly, so the push is felt even for a blast at the target's height.
 357: 	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
 358: 	const float KnockbackMinLift = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBProjectileKey::KnockbackMinLift, 0.35f) : 0.35f;
 359: 
 360: 	FCollisionObjectQueryParams ObjectParams;
 361: 	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_Pawn);
 362: 	FCollisionQueryParams QueryParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBExplosionKnockback), false, this);
 363: 	TArray<FOverlapResult> Overlaps;
 364: 	World->OverlapMultiByObjectType(Overlaps, Origin, FQuat::Identity, ObjectParams, FCollisionShape::MakeSphere(Radius), QueryParams);
 365: 
 366: 	AActor* ProjectileInstigator = Config.Damage.Instigator.Get();
 367: 	if (!IsValid(ProjectileInstigator))
 368: 	{
 369: 		ProjectileInstigator = nullptr;
 370: 	}
 371: 
 372: 	TSet<ACharacter*> Visited;
 373: 	for (const FOverlapResult& Overlap : Overlaps)
 374: 	{
 375: 		ACharacter* Character = Cast<ACharacter>(Overlap.GetActor());
 376: 		if (!IsValid(Character) || Character == ProjectileInstigator || Visited.Contains(Character))
 377: 		{
 378: 			continue;
 379: 		}
 380: 		Visited.Add(Character);
 381: 
 382: 		if (!UGBCombatLibrary::CanDamage(ProjectileInstigator, Character))
 383: 		{
 384: 			continue;
 385: 		}
 386: 
 387: 		const FVector TargetLocation = Character->GetActorLocation();
 388: 		const float Distance = FVector::Dist(TargetLocation, Origin);
 389: 		if (Distance > Radius)
 390: 		{
 391: 			continue;
 392: 		}
 393: 
 394: 		// Same rule as the blast damage: solid geometry between the blast and the target stops the push.
 395: 		FCollisionQueryParams TraceParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBKnockbackObstruction), true, this);
 396: 		TraceParams.AddIgnoredActor(Character);
 397: 		FHitResult Obstruction;
 398: 		const FVector TraceStart = Origin + FVector(0.f, 0.f, GBProjectileLocal::KnockbackTraceLiftCm);
 399: 		if (World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Obstruction, TraceStart, TargetLocation, ECC_Visibility, TraceParams)
 400: 			&& Obstruction.bBlockingHit && Obstruction.GetActor() && Obstruction.GetActor() != Character)
 401: 		{
 402: 			continue;
 403: 		}
 404: 
 405: 		const float Strength = Config.ExplosionKnockback * FMath::Clamp(1.f - Distance / Radius, 0.f, 1.f);
 406: 		if (Strength <= 0.f)
 407: 		{
 408: 			continue;
 409: 		}
 410: 
 411: 		FVector Direction = (TargetLocation - Origin).GetSafeNormal();
 412: 		if (Direction.IsNearlyZero())
 413: 		{
 414: 			// Blast right on the target: push it back along the projectile's flight.
 415: 			Direction = Movement ? Movement->Velocity.GetSafeNormal() : FVector::ZeroVector;
 416: 		}
 417: 		Direction.Z = FMath::Max(Direction.Z, KnockbackMinLift);
 418: 		Direction = Direction.GetSafeNormal();
 419: 		Character->LaunchCharacter(Direction * Strength, /*bXYOverride*/ true, /*bZOverride*/ true);
 420: 	}
 421: }
 422: 
 423: void AGBProjectile::Finish(AActor* HitActor, const FVector& Location)
 424: {
 425: 	if (bFinished)
 426: 	{
 427: 		return;
 428: 	}
 429: 	bFinished = true;
 430: 
 431: 	if (Movement)
 432: 	{
 433: 		Movement->StopMovementImmediately();
 434: 	}
 435: 	OnImpact.Broadcast(this, HitActor, Location);
 436: 	Destroy();
 437: }

   2: #pragma once

  16: /** Keys of Game.json "Values" read when a projectile is launched. */
  17: namespace GBProjectileKey
  18: {
  19: 	inline const FName KnockbackMinLift(TEXT("KnockbackMinLift")); // smallest upward part of the blast push direction
  20: 	inline const FName ProjectileMaxLifetime(TEXT("ProjectileMaxLifetime")); // seconds before a projectile removes itself
  21: 	inline const FName SpellProjectileLifetimeMargin(TEXT("SpellProjectileLifetimeMargin")); // extra seconds on top of Range / Speed, so a spell bolt is never removed before reaching its range
  22: }
  23: 
  24: /** Everything a projectile needs; filled from weapon stats, spell params or item stats by whoever fires it. */
  25: USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
  26: struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBProjectileConfig
  27: {
  28: 	GENERATED_BODY()
  29: 
  30: 	/** The hit dealt on impact (Amount, Element, Instigator, SourceId, OnHitEffect...). Causer is set to the projectile. */
  31: 	UPROPERTY() FGBDamageInfo Damage;
  32: 
  33: 	/** Weak point multiplier (GDD 3): 1 = no bonus. Applied to direct hits via UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyWeakPointBonus. */
  34: 	UPROPERTY() float HeadshotMult = 1.f;
  35: 
  36: 	UPROPERTY() float Speed = 3000.f;
  37: 	/** Collision radius, cm. */
  38: 	UPROPERTY() float Radius = 12.f;
  39: 	/** 0 = flies straight; 1 = normal gravity (grenades). */
  40: 	UPROPERTY() float GravityScale = 0.f;
  41: 	/** The projectile disappears after travelling this far (cm). 0 = use MaxLifetime only. */
  42: 	UPROPERTY() float MaxRange = 5000.f;
  43: 	UPROPERTY() float MaxLifetime = 10.f;
  44: 	/** > 0: on impact (or when the fuse / range ends) it deals Damage in this radius instead of a single hit. */
  45: 	UPROPERTY() float ExplosionRadius = 0.f;
  46: 	UPROPERTY() bool bExplosionFalloff = true;
  47: 	/** cm/s pushed onto hostile characters by the blast: full at the center, 0 at the edge. 0 = no push. */
  48: 	UPROPERTY() float ExplosionKnockback = 0.f;
  49: 	/** Seconds the explosion shockwave (AGBExplosionEffect) is shown. Every explosion shows it, hit or miss. */
  50: 	UPROPERTY() float ExplosionEffectTime = 0.35f;
  51: 	/** > 0: it does not explode on contact with the world; it bounces and explodes after this many seconds (grenades). */
  52: 	UPROPERTY() float FuseTime = 0.f;
  53: 	/** Bounce retention (UProjectileMovementComponent::Bounciness). Only used when FuseTime > 0. */
  54: 	UPROPERTY() float Bounciness = 0.6f;
  55: 	/** Surface friction when bouncing (UProjectileMovementComponent::Friction). Only used when FuseTime > 0. */
  56: 	UPROPERTY() float Friction = 0.2f;
  57: 	/** Keeps going through actors it damages (each actor once). */
  58: 	UPROPERTY() bool bPierce = false;
  59: 
  60: 	// ---- Looks (art rule: bPixelArt = camera-facing sprite, otherwise a small 3D mesh) ----
  61: 	UPROPERTY() bool bPixelArt = true;
  62: 	/** Sprite id from Sprites.json (pixel art). */
  63: 	UPROPERTY() FName SpriteId = NAME_None;
  64: 	UPROPERTY() FLinearColor Color = FLinearColor::White;
  65: 	/** Visual scale relative to the collision radius. */
  66: 	UPROPERTY() float VisualScale = 1.f;
  67: 
  68: 	// ---- Energy bolt look (3D bosses): off by default, so every other projectile keeps its look ----
  69: 	/** Non-pixel-art only: an elongated glowing core along the velocity, a dim stretched trail behind it and a point light. */
  70: 	UPROPERTY() bool bEnergyBolt = false;
  71: 	/** Core length and thickness, cm. */
  72: 	UPROPERTY() float BoltLength = 75.f;
  73: 	UPROPERTY() float BoltThickness = 12.f;
  74: 	/** Length of the dim stretched trail behind the core, cm. */
  75: 	UPROPERTY() float BoltTrailLength = 220.f;
  76: 	/** Point light riding the bolt: candelas (0 = no light) and attenuation radius in cm. Shadows are off. */
  77: 	UPROPERTY() float BoltLightIntensity = 0.f;
  78: 	UPROPERTY() float BoltLightRadius = 600.f;
  79: };

  10: 	// Technical constants, not tuning: bone name shape, FABRIK budget, lag clamp, ripple shape.

  12: 	constexpr int32 ReachIterations = 10;
  13: 	constexpr float ReachToleranceCm = 0.5f;    // well under the 15 cm the smoke run allows

  21: 	FQuat AxisAngleQuat(const FVector& Axis, float AngleDeg)
  22: 	{
  23: 		FVector SafeAxis = Axis.GetSafeNormal();
  24: 		if (SafeAxis.IsNearlyZero())
  25: 		{
  26: 			return FQuat::Identity;
  27: 		}
  28: 		return FQuat(SafeAxis, FMath::DegreesToRadians(AngleDeg));
  29: 	}

  63: void UGBTentacleRigComponent::ReadTuning(FGBTentacleTuning& OutTuning) const
  64: {
  65: 	using namespace GBEntityTentacle;
  66: 	OutTuning.WaveFreq = GetGameValue(WaveFreq, WaveFreqDefault);
  67: 	OutTuning.WaveAmp = GetGameValue(WaveAmp, WaveAmpDefault);

  70: 	OutTuning.LagStiffness = GetGameValue(LagStiffness, LagStiffnessDefault);

  74: 	OutTuning.ReachBlendOut = GetGameValue(ReachBlendOut, ReachBlendOutDefault);

  79: 
  80: void UGBTentacleRigComponent::Setup(UPoseableMeshComponent* InMesh)
  81: {
  82: 	Chains.Reset();
  83: 	TentacleMesh = InMesh;
  84: 	ReachWeight = 0.f;
  85: 	ReachDesired = 0.f;
  86: 
  87: 	if (!IsValid(InMesh) || !InMesh->GetSkinnedAsset())
  88: 	{
  89: 		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("TentacleRig: no poseable mesh with a skeletal mesh, tentacles stay still"));
  90: 		return;
  91: 	}
  92: 
  93: 	TArray<FName> BoneNames;
  94: 	InMesh->GetBoneNames(BoneNames);
  95: 
  96: 	// Group T<cc>_<bb> bones into chains sorted by bone index.
  97: 	TMap<int32, TArray<TPair<int32, FName>>> Groups;
  98: 	for (const FName& BoneName : BoneNames)
  99: 	{
 100: 		const FString Name = BoneName.ToString();
 101: 		if (Name.Len() != GBTentacleRigLocal::MaxNameLength || !Name.StartsWith(TEXT("T")) || Name[3] != TCHAR('_'))
 102: 		{
 103: 			continue;
 104: 		}
 105: 		const int32 ChainIndex = FCString::Atoi(*Name.Mid(1, 2));
 106: 		const int32 BoneIndex = FCString::Atoi(*Name.Mid(4, 2));
 107: 		// Guard the shape: only T<digits>_<digits>.
 108: 		if (!FChar::IsDigit(Name[1]) || !FChar::IsDigit(Name[2]) || !FChar::IsDigit(Name[4]) || !FChar::IsDigit(Name[5]))
 109: 		{
 110: 			continue;
 111: 		}
 112: 		Groups.FindOrAdd(ChainIndex).Add(TPair<int32, FName>(BoneIndex, BoneName));
 113: 	}
 114: 
 115: 	TArray<int32> ChainKeys;
 116: 	Groups.GetKeys(ChainKeys);
 117: 	ChainKeys.Sort();
 118: 
 119: 	RootCompQuat = FQuat::Identity;
 120: 	if (BoneNames.Contains(TEXT("root")))
 121: 	{
 122: 		RootCompQuat = InMesh->GetBoneTransformByName(TEXT("root"), EBoneSpaces::ComponentSpace).GetRotation();
 123: 	}
 124: 
 125: 	for (int32 ChainKey : ChainKeys)
 126: 	{
 127: 		TArray<TPair<int32, FName>>& Pairs = Groups.FindChecked(ChainKey);
 128: 		Pairs.Sort([](const TPair<int32, FName>& A, const TPair<int32, FName>& B) { return A.Key < B.Key; });
 129: 
 130: 		FGBTentacleChain Chain;
 131: 		Chain.Phase = FMath::FRand();
 132: 		FQuat ParentComp = RootCompQuat;
 133: 		for (const TPair<int32, FName>& Pair : Pairs)
 134: 		{
 135: 			const FTransform CompT = InMesh->GetBoneTransformByName(Pair.Value, EBoneSpaces::ComponentSpace);
 136: 			Chain.Bones.Add(Pair.Value);
 137: 			Chain.RefComp.Add(CompT.GetRotation());
 138: 			Chain.RefJoints.Add(CompT.GetLocation());
 139: 			Chain.RefLocal.Add(ParentComp.Inverse() * CompT.GetRotation());
 140: 			ParentComp = CompT.GetRotation();
 141: 		}
 142: 		const int32 BoneCount = Chain.Bones.Num();
 143: 		if (BoneCount == 0)
 144: 		{
 145: 			continue;
 146: 		}
 147: 		// The tip joint continues past the last bone along the last segment.
 148: 		const FVector LastDir = (Chain.RefJoints[BoneCount - 1] - Chain.RefJoints[BoneCount - 2 >= 0 ? BoneCount - 2 : 0]).GetSafeNormal();
 149: 		const float LastLen = BoneCount >= 2 ? (Chain.RefJoints[BoneCount - 1] - Chain.RefJoints[BoneCount - 2]).Size() : 10.f;
 150: 		Chain.RefJoints.Add(Chain.RefJoints[BoneCount - 1] + LastDir * LastLen);
 151: 
 152: 		for (int32 Bone = 0; Bone < BoneCount; ++Bone)
 153: 		{
 154: 			const float Segment = (Chain.RefJoints[Bone + 1] - Chain.RefJoints[Bone]).Size();
 155: 			Chain.SegLengths.Add(FMath::Max(Segment, KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER));
 156: 			const FVector RefDir = (Chain.RefJoints[Bone + 1] - Chain.RefJoints[Bone]).GetSafeNormal();
 157: 			const FVector LocalDir = Chain.RefComp[Bone].Inverse() * RefDir;
 158: 			Chain.LocalDirs.Add(LocalDir);
 159: 			// Bend about the two axes perpendicular to the bone's dominant axis, whatever the rig's convention is.
 160: 			const float AX = FMath::Abs(LocalDir.X);
 161: 			const float AY = FMath::Abs(LocalDir.Y);
 162: 			if (AX >= AY && AX >= FMath::Abs(LocalDir.Z))
 163: 			{
 164: 				Chain.BendAxisA.Add(1);
 165: 				Chain.BendAxisB.Add(2);
 166: 			}
 167: 			else if (AY >= AX && AY >= FMath::Abs(LocalDir.Z))
 168: 			{
 169: 				Chain.BendAxisA.Add(0);
 170: 				Chain.BendAxisB.Add(2);
 171: 			}
 172: 			else
 173: 			{
 174: 				Chain.BendAxisA.Add(0);
 175: 				Chain.BendAxisB.Add(1);
 176: 			}
 177: 		}
 178: 		Chain.FKJoints = Chain.RefJoints;
 179: 		Chains.Add(MoveTemp(Chain));
 180: 	}
 181: 
 182: 	UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("TentacleRig: %d chains ready"), Chains.Num());
 183: }
 184: 
 185: int32 UGBTentacleRigComponent::GetBoneCount() const
 186: {
 187: 	int32 Count = 0;
 188: 	for (const FGBTentacleChain& Chain : Chains)
 189: 	{
 190: 		Count += Chain.Bones.Num();
 191: 	}
 192: 	return Count;
 193: }

 195: FVector UGBTentacleRigComponent::GetChainTipPosition(int32 ChainIndex) const
 196: {
 197: 	if (!Chains.IsValidIndex(ChainIndex) || Chains[ChainIndex].FKJoints.Num() == 0)
 198: 	{
 199: 		return FVector::ZeroVector;
 200: 	}
 201: 	return Chains[ChainIndex].FKJoints.Last();
 202: }

 207: 	{
 208: 		return FVector::ZeroVector;
 209: 	}
 210: 	return TentacleMesh->GetComponentTransform().TransformPosition(GetChainTipPosition(ChainIndex));

 239: 
 240: int32 UGBTentacleRigComponent::PickReachChain() const
 241: {
 242: 	if (Chains.Num() == 0)
 243: 	{
 244: 		return INDEX_NONE;
 245: 	}
 246: 	if (Chains.IsValidIndex(ReachChain))
 247: 	{
 248: 		return ReachChain;
 249: 	}
 250: 	// The chain whose tip is currently closest to the target reaches.
 251: 	const FVector TargetLocal = TentacleMesh.IsValid()
 252: 		? TentacleMesh->GetComponentTransform().InverseTransformPosition(ReachTargetWorld)
 253: 		: ReachTargetWorld;
 254: 	int32 Best = 0;
 255: 	float BestDist = FLT_MAX;
 256: 	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Chains.Num(); ++Index)
 257: 	{
 258: 		const float Dist = FVector::Dist(GetChainTipPosition(Index), TargetLocal);
 259: 		if (Dist < BestDist)
 260: 		{
 261: 			BestDist = Dist;
 262: 			Best = Index;
 263: 		}
 264: 	}
 265: 	return Best;
 266: }
 267: 
 268: void UGBTentacleRigComponent::TickComponent(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType, FActorComponentTickFunction* ThisTickFunction)
 269: {
 270: 	Super::TickComponent(DeltaTime, TickType, ThisTickFunction);
 271: 	if (!IsReady())
 272: 	{
 273: 		return;
 274: 	}
 275: 
 276: 	FGBTentacleTuning Tuning;
 277: 	ReadTuning(Tuning);
 278: 
 279: 	const float Dt = FMath::Min(FMath::Max(DeltaTime, 0.f), GBTentacleRigLocal::MaxDeltaTime);
 280: 	Time += Dt;
 281: 
 282: 	// Inertia: actor velocity and yaw rate in the actor frame (X forward, Y right, Z up).
 283: 	FVector LocalVel = FVector::ZeroVector;
 284: 	float YawRate = 0.f;
 285: 	if (AActor* Owner = GetOwner())
 286: 	{
 287: 		const FQuat ActorQuat = Owner->GetActorQuat();
 288: 		LocalVel = ActorQuat.Inverse().RotateVector(Owner->GetVelocity());
 289: 		const float Yaw = Owner->GetActorRotation().Yaw;
 290: 		if (bHasPrevYaw && Dt > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
 291: 		{
 292: 			YawRate = FMath::DegreesToRadians(FMath::FindDeltaAngleDegrees(PrevYaw, Yaw) / Dt);
 293: 		}
 294: 		PrevYaw = Yaw;
 295: 		bHasPrevYaw = true;
 296: 	}
 297: 
 298: 	const float FwdTarget = FMath::Clamp(LocalVel.X * Tuning.LagStrength,
 299: 		-GBTentacleRigLocal::MaxLagDegrees, GBTentacleRigLocal::MaxLagDegrees);
 300: 	const float SideTarget = FMath::Clamp(-(LocalVel.Y + YawRate * GBTentacleRigLocal::YawLeverCm) * Tuning.LagStrength,
 301: 		-GBTentacleRigLocal::MaxLagDegrees, GBTentacleRigLocal::MaxLagDegrees);
 302: 	GBTentacle::SpringStep(LagFwd, LagFwdVel, FwdTarget, Tuning.LagStiffness, Tuning.LagDamping, Dt);
 303: 	GBTentacle::SpringStep(LagSide, LagSideVel, SideTarget, Tuning.LagStiffness, Tuning.LagDamping, Dt);
 304: 
 305: 	// Ramps: reach weight and limp.
 306: 	const float BlendIn = FMath::Max(Tuning.ReachBlendIn, KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER);
 307: 	const float BlendOut = FMath::Max(Tuning.ReachBlendOut, KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER);
 308: 	const float ReachStep = (ReachDesired > ReachWeight ? Dt / BlendIn : Dt / BlendOut);
 309: 	ReachWeight = FMath::Clamp(ReachWeight + FMath::Clamp(ReachDesired - ReachWeight, -ReachStep, ReachStep), 0.f, 1.f);
 310: 
 311: 	const float LimpGoal = bLimp ? 1.f : 0.f;
 312: 	LimpWeight = FMath::Clamp(LimpWeight + FMath::Clamp(LimpGoal - LimpWeight, -Dt, Dt), 0.f, 1.f);
 313: 	const float WaveScale = 1.f - LimpWeight;
 314: 
 315: 	RippleElapsed += Dt;
 316: 
 317: 	const int32 SolvingChain = (ReachWeight > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER) ? PickReachChain() : INDEX_NONE;
 318: 	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Chains.Num(); ++Index)
 319: 	{
 320: 		PoseChain(Chains[Index], Tuning, WaveScale, Index == SolvingChain);
 321: 	}
 322: }
 323: 
 324: void UGBTentacleRigComponent::PoseChain(FGBTentacleChain& Chain, const FGBTentacleTuning& Tuning, float WaveScale, bool bSolving)
 325: {
 326: 	UPoseableMeshComponent* Mesh = TentacleMesh.Get();
 327: 	if (!Mesh)
 328: 	{
 329: 		return;
 330: 	}
 331: 	const int32 BoneCount = Chain.Bones.Num();
 332: 	if (BoneCount == 0)
 333: 	{
 334: 		return;
 335: 	}
 336: 
 337: 	// Reach blend of this chain: the other layers fade out by the weight.
 338: 	const float ReachW = bSolving ? ReachWeight : 0.f;
 339: 	const float OtherScale = (1.f - ReachW) * WaveScale;
 340: 
 341: 	// Hit ripple: a pulse running from the root to the tip over RippleDuration.
 342: 	float RippleAmp = 0.f;
 343: 	float RippleFreq = 0.f;
 344: 	const float RippleDuration = FMath::Max(Tuning.RippleDuration, KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER);
 345: 	const float RippleFront = RippleElapsed / RippleDuration;
 346: 	if (RippleFront < 1.5f)
 347: 	{
 348: 		RippleAmp = RippleStrengthNow;
 349: 		RippleFreq = RippleFront;
 350: 	}
 351: 
 352: 	// FABRIK for the solving chain, from the current pose towards the target.
 353: 	TArray<FVector> Solved;
 354: 	if (bSolving)
 355: 	{
 356: 		Solved = Chain.FKJoints;
 357: 		const FVector TargetLocal = Mesh->GetComponentTransform().InverseTransformPosition(ReachTargetWorld);
 358: 		GBTentacle::SolveFabrik(Solved, TargetLocal, GBTentacleRigLocal::ReachIterations, GBTentacleRigLocal::ReachToleranceCm);
 359: 	}
 360: 
 361: 	FQuat ParentComp = RootCompQuat;
 362: 	for (int32 Bone = 0; Bone < BoneCount; ++Bone)
 363: 	{
 364: 		const float Growth = static_cast<float>(Bone + 1) / static_cast<float>(BoneCount);
 365: 		const FVector RefDir = (Chain.RefJoints[Bone + 1] - Chain.RefJoints[Bone]).GetSafeNormal();
 366: 
 367: 		// Layer 1: idle wave, two perpendicular bends, out of step per chain.
 368: 		float BendA = GBTentacle::WaveAngle(Time, Tuning.WaveFreq, Chain.Phase, static_cast<float>(Bone),
 369: 			Tuning.WaveLength, Tuning.WaveAmp, BoneCount);
 370: 		float BendB = GBTentacle::WaveAngle(Time, Tuning.WaveFreq, Chain.Phase + 0.25f, static_cast<float>(Bone),
 371: 			Tuning.WaveLength, Tuning.WaveAmp, BoneCount);
 372: 		// Layer 4: hit ripple on the first bend axis.
 373: 		if (RippleAmp != 0.f)
 374: 		{
 375: 			const float D = (RippleFreq - Growth) * GBTentacleRigLocal::RippleWidth;
 376: 			BendA += RippleAmp * FMath::Exp(-D * D)
 377: 				* FMath::Sin(2.f * PI * (GBTentacleRigLocal::RippleWaves * RippleFreq - Growth));
 378: 		}
 379: 		BendA *= OtherScale;
 380: 		BendB *= OtherScale;
 381: 		if (Bone < GBTentacleRigLocal::RootLimitedBones)
 382: 		{
 383: 			BendA = FMath::Clamp(BendA, -Tuning.RootLimit, Tuning.RootLimit);
 384: 			BendB = FMath::Clamp(BendB, -Tuning.RootLimit, Tuning.RootLimit);
 385: 		}
 386: 		const FQuat Extra = FQuat(GBTentacleRigLocal::AxisVector(Chain.BendAxisA[Bone]), FMath::DegreesToRadians(BendA))
 387: 			* FQuat(GBTentacleRigLocal::AxisVector(Chain.BendAxisB[Bone]), FMath::DegreesToRadians(BendB));
 388: 		const FQuat Local = Chain.RefLocal[Bone] * Extra;
 389: 
 390: 		// Layer 2: inertia lag as a small component-space bend (scaled out on the reach chain).
 391: 		const float LagScale = Growth * (1.f - ReachW);
 392: 		const FQuat LagDelta = FQuat(FVector::YAxisVector, FMath::DegreesToRadians(LagFwd * LagScale))
 393: 			* FQuat(FVector::XAxisVector, FMath::DegreesToRadians(LagSide * LagScale));
 394: 		FQuat Comp = LagDelta * ParentComp * Local;
 395: 
 396: 		// Limp droop: rotate the bone direction towards -Z (frame-independent).
 397: 		const float Droop = Tuning.LimpDroop * LimpWeight * Growth * (1.f - ReachW);
 398: 		if (FMath::Abs(Droop) > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
 399: 		{
 400: 			const FVector Dir = (Comp * Chain.LocalDirs[Bone]).GetSafeNormal();
 401: 			const FVector Axis = FVector::CrossProduct(Dir, FVector::DownVector);
 402: 			if (!Axis.IsNearlyZero())
 403: 			{
 404: 				Comp = GBTentacleRigLocal::AxisAngleQuat(Axis, Droop) * Comp;
 405: 			}
 406: 		}
 407: 
 408: 		if (bSolving && Solved.Num() == Chain.FKJoints.Num())
 409: 		{
 410: 			// Layer 3: turn the solved joint positions back into bone rotations (twist-minimal
 411: 			// against the reference) and blend the composed pose towards them by the weight.
 412: 			// At full weight the FK below reproduces the solved joints exactly.
 413: 			const FVector SolvedDir = (Solved[Bone + 1] - Solved[Bone]).GetSafeNormal();
 414: 			const FQuat SolvedComp = FQuat::FindBetweenNormals(RefDir, SolvedDir) * Chain.RefComp[Bone];
 415: 			Comp = FQuat::Slerp(Comp, SolvedComp, ReachW);
 416: 		}
 417: 		Comp.Normalize();
 418: 
 419: 		Mesh->SetBoneRotationByName(Chain.Bones[Bone], Comp.Rotator(), EBoneSpaces::ComponentSpace);
 420: 
 421: 		// Forward kinematics for the next bone (and the tip queries): exact, from the same rotations.
 422: 		const FVector BoneDir = (Comp * Chain.LocalDirs[Bone]).GetSafeNormal();
 423: 		Chain.FKJoints[Bone + 1] = Chain.FKJoints[Bone] + BoneDir * Chain.SegLengths[Bone];
 424: 		ParentComp = Comp;
 425: 	}
 426: 	// The root joint never moves.
 427: 	Chain.FKJoints[0] = Chain.RefJoints[0];
 428: }
   1: #include "AI/GBTentacleMath.h"
   2: 
   3: namespace GBTentacle
   4: {
   5: 	void SolveFabrik(TArray<FVector>& Joints, const FVector& Target, int32 Iterations, float Tolerance)
   6: 	{
   7: 		const int32 Count = Joints.Num();
   8: 		if (Count < 2)
   9: 		{
  10: 			return;
  11: 		}
  12: 		if (Tolerance < 0.f)
  13: 		{
  14: 			Tolerance = 0.f;
  15: 		}
  16: 
  17: 		TArray<float> Lengths;
  18: 		Lengths.SetNumUninitialized(Count - 1);
  19: 		float TotalLength = 0.f;
  20: 		for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Count - 1; ++Index)
  21: 		{
  22: 			const float Segment = (Joints[Index + 1] - Joints[Index]).Size();
  23: 			Lengths[Index] = Segment;
  24: 			TotalLength += Segment;
  25: 		}
  26: 
  27: 		const FVector Root = Joints[0];
  28: 		const FVector ToTarget = Target - Root;
  29: 		if (ToTarget.Size() >= TotalLength && TotalLength > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
  30: 		{
  31: 			// Out of reach: point every segment straight at the target.
  32: 			const FVector Direction = ToTarget.GetSafeNormal();
  33: 			for (int32 Index = 1; Index < Count; ++Index)
  34: 			{
  35: 				Joints[Index] = Joints[Index - 1] + Direction * Lengths[Index - 1];
  36: 			}
  37: 			return;
  38: 		}
  39: 
  40: 		const int32 Passes = FMath::Max(Iterations, 0);
  41: 		for (int32 Pass = 0; Pass <= Passes; ++Pass)
  42: 		{
  43: 			if ((Joints[Count - 1] - Target).Size() <= Tolerance)
  44: 			{
  45: 				break;
  46: 			}
  47: 			if (Pass >= Passes)
  48: 			{
  49: 				break;
  50: 			}
  51: 			// Forward: pin the tip to the target, walk back to the root.
  52: 			Joints[Count - 1] = Target;
  53: 			for (int32 Index = Count - 2; Index >= 0; --Index)
  54: 			{
  55: 				const FVector Direction = (Joints[Index] - Joints[Index + 1]).GetSafeNormal();
  56: 				Joints[Index] = Joints[Index + 1] + Direction * Lengths[Index];
  57: 			}
  58: 			// Backward: pin the root, walk out to the tip.
  59: 			Joints[0] = Root;
  60: 			for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Count - 1; ++Index)
  61: 			{
  62: 				const FVector Direction = (Joints[Index + 1] - Joints[Index]).GetSafeNormal();
  63: 				Joints[Index + 1] = Joints[Index] + Direction * Lengths[Index];
  64: 			}
  65: 		}
  66: 
  67: 		// The root never moves, even with zero iterations.
  68: 		Joints[0] = Root;
  69: 	}
  70: 
  71: 	float SpringStep(float& Value, float& Velocity, float Target, float Stiffness, float Damping, float Dt)
  72: 	{
  73: 		if (Dt <= 0.f)
  74: 		{
  75: 			return Value;
  76: 		}
  77: 		// Semi-implicit Euler at a small fixed sub-step: stable for stiff springs at large frame times.
  78: 		const float MaxStep = 1.f / 120.f;
  79: 		float Remaining = Dt;
  80: 		while (Remaining > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
  81: 		{
  82: 			const float Step = FMath::Min(Remaining, MaxStep);
  83: 			Velocity += (Stiffness * (Target - Value) - Damping * Velocity) * Step;
  84: 			Value += Velocity * Step;
  85: 			Remaining -= Step;
  86: 		}
  87: 		return Value;
  88: 	}
  89: 
  90: 	float WaveAngle(float Time, float Frequency, float Phase, float BoneIndex, float WaveLength, float Amplitude, int32 BoneCount)
  91: 	{
  92: 		if (BoneCount <= 0 || Amplitude == 0.f)
  93: 		{
  94: 			return 0.f;
  95: 		}
  96: 		const float SafeLength = FMath::Max(WaveLength, KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER);
  97: 		const float Growth = (BoneIndex + 1.f) / static_cast<float>(BoneCount);
  98: 		return Amplitude * Growth * FMath::Sin(2.f * PI * (Frequency * Time + Phase) - BoneIndex / SafeLength);
  99: 	}
 100: }
   1: // Pure-logic tests of the Entity tentacle math (AI/GBTentacleMath): FABRIK reach and the lag spring.

   3: #include "Misc/AutomationTest.h"
   4: #include "AI/GBTentacleMath.h"
   5: 
   6: #if WITH_DEV_AUTOMATION_TESTS
   7: 
   8: namespace GBEntityTestsLocal
   9: {
  10: 	TArray<FVector> StraightChain(int32 Joints, float Spacing)
  11: 	{
  12: 		TArray<FVector> Chain;
  13: 		for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Joints; ++Index)
  14: 		{
  15: 			Chain.Add(FVector(Index * Spacing, 0.f, 0.f));
  16: 		}
  17: 		return Chain;
  18: 	}
  19: 
  20: 	bool SegmentsKept(const TArray<FVector>& Chain, float Spacing, float Tolerance)
  21: 	{
  22: 		for (int32 Index = 0; Index + 1 < Chain.Num(); ++Index)
  23: 		{
  24: 			if (!FMath::IsNearlyEqual((Chain[Index + 1] - Chain[Index]).Size(), Spacing, Tolerance))
  25: 			{
  26: 				return false;
  27: 			}
  28: 		}
  29: 		return true;
  30: 	}
  31: }

  60: 	for (const FVector& Joint : Joints)
  61: 	{
  62: 		// Distance of the joint from the root-to-target line.
  63: 		const float LineDist = FVector::CrossProduct(Joint - FVector::ZeroVector, Direction).Size();
  64: 		bStraight = bStraight && LineDist <= 0.1f;
  65: 	}
  66: 	TestTrue(TEXT("The chain is straight and points at the target"), bStraight);
  67: 	return true;

  73: bool FGBEntitySpringSettlesTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
  74: {
  75: 	constexpr float Stiffness = 60.f;
  76: 	const float Damping = 2.f * FMath::Sqrt(Stiffness); // critical-ish damping
  77: 	bool bOvershot = false;
  78: 	float Value = 0.f;
  79: 	float Velocity = 0.f;
  80: 	for (int32 Step = 0; Step < 60; ++Step) // 1 s at 60 Hz
  81: 	{
  82: 		GBTentacle::SpringStep(Value, Velocity, 10.f, Stiffness, Damping, 1.f / 60.f);
  83: 		bOvershot = bOvershot || Value > 10.5f;
  84: 	}
  85: 	TestTrue(TEXT("Never overshoots by more than 5 percent"), !bOvershot);
  86: 	TestTrue(TEXT("Settled within 0.1 of the target in 1 s"), FMath::Abs(Value - 10.f) <= 0.1f);
  87: 
  88: 	// A long frame (dt 0.1) stays finite and settles too.
  89: 	float BigValue = 0.f;
  90: 	float BigVelocity = 0.f;
  91: 	bool bFinite = true;
  92: 	for (int32 Step = 0; Step < 200; ++Step)
  93: 	{
  94: 		GBTentacle::SpringStep(BigValue, BigVelocity, 10.f, Stiffness, Damping, 0.1f);
  95: 		bFinite = bFinite && FMath::IsFinite(BigValue) && FMath::IsFinite(BigVelocity);
  96: 	}
  97: 	TestTrue(TEXT("Stable at dt 0.1"), bFinite);
  98: 	TestTrue(TEXT("Settled at dt 0.1 too"), FMath::Abs(BigValue - 10.f) <= 0.2f);
  99: 	return true;

#include "World/GBEraComponent.h"

#include "GameFramework/Actor.h"
#include "GameFramework/Pawn.h"
#include "GameFramework/PawnMovementComponent.h"
#include "World/GBEraSubsystem.h"
#include "World/GBProgressSubsystem.h"

UGBEraComponent::UGBEraComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = false;
}

void UGBEraComponent::Configure(EGBEraPresence InPresence, FName InRequiredFlag, FName InRemovedByFlag)
{
	Presence = InPresence;
	RequiredFlag = InRequiredFlag;
	RemovedByFlag = InRemovedByFlag;
	if (HasBegunPlay())
	{
		Refresh();
	}
}

bool UGBEraComponent::ComputePresent(EGBEraPresence InPresence, EGBEra CurrentEra, bool bRequiredFlagOk, bool bRemovedFlagSet)
{
	return UGBEraSubsystem::IsPresentIn(InPresence, CurrentEra) && bRequiredFlagOk && !bRemovedFlagSet;
}

void UGBEraComponent::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		EraChangedHandle = Era->OnEraChanged.AddUObject(this, &UGBEraComponent::HandleEraChanged);
	}
	if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		FlagChangedHandle = Progress->OnFlagChanged.AddUObject(this, &UGBEraComponent::HandleFlagChanged);
	}
	Refresh();
}

void UGBEraComponent::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Era->OnEraChanged.Remove(EraChangedHandle);
	}
	if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Progress->OnFlagChanged.Remove(FlagChangedHandle);
	}
	EraChangedHandle.Reset();
	FlagChangedHandle.Reset();

	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void UGBEraComponent::HandleEraChanged(EGBEra NewEra)
{
	Refresh();
}

void UGBEraComponent::HandleFlagChanged(FName Flag)
{
	if (Flag == RequiredFlag || Flag == RemovedByFlag)
	{
		Refresh();
	}
}

void UGBEraComponent::Refresh()
{
	const UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this);
	const EGBEra CurrentEra = Era ? Era->GetCurrentEra() : EGBEra::Past;

	const UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this);
	const bool bRequiredOk = RequiredFlag.IsNone() || (Progress && Progress->HasFlag(RequiredFlag));
	const bool bRemoved = !RemovedByFlag.IsNone() && Progress && Progress->HasFlag(RemovedByFlag);

	ApplyPresence(ComputePresent(Presence, CurrentEra, bRequiredOk, bRemoved));
}

void UGBEraComponent::ApplyPresence(bool bNewPresent)
{
	AActor* Owner = GetOwner();
	if (!Owner || bNewPresent == bPresent)
	{
		return;
	}
	bPresent = bNewPresent;

	APawn* Pawn = Cast<APawn>(Owner);
	UMovementComponent* Movement = Pawn ? Pawn->GetMovementComponent() : nullptr;

	if (!bNewPresent)
	{
		// Remember the owner's own settings (it may have turned collision or tick off itself).
		bSavedHidden = Owner->IsHidden();
		bSavedCollision = Owner->GetActorEnableCollision();
		bSavedTick = Owner->IsActorTickEnabled();
		bHasSaved = true;

		// Remember every ticking component first (movement included), so the other era simulates nothing.
		SavedTickComponents.Reset();
		TArray<UActorComponent*> Components;
		Owner->GetComponents<UActorComponent>(Components);
		for (UActorComponent* Comp : Components)
		{
			if (Comp && Comp != this && Comp->IsComponentTickEnabled())
			{
				SavedTickComponents.Add(Comp);
				Comp->SetComponentTickEnabled(false);
			}
		}

		Owner->SetActorHiddenInGame(true);
		Owner->SetActorEnableCollision(false);
		Owner->SetActorTickEnabled(false);
		// An enemy of the other era must neither fall (its collision is off) nor walk.
		if (Movement)
		{
			Movement->StopMovementImmediately();
			Movement->SetComponentTickEnabled(false);
		}
	}
	else
	{
		Owner->SetActorHiddenInGame(bHasSaved ? bSavedHidden : false);
		Owner->SetActorEnableCollision(bHasSaved ? bSavedCollision : true);
		Owner->SetActorTickEnabled(bHasSaved ? bSavedTick : true);
		// Re-enable exactly the components that were ticking (a burning enemy keeps burning on return).
		for (TWeakObjectPtr<UActorComponent>& Saved : SavedTickComponents)
		{
			if (UActorComponent* Comp = Saved.Get())
			{
				Comp->SetComponentTickEnabled(true);
			}
		}
		SavedTickComponents.Reset();
		if (Movement && !bHasSaved)
		{
			Movement->SetComponentTickEnabled(true);
		}
	}

	OnPresenceChanged.Broadcast();
}
