304: 
305: 	if (State == EGBEnemyState::Dead || !EraComponent->IsPresent())
306: 	{
307: 		return;
308: 	}
309: 
310: 	if (ControlLockTime > 0.f)
311: 	{
312: 		ControlLockTime -= DeltaSeconds;
313: 		ResetStuck();
314: 		return;
315: 	}
316: 
317: 	StateTime += DeltaSeconds;
318: 	NavRetryTimer = FMath::Max(0.f, NavRetryTimer - DeltaSeconds);
319: 	SteerSideHoldTimer = FMath::Max(0.f, SteerSideHoldTimer - DeltaSeconds);
320: 	GoAroundTimer = FMath::Max(0.f, GoAroundTimer - DeltaSeconds);
321: 	bWantsToMove = false;
322: 
323: 	AActor* Goal = FindTarget();
324: 	Target = Goal;
325: 	if (!Goal)
326: 	{
327: 		if (State != EGBEnemyState::Idle)
328: 		{
329: 			StopMoving();
330: 			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Idle);
331: 		}
332: 		bProvoked = false;
333: 		bRepositioning = false;
334: 		ResetStuck();
335: 		return;
336: 	}
337: 
338: 	const float AggroRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AggroRange, 0.f);
339: 	const float AttackRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackRange, 0.f);
340: 	const bool bCanAttack = AttackRange > 0.f && Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackDamage, 0.f) > 0.f;
341: 	const float Distance = FVector::Dist(GetActorLocation(), Goal->GetActorLocation());
342: 
343: 	switch (State)
344: 	{
345: 	case EGBEnemyState::Idle:
346: 		// AggroRange <= 0: never acts (training dummy), even when hit.
347: 		if (AggroRange > 0.f && (bProvoked || Distance <= AggroRange))
348: 		{
349: 			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
350: 		}
351: 		break;
352: 
353: 	case EGBEnemyState::Chase:
354: 		// Line of sight for melee too: an orc on the other side of a low wall keeps going around it instead of swinging at stone.
355: 		if (bCanAttack && Distance <= AttackRange && HasLineOfSight(Goal))
356: 		{
357: 			StopMoving();
358: 			FaceTowards(Goal);
359: 			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Windup);
360: 		}
361: 		else
362: 		{
363: 			MoveTowards(Goal, DeltaSeconds);
364: 		}
365: 		break;
366: 
367: 	case EGBEnemyState::Windup:
368: 		// A shot needs a clear line: the player stepped behind cover, so reposition instead of shooting the wall.
369: 		if (IsRanged() && !HasLineOfSight(Goal))
370: 		{
371: 			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
372: 			MoveTowards(Goal, DeltaSeconds);
373: 			break;
374: 		}
375: 		FaceTowards(Goal);
376: 		if (StateTime >= Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackWindup, 0.f))
377: 		{
378: 			PerformAttack();
379: 			if (State == EGBEnemyState::Windup)
380: 			{
381: 				SetState(EGBEnemyState::Recover);
382: 			}
383: 		}
384: 		break;
385: 
386: 	case EGBEnemyState::Recover:
387: 		// Ranged enemies do not wait out the interval behind a wall: they move until they see the player again.
388: 		if (IsRanged() && !HasLineOfSight(Goal))
389: 		{
390: 			MoveTowards(Goal, DeltaSeconds);
391: 			bRepositioning = true;
392: 		}
393: 		else
394: 		{
395: 			if (bRepositioning)
396: 			{
397: 				StopMoving();
398: 				bRepositioning = false;
399: 			}
400: 			FaceTowards(Goal);
401: 		}
402: 		if (StateTime >= Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackInterval, 0.f))
403: 		{
404: 			bRepositioning = false;
405: 			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
406: 		}
407: 		break;
408: 
409: 	default:
410: 		break;
411: 	}
412: 
413: 	UpdateStuck(DeltaSeconds);
414: }
415: 
416: void AGBEnemyCharacter::MoveTowards(AActor* Goal, float DeltaSeconds)
417: {
418: 	if (!Goal || GetCharacterMovement()->MaxWalkSpeed <= 0.f)
419: 	{
420: 		return;
511: bool AGBEnemyCharacter::IsDirectionBlocked(const FVector& Direction, float Distance, const AActor* Goal) const
512: {
513: 	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
514: 	const UCapsuleComponent* Capsule = GetCapsuleComponent();
515: 	const UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement();
516: 	if (!World || !Capsule || !Move)
517: 	{
518: 		return false;
519: 	}
520: 
521: 	// Bottom lifted by the step height (the character walks over anything lower), top unchanged, a bit thinner.
522: 	const float StepHeight = FMath::Max(Move->MaxStepHeight, 0.f);
523: 	const float Radius = Capsule->GetScaledCapsuleRadius() * GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::SteerSweepRadiusScale, 0.9f);
524: 	const float HalfHeight = FMath::Max(Capsule->GetScaledCapsuleHalfHeight() - StepHeight * 0.5f, Radius);
525: 	const FVector Start = GetActorLocation() + FVector::UpVector * (StepHeight * 0.5f);
526: 
527: 	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBEnemySteer), false, this);
528: 	if (Goal)
529: 	{
530: 		Params.AddIgnoredActor(Goal);
531: 	}
532: 	// Other enemies are not obstacles to plan around: they move, and the capsules push each other aside.
533: 	FCollisionResponseParams Response;
534: 	Response.CollisionResponse.SetResponse(ECC_Pawn, ECR_Ignore);
535: 
536: 	FHitResult Hit;
537: 	if (World->SweepSingleByChannel(Hit, Start, Start + Direction * Distance, FQuat::Identity, ECC_Pawn,
538: 		FCollisionShape::MakeCapsule(Radius, HalfHeight), Params, Response))
539: 	{
540: 		if (Hit.bStartPenetrating)
541: 		{
542: 			// Already touching something: only directions into it are blocked.
543: 			if (FVector::DotProduct(Hit.Normal, Direction) < 0.f)
544: 			{
545: 				return true;
546: 			}
547: 		}
548: 		else
549: 		{
550: 			// A ramp or a slope it can walk up is not an obstacle.
551: 			if (Hit.ImpactNormal.Z < Move->GetWalkableFloorZ())
552: 			{
553: 				return true;
554: 			}
555: 		}
556: 	}
557: 
558: 	// No walkable ground ahead: the direction leads off a ledge.
559: 	const float ProbeDistance = FMath::Max(GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::SteerLedgeProbeDistance, 80.f), 0.f);
560: 	const float LedgeDrop = FMath::Max(GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::SteerLedgeDrop, 150.f), 0.f);
561: 	if (ProbeDistance <= 0.f)
562: 	{
563: 		return false;
564: 	}
565: 	if (Goal && FVector::Dist2D(GetActorLocation(), Goal->GetActorLocation()) <= ProbeDistance)
566: 	{
567: 		// The goal itself is within the probe distance: its position is never a ledge.
568: 		return false;
569: 	}
570: 	const float ScaledHalfHeight = Capsule->GetScaledCapsuleHalfHeight();
669: void AGBEnemyCharacter::StopMoving()
670: {
671: 	if (AController* C = GetController())
672: 	{
673: 		C->StopMovement();
674: 	}
675: 	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement())
676: 	{
677: 		Move->StopMovementImmediately();
678: 	}
679: }
680: 
681: void AGBEnemyCharacter::FaceTowards(const AActor* Goal)
682: {
683: 	if (!Goal)
684: 	{
685: 		return;
686: 	}
687: 	const FVector Direction = (Goal->GetActorLocation() - GetActorLocation()).GetSafeNormal2D();
688: 	if (!Direction.IsNearlyZero())
689: 	{
690: 		SetActorRotation(FRotator(0.f, Direction.Rotation().Yaw, 0.f));
691: 	}
692: }
693: 
694: FVector AGBEnemyCharacter::GetChestLocation() const
695: {
696: 	return GetActorLocation() + FVector::UpVector * (GetCapsuleComponent()->GetScaledCapsuleHalfHeight() * GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::AimHeightFraction, 0.5f));
697: }
698: 
699: bool AGBEnemyCharacter::HasLineOfSight(const AActor* Goal) const
700: {
701: 	if (!Goal || !GetWorld())
702: 	{
703: 		return false;
704: 	}
705: 	const FVector Start = GetChestLocation();
706: 	const FVector End = Goal->GetActorLocation();
707: 	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBEnemyLineOfSight), false, this);
708: 	for (int32 Attempt = 0; Attempt < 8; ++Attempt)
709: 	{
710: 		FHitResult Hit;
711: 		if (!GetWorld()->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, Start, End, ECC_Visibility, Params))
712: 		{
713: 			return true;
714: 		}
715: 		AActor* HitActor = Hit.GetActor();
716: 		if (HitActor == Goal)
717: 		{
718: 			return true;
719: 		}
720: 		if (HitActor && HitActor->IsA<APawn>())
721: 		{
722: 			Params.AddIgnoredActor(HitActor);
723: 			continue;
724: 		}
725: 		return false;
807: void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDamaged(const FGBDamageInfo& Info, float ActualDamage)
808: {
809: 	if (State != EGBEnemyState::Dead)
810: 	{
811: 		bProvoked = true;
812: 	}
813: 	OnDamagedVisual(Info, ActualDamage);
814: }
815: 
816: void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandlePresenceChanged()
817: {
818: 	ResetStuck();
819: 	if (!EraComponent->IsPresent() && State != EGBEnemyState::Dead)
820: 	{
821: 		StopMoving();
822: 		bRepositioning = false;
823: 		if (State == EGBEnemyState::Windup)
824: 		{
825: 			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
826: 		}
827: 	}
828: }
829: 
830: void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow)
831: {
832: 	if (State == EGBEnemyState::Dead)
833: 	{
834: 		return;
835: 	}
836: 	SetState(EGBEnemyState::Dead);
837: 	ControlLockTime = 0.f;
838: 
839: 	StopMoving();
840: 	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement())
841: 	{
842: 		Move->DisableMovement();
843: 	}
844: 	DetachFromControllerPendingDestroy();
845: 	SetActorEnableCollision(false);
846: 
847: 	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
848: 	const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
849: 	if (Def && !bFellOutOfWorld)
850: 	{
851: 		AGBPickup::SpawnDrops(World, Def->Drops, GetActorLocation());
852: 	}
853: 
854: 	OnDied.Broadcast(this);
855: 
856: 	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
857: 	const float CorpseTime = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::CorpseTime, 1.5f) : 1.5f;
858: 	// SetLifeSpan(0) would mean "forever"; keep a tiny minimum instead of destroying inside the damage call.
859: 	SetLifeSpan(FMath::Max(CorpseTime, 0.05f));
860: }
861: 
862: void AGBEnemyCharacter::InterruptAttack()
863: {
864: 	if (State == EGBEnemyState::Windup)
865: 	{
866: 		SetState(EGBEnemyState::Recover);
867: 	}
868: }

870: void AGBEnemyCharacter::ApplyControlLock(float Seconds)
rg: Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Weapons/GBMelee*: El nombre de archivo, el nombre de directorio o la sintaxis de la etiqueta del volumen no son correctos. (os error 123)
rg: Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Spells/*: El nombre de archivo, el nombre de directorio o la sintaxis de la etiqueta del volumen no son correctos. (os error 123)
// The Entity, phase 1 "the machine" (GDD 11): a floating metal shell with a big central eye
// (the weak point) and hologram tentacles driven by UGBTentacleRigComponent.
// The body, the eye and the tentacles are visuals parented to a scene root that floats above the
// ground capsule. The capsule keeps walking on the navmesh like every other enemy (pathing, local
// steering, ledge checks), but it no longer blocks the weapon channel: a sphere on the shell does
// (guns and projectiles hit what is drawn). The visual root flies: altitude drift, banking into
// motion, a 3D aim, a limited turn rate and spring knockback. The base class placeholder look is
// hidden once the real art loads; if any asset is missing the placeholder stays (one warning).
// Every tuning number is a Game.json "Values" entry prefixed Entity, with the default in code.
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "AI/GBEnemyCharacter.h"
#include "GBEntity.generated.h"

class UGBTentacleRigComponent;
class UPoseableMeshComponent;
class UStaticMeshComponent;
class USceneComponent;
class UMeshComponent;
class UMaterialInstanceDynamic;
class USphereComponent;
class UPointLightComponent;
class AGBProjectile;

/** Critically-tunable spring: X follows Target with natural frequency Omega (rad/s) and damping ratio Zeta. */
struct FGBEntitySpring
{
	float X = 0.f;
	float V = 0.f;
	void Step(float Target, float Dt, float Omega, float Zeta);
};

/** Entity flight, turn, hit and light numbers; filled from Game.json every tick (defaults in GBEntity.cpp). */
struct FGBEntityTuning
{
	float TurnMaxRate = 90.f, TurnGain = 3.f, TurnAccel = 5.f;
	float PitchMax = 30.f, AimOmega = 6.f, EyeTrackMax = 12.f, EyeTrackSpeed = 14.f;
	float AltMin = 250.f, AltMax = 450.f, AltDriftAmp = 70.f, AltDriftHz = 0.22f, AltRise = 60.f, AltDip = 45.f, AltDipTime = 0.7f, AltOmega = 3.f;
	float BankMax = 20.f, BankPerYawRate = 0.07f, LeanDeg = 10.f, AccelPitchDeg = 8.f, TiltOmega = 7.f, TiltZeta = 0.75f;
	float Acceleration = 700.f, Braking = 600.f, GroundFriction = 2.f;
	float StrafeSpeed = 0.7f, StrafeFlipMin = 1.8f, StrafeFlipMax = 4.f, StrafeKeep = 0.5f, StrafeProbe = 220.f;
	float HitTiltKick = 90.f, HitPushKick = 220.f, HitRefDamage = 100.f, HitMinShare = 0.15f, HitWeakMult = 2.f, HitOmega = 8.f, HitZeta = 0.3f;
	float ShellRadius = 88.f, EyeWeakRadius = 40.f;
	float EyeLightIntensity = 1500.f, EyeLightRadius = 1600.f, EyeLightPulseHz = 0.7f, EyeLightPulse = 0.15f;
	float EyeLightWindupFlare = 0.8f, EyeLightHitFlare = 2.f, EyeLightFade = 0.8f;
	float BoltLength = 80.f, BoltThickness = 12.f, BoltTrail = 240.f, BoltLightIntensity = 4000.f, BoltLightRadius = 700.f;
	float MuzzleIntensity = 15000.f, MuzzleRadius = 700.f, MuzzleTime = 0.12f;
};

UCLASS()
class GLYPHBOUND_API AGBEntity : public AGBEnemyCharacter
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

public:
	AGBEntity();

	virtual void Tick(float DeltaSeconds) override;

	/** Weak point: a hit inside the eye sphere counts (the top of the shell does not). */
	virtual bool IsHeadshotLocation(const FVector& HitLocation) const override;
	/** The Entity turns with weight in Tick (rate-limited), so the base class snap does nothing. */
	virtual void FaceTowards(const AActor* Goal) override;

	UGBTentacleRigComponent* GetTentacleRig() const { return TentacleRig; }
	FVector GetEyeCentreWorld() const;
	FVector GetShellTopWorld() const;
	FVector GetShellCentreWorld() const;

	/** One line for the smoke run: where the visual is and whether every part renders. */
	FString DescribeVisual() const;

	/** Smoke/tests: true suppresses attacks and holds the actor still (windup reach stays off too). */
	void SetAttackSuppressed(bool bSuppressed) { bAttackSuppressed = bSuppressed; }

	/** Smoke/tests: strafe round the target for this long even with attacks suppressed. */
	void ForceStrafe(float Seconds) { ForcedStrafeTime = Seconds; }
	/** Smoke/tests: fires one energy bolt from the closest tentacle tip along Direction (Speed > 0 overrides the speed). */
	AGBProjectile* FireDebugBolt(const FVector& Direction, float Speed);
	AGBProjectile* GetLastBolt() const;

	/** Smoke: peak values since the last reset. */
	void ResetMotionStats();
	float GetPeakYawRate() const { return PeakYawRate; }
	float GetShellZRange() const { return ShellZMax - ShellZMin; }
	float GetPeakRoll() const { return PeakRoll; }

protected:
	virtual void BeginPlay() override;
	virtual void InitFromDefinition(const FGBEnemyDefinition& Definition) override;
	virtual void PerformAttack() override;
	virtual void HandleDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow) override;
	virtual void OnDamagedVisual(const FGBDamageInfo& Info, float ActualDamage) override;

private:
	/** World start point of the ranged shot: the tip closest to the target, or the chest fallback. */
	FVector GetAttackOrigin(AActor* Goal) const;
	FString DescribeMaterials(UMeshComponent* InMesh) const;
	void ReadTuning();
	AGBProjectile* FireBolt(AActor* Goal, const FVector& Origin, const FVector& Direction, float SpeedOverride);
	FVector GetGoalHeadLocation(const AActor* Goal) const;
	void UpdateTurn(float Dt, const AActor* Goal);
	void UpdateStrafe(float Dt, const AActor* Goal);
	void UpdateFlight(float Dt, const AActor* Goal);
	void UpdateLights(float Dt);
	void BuildVisual();
	void ApplyFlash();

	UPROPERTY() TObjectPtr<USceneComponent> VisualRoot;
	UPROPERTY() TObjectPtr<UStaticMeshComponent> EntityBody;
	UPROPERTY() TObjectPtr<UStaticMeshComponent> EntityEye;
	UPROPERTY() TObjectPtr<UPoseableMeshComponent> EntityTentacles;
	UPROPERTY() TObjectPtr<UGBTentacleRigComponent> TentacleRig;

	UPROPERTY() TArray<TObjectPtr<UMaterialInstanceDynamic>> BodyMIDs;
	UPROPERTY() TArray<TObjectPtr<UMaterialInstanceDynamic>> EyeMIDs;
	UPROPERTY() TObjectPtr<UMaterialInstanceDynamic> TentacleMID;

	UPROPERTY() TObjectPtr<USphereComponent> ShellHit;
	UPROPERTY() TObjectPtr<UPointLightComponent> EyeLight;
	UPROPERTY() TObjectPtr<UPointLightComponent> MuzzleLight;
	TWeakObjectPtr<AGBProjectile> LastBolt;

	FGBEntityTuning Tuning;
	float CapsuleHalfHeightCached = 135.f;

	// Turn
	float YawRateDeg = 0.f;       // current turn rate, deg/s
	float PeakYawRate = 0.f;

	// Flight springs: altitude of the shell centre above the floor, aim pitch, motion pitch, bank roll,
	// hit tilt and the push offset (actor frame).
	FGBEntitySpring AltSpring, AimPitchSpring, MotionPitchSpring, BankRollSpring, HitPitchSpring, HitRollSpring;
	FGBEntitySpring PushX, PushY, PushZ;
	float EyeYaw = 0.f;
	float EyePitch = 0.f;
	float PrevLocalFwd = 0.f;
	float FwdAccelSmoothed = 0.f;
	float AltDipTimer = 0.f;
	float WindupAlpha = 0.f;
	bool bAltInitialised = false;
	float ShellZMin = 1e9f;
	float ShellZMax = -1e9f;
	float PeakRoll = 0.f;

	// Strafe
	float StrafeSign = 1.f;
	float StrafeTimer = 0.f;
	float ForcedStrafeTime = 0.f;
	bool bStrafingLastTick = false;

	// Lights
	float MuzzleTimer = 0.f;
	float DeathAlpha = 1.f;
	bool bDying = false;
	FLinearColor EyeLightColor = FLinearColor(0.23f, 1.f, 0.69f);

	float VisualBaseZ = 0.f;
	float FlashValue = 0.f;
	float BobTime = 0.f;
	bool bAttackSuppressed = false;
	bool bVisualBuilt = false;
};

	/** Same lift as the radial damage obstruction trace (GBCombatLibrary.cpp), so a blast on the floor is not blocked by it. */
	constexpr float KnockbackTraceLiftCm = 5.f;

AGBProjectile::AGBProjectile()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;

	Collision = CreateDefaultSubobject<USphereComponent>(TEXT("Collision"));
	Collision->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::QueryOnly);
	Collision->SetCollisionObjectType(ECC_WorldDynamic);
	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToAllChannels(ECR_Ignore);
	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Pawn, ECR_Overlap);
	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_WorldDynamic, ECR_Overlap);
	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_WorldStatic, ECR_Block);
	Collision->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(true);
	Collision->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
	Collision->OnComponentHit.AddDynamic(this, &AGBProjectile::OnCollisionHit);
	Collision->OnComponentBeginOverlap.AddDynamic(this, &AGBProjectile::OnCollisionOverlap);
	RootComponent = Collision;

	Movement = CreateDefaultSubobject<UProjectileMovementComponent>(TEXT("Movement"));
	Movement->UpdatedComponent = Collision;
	Movement->bRotationFollowsVelocity = true;
	// The launch velocity is given in world space by InitProjectile.
	Movement->bInitialVelocityInLocalSpace = false;
	Movement->bShouldBounce = false;
	Movement->ProjectileGravityScale = 0.f;

	Sprite = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBillboardComponent>(TEXT("Sprite"));
	Sprite->SetupAttachment(Collision);

	Mesh = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("Mesh"));
	Mesh->SetupAttachment(Collision);
	Mesh->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Mesh->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Mesh->SetCastShadow(false);
	Mesh->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);

	// Energy bolt parts: empty and dark unless InitProjectile turns them on.
	BoltTrail = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("BoltTrail"));
	BoltTrail->SetupAttachment(Collision);
	BoltTrail->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	BoltTrail->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	BoltTrail->SetCastShadow(false);
	BoltTrail->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);

	BoltLight = CreateDefaultSubobject<UPointLightComponent>(TEXT("BoltLight"));
	BoltLight->SetupAttachment(Collision);
	BoltLight->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
	BoltLight->CastShadows = false;
	BoltLight->IntensityUnits = ELightUnits::Candelas;
	BoltLight->Intensity = 0.f;
	BoltLight->SetVisibility(false);
}

AGBProjectile* AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(UWorld* World, const FGBProjectileConfig& InConfig, const FVector& Origin, const FVector& Direction, AActor* InInstigator)
{
	if (!World)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	const FVector LaunchDirection = Direction.IsNearlyZero() ? FVector::ForwardVector : Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	const FTransform SpawnTransform(LaunchDirection.Rotation(), Origin);

	AGBProjectile* Projectile = World->SpawnActorDeferred<AGBProjectile>(AGBProjectile::StaticClass(), SpawnTransform, InInstigator,
		Cast<APawn>(InInstigator), ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn);
	if (!Projectile)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Projectile: spawn failed"));
		return nullptr;
	}

	Projectile->InitProjectile(InConfig, LaunchDirection, InInstigator);
	Projectile->FinishSpawning(SpawnTransform);

	if (IsValid(InInstigator) && IsValid(Projectile))
	{
		Projectile->Collision->IgnoreActorWhenMoving(InInstigator, true);
		if (UPrimitiveComponent* InstigatorRoot = Cast<UPrimitiveComponent>(InInstigator->GetRootComponent()))
		{
			InstigatorRoot->IgnoreActorWhenMoving(Projectile, true);
		}
	}

	return IsValid(Projectile) ? Projectile : nullptr;
}

void AGBProjectile::InitProjectile(const FGBProjectileConfig& InConfig, const FVector& Direction, AActor* InInstigator)
{
	Config = InConfig;
	if (InInstigator)
	{
		Config.Damage.Instigator = InInstigator;
	}
	Config.Damage.Causer = this;
	StartLocation = GetActorLocation();

	const float Radius = FMath::Max(Config.Radius, 1.f);
	Collision->SetSphereRadius(Radius);

	Movement->InitialSpeed = Config.Speed;
	{
		return 0;
	}

	UWorld* World = GEngine->GetWorldFromContextObject(WorldContextObject, EGetWorldErrorMode::LogAndReturnNull);
	if (!World)
	{
		return 0;
	}

	FCollisionObjectQueryParams ObjectParams;
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_Pawn);
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_WorldDynamic);

	FCollisionQueryParams QueryParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBRadialDamage), false);
	for (AActor* Ignored : IgnoreActors)
	{
		if (Ignored)
		{
			QueryParams.AddIgnoredActor(Ignored);
		}
	}

	TArray<FOverlapResult> Overlaps;
	World->OverlapMultiByObjectType(Overlaps, Origin, FQuat::Identity, ObjectParams, FCollisionShape::MakeSphere(Radius), QueryParams);

	AActor* DamageInstigator = Info.Instigator.Get();
	if (!IsValid(DamageInstigator))
	{
		DamageInstigator = nullptr;
	}

	TSet<AActor*> Visited;
	int32 DamagedCount = 0;

	for (const FOverlapResult& Overlap : Overlaps)
	{
		AActor* Target = Overlap.GetActor();
		if (!IsValid(Target) || Visited.Contains(Target))
		{
			continue;
		}
		Visited.Add(Target);

		if (IgnoreActors.Contains(Target) || !UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target))
		{
			continue;
		}

		if (!CanDamage(DamageInstigator, Target))
		{
			continue;
		}

		const FVector TargetLocation = Target->GetActorLocation();
		const float Distance = FVector::Dist(TargetLocation, Origin);
		if (Distance > Radius)
		{
			continue;
		}

		// Solid geometry between the blast and the target blocks the damage.
		FCollisionQueryParams TraceParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBRadialObstruction), true);
		TraceParams.AddIgnoredActor(Target);
		for (AActor* Ignored : IgnoreActors)
		{
			if (Ignored)
			{
				TraceParams.AddIgnoredActor(Ignored);
			}
		}
		FHitResult Obstruction;
		const FVector TraceStart = Origin + FVector(0.f, 0.f, GBCombatLocal::RadialTraceLiftCm);
		if (World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Obstruction, TraceStart, TargetLocation, ECC_Visibility, TraceParams)
			&& Obstruction.bBlockingHit && Obstruction.GetActor() && Obstruction.GetActor() != Target)
		{
			continue;
		}

		FGBDamageInfo TargetInfo = Info;
		TargetInfo.HitLocation = TargetLocation;
		TargetInfo.HitDirection = (TargetLocation - Origin).GetSafeNormal();
		if (bLinearFalloff)
		{
			TargetInfo.Amount *= FMath::Clamp(1.f - Distance / Radius, 0.f, 1.f);
		}

		if (DealDamage(Target, TargetInfo) > 0.f)
		{
			++DamagedCount;
		}
	}

		Add(61.8f + S, TEXT("entity: spawn where the player can see it, attacks off"), [](UWorld* World, AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
		{
			Player->GetAttributes()->SetInvulnerable(true);
			const FVector PlayerLoc = Player->GetActorLocation();
			const FVector EyeLoc = Player->GetAimOrigin();
			EGBEraPresence Presence = EGBEraPresence::Both;
			if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(World))
			{
				Presence = Era->GetCurrentEra() == EGBEra::Past ? EGBEraPresence::PastOnly : EGBEraPresence::FutureOnly;
			}
			// The player may stand anywhere (an earlier step's grenade can throw them); try yaw
			// offsets until the line from the eyes to the spawn is clear, so no pillar hides the Entity.
			AGBEntity* Entity = nullptr;
			float PickedYaw = 0.f;
			float PickedDist = 0.f;
			for (float Dist : { 800.f, 500.f })
			{
				for (int32 K = 0; K < 8 && !Entity; ++K)
				{
					const float Yaw = K * 45.f;
					const float YawRad = FMath::DegreesToRadians(Yaw);
					const FVector At = PlayerLoc + FVector(FMath::Cos(YawRad), FMath::Sin(YawRad), 0.f) * Dist;
					FCollisionQueryParams LOSParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSmokeEntityLOS), false, Player);
					FHitResult LOSHit;
					if (World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(LOSHit, EyeLoc, At + FVector(0.f, 0.f, 100.f), ECC_Visibility, LOSParams))
					{
						continue;
					}
					Entity = Cast<AGBEntity>(AGBEnemyCharacter::SpawnEnemy(World, FName(TEXT("Entity_Phase1")), At, FRotator::ZeroRotator, Presence));
					if (Entity)
					{
						PickedYaw = Yaw;
						PickedDist = Dist;
					}
				}
				if (Entity)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			if (Entity)
			{
				Entity->SetAttackSuppressed(true);
				SmokeEntity = Entity;
			}
			else
			{
				SmokeEntity.Reset();
				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE entity: could not spawn Entity_Phase1 with a clear view"));
			}
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE entity spawned=%d yaw=%.0f dist=%.0f"), Entity ? 1 : 0, PickedYaw, PickedDist);
		});

UGBDataSubsystem* UGBDataSubsystem::Get(const UObject* WorldContextObject)
{
	if (!WorldContextObject)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	if (const UGameInstance* DirectInstance = Cast<UGameInstance>(WorldContextObject))
	{
		return DirectInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBDataSubsystem>();
	}
	const UWorld* World = GEngine ? GEngine->GetWorldFromContextObject(WorldContextObject, EGetWorldErrorMode::ReturnNull) : nullptr;
	const UGameInstance* GameInstance = World ? World->GetGameInstance() : nullptr;
	return GameInstance ? GameInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBDataSubsystem>() : nullptr;
}

void UGBDataSubsystem::Initialize(FSubsystemCollectionBase& Collection)
{
	Super::Initialize(Collection);
	LoadAll();
}

void UGBDataSubsystem::ReloadAll()
{
	LoadAll();
	UE_LOG(LogGB, Log, TEXT("Data: reloaded from %s"), *GetDataDir());
	OnDataReloaded.Broadcast();
}

FString UGBDataSubsystem::GetDataDir()
{
	return FPaths::ProjectContentDir() / TEXT("Data/");
}
		return false;
	}

	const FVector Origin = Player->GetAimOrigin();
	const FVector Forward = Player->GetAimDirection().GetSafeNormal();
	const float Range = GetStat(GBStat::MeleeRange, 200.f);
	const float HalfArcCos = FMath::Cos(FMath::DegreesToRadians(FMath::Clamp(GetStat(GBStat::MeleeArc, 90.f), 0.f, 360.f) * 0.5f));
	if (Range <= 0.f)
	{
		return true;
	}

	FCollisionObjectQueryParams ObjectParams;
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_Pawn);
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_WorldDynamic);
	FCollisionQueryParams OverlapParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSwordOverlap), false, Player);
	OverlapParams.AddIgnoredActor(this);

	TArray<FOverlapResult> Overlaps;
	World->OverlapMultiByObjectType(Overlaps, Origin, FQuat::Identity, ObjectParams, FCollisionShape::MakeSphere(Range), OverlapParams);

	const FGBDamageInfo BaseInfo = MakeDamageInfo();
	const float HeadshotMult = GetStat(GBStat::HeadshotMult, 1.f);
	TSet<AActor*> Done;
	for (const FOverlapResult& Overlap : Overlaps)
	{
		AActor* Target = Overlap.GetActor();
		if (!Target || Done.Contains(Target))
		{
			continue;
		}
		Done.Add(Target);

		if (!UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target) || !UGBCombatLibrary::IsAlive(Target) || !UGBCombatLibrary::AreHostile(Player, Target))
		{
			continue;
		}

		// Arc and range selection stays on the target's center, so nothing previously hit is missed.
		const FVector TargetLocation = Target->GetActorLocation();
		const FVector ToTarget = TargetLocation - Origin;
		const FVector ToTargetDir = ToTarget.GetSafeNormal();
		if (!ToTargetDir.IsNearlyZero() && FVector::DotProduct(Forward, ToTargetDir) < HalfArcCos)
		{
			continue;
		}

		FVector BodyBottom;
		FVector BodyTop;
		GetBodySegment(Target, BodyBottom, BodyTop);
		const FVector HitLocation = UGBCombatLibrary::ClosestBodyPointToAim(Origin, Forward, Range, BodyBottom, BodyTop);

		// Not through walls: trace to the aimed hit location, not the target's center.
		FCollisionQueryParams WallParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSwordWall), false, Player);
		WallParams.AddIgnoredActor(this);
		WallParams.AddIgnoredActor(Target);
		if (World->LineTraceTestByChannel(Origin, HitLocation, ECC_Visibility, WallParams))
		{
			continue;
		}

		FGBDamageInfo Info = BaseInfo;
		Info.HitLocation = HitLocation;
		Info.HitDirection = ToTargetDir;
		UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyWeakPointBonus(Target, Info, HeadshotMult);
		UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Target, Info);
	}
	return true;
}

void UPoseableMeshComponent::SetBoneTransformByName(FName BoneName, const FTransform& InTransform, EBoneSpaces::Type BoneSpace)
{
	if( !GetSkinnedAsset() || !RequiredBones.IsValid() )
	{
		return;
	}

	check(!LeaderPoseComponent.IsValid()); //Shouldn't call set bone functions when we are using LeaderPoseComponent

	int32 BoneIndex = GetBoneIndex(BoneName);
	if(BoneIndex >=0 && BoneIndex < BoneSpaceTransforms.Num())
	{
		BoneSpaceTransforms[BoneIndex] = InTransform;

		// If we haven't requested local space we need to transform the position passed in
		//if(BoneSpace != EBoneSpaces::LocalSpace)
		{
			if(BoneSpace == EBoneSpaces::WorldSpace)
			{
				BoneSpaceTransforms[BoneIndex].SetToRelativeTransform(GetComponentToWorld());
			}

			int32 ParentIndex = RequiredBones.GetParentBoneIndex(BoneIndex);
			if(ParentIndex >=0)
			{
				FA2CSPose CSPose;
				CSPose.AllocateLocalPoses(RequiredBones, BoneSpaceTransforms);

				BoneSpaceTransforms[BoneIndex].SetToRelativeTransform(CSPose.GetComponentSpaceTransform(ParentIndex));
			}

			MarkRefreshTransformDirty();
		}
	}
}

void UPoseableMeshComponent::SetBoneLocationByName(FName BoneName, FVector InLocation, EBoneSpaces::Type BoneSpace)
{
	FTransform CurrentTransform = GetBoneTransformByName(BoneName, BoneSpace);
	CurrentTransform.SetLocation(InLocation);
	SetBoneTransformByName(BoneName, CurrentTransform, BoneSpace);
}

void UPoseableMeshComponent::SetBoneRotationByName(FName BoneName, FRotator InRotation, EBoneSpaces::Type BoneSpace)
{
	FTransform CurrentTransform = GetBoneTransformByName(BoneName, BoneSpace);
	CurrentTransform.SetRotation(FQuat(InRotation));
	SetBoneTransformByName(BoneName, CurrentTransform, BoneSpace);
}

void UPoseableMeshComponent::SetBoneScaleByName(FName BoneName, FVector InScale3D, EBoneSpaces::Type BoneSpace)
{
	FTransform CurrentTransform = GetBoneTransformByName(BoneName, BoneSpace);
	CurrentTransform.SetScale3D(InScale3D);
	SetBoneTransformByName(BoneName, CurrentTransform, BoneSpace);
}

template<class CompType>
FTransform GetBoneTransformByNameHelper(FName BoneName, EBoneSpaces::Type BoneSpace, FBoneContainer& RequiredBones, CompType* Component)
{
	int32 BoneIndex = Component->GetBoneIndex(BoneName);
	if (BoneIndex == INDEX_NONE)
	{
		FString Message = FString::Printf(TEXT("Invalid Bone Name '%s'"), *BoneName.ToString());
		FFrame::KismetExecutionMessage(*Message, ELogVerbosity::Warning);
		return FTransform();
	}

	/*if(BoneSpace == EBoneSpaces::LocalSpace)
	{
		return Component->BoneSpaceTransforms[i];
	}*/

	FA2CSPose CSPose;
	CSPose.AllocateLocalPoses(RequiredBones, Component->GetBoneSpaceTransforms());

	if (BoneSpace == EBoneSpaces::ComponentSpace)
	{
		return CSPose.GetComponentSpaceTransform(BoneIndex);
	}
	else
	{
		return CSPose.GetComponentSpaceTransform(BoneIndex) * Component->GetComponentTransform();
	}
}

FTransform UPoseableMeshComponent::GetBoneTransformByName(FName BoneName, EBoneSpaces::Type BoneSpace)
{
	if( !GetSkinnedAsset() || !RequiredBones.IsValid() )
	{
		return FTransform();
	}

	USkinnedMeshComponent* MPCPtr = LeaderPoseComponent.Get();
	if (MPCPtr)
	{
		if (USkeletalMeshComponent* SMC = Cast<USkeletalMeshComponent>(MPCPtr))
		{
			if (UAnimInstance* AnimInstance = SMC->GetAnimInstance())
			{
				return GetBoneTransformByNameHelper(BoneName, BoneSpace, AnimInstance->GetRequiredBones(), SMC);
			}
			FString Message = FString::Printf(TEXT("Cannot return valid bone transform. Leader Pose Component has no anim instance"));
			FFrame::KismetExecutionMessage(*Message, ELogVerbosity::Warning);
			return FTransform();
		}
		FString Message = FString::Printf(TEXT("Cannot return valid bone transform. Leader Pose Component is not of type USkeletalMeshComponent"));
		FFrame::KismetExecutionMessage(*Message, ELogVerbosity::Warning);
		return FTransform();
	}
	return GetBoneTransformByNameHelper(BoneName, BoneSpace, RequiredBones, this);
}

namespace
{
	// Visual only: how many sprites draw the cone.
	constexpr int32 BeamSpriteCount = 4;
}

AGBSpell_Beam::AGBSpell_Beam()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryActorTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = false;
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::StartSpell()
{
	BeamRange = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Range, 600.f));
	ConeHalfAngleDeg = FMath::Clamp(ReadParam(GBSpellParam::ConeAngle, 20.f), 1.f, 89.f);
	// Duration <= 0 (or missing) = no time limit: Get, not ReadParam, so no warning is logged.
	BeamMaxDuration = FMath::Max(0.f, SpellCast.Get(GBStat::Duration, 0.f));
	BeamMinDuration = FMath::Max(0.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::MinDuration, 0.f));
	bHoldToSustain = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::HoldToSustain, 0.f) > 0.5f;
	BeamStartTime = GetWorld() ? GetWorld()->GetTimeSeconds() : 0.0;
	InterruptFraction = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::InterruptHitFraction, 0.f);
	const float Interval = SafeInterval(ReadParam(GBStat::TickInterval, 0.2f));
	DamagePerTick = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadParam(GBStat::DamagePerSecond, 30.f)) * Interval;
	ManaPerTick = FMath::Max(0.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::ManaPerSecond, 0.f)) * Interval;

	// Sprites along the cone, growing with its width.
	const FName SpriteId = GetSpriteId();
	UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* Library = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(this);
	if (!SpriteId.IsNone() && Library)
	{
		const float NearHeight = FMath::Max(1.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::SpriteSize, 40.f));
		const float TanHalfAngle = FMath::Tan(FMath::DegreesToRadians(ConeHalfAngleDeg));
		for (int32 Index = 0; Index < BeamSpriteCount; ++Index)
		{
			const float Along = BeamRange * (Index + 1) / (BeamSpriteCount + 1);
			UBillboardComponent* Sprite = NewObject<UBillboardComponent>(this);
			Sprite->SetupAttachment(Root);
			Sprite->RegisterComponent();
			AddInstanceComponent(Sprite);
			Sprite->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(Along, 0.f, 0.f));
			Library->SetupBillboard(Sprite, SpriteId, FMath::Max(NearHeight, 2.f * Along * TanHalfAngle));
			Sprites.Add(Sprite);
		}
	}

	FollowAim();
	SetActorTickEnabled(true);
	GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(TickTimer, this, &AGBSpell_Beam::DamageTick, Interval, true, 0.f);
	SubscribeCasterDamage();
	if (BeamMaxDuration > 0.f)
	{
		EndSpellAfter(BeamMaxDuration);
	}
}

bool AGBSpell_Beam::ShouldBeamContinue(bool bHoldToSustain, bool bKeyHeld, float Elapsed, float MinDuration, float MaxDuration)
{
	if (MaxDuration > 0.f && Elapsed >= MaxDuration)
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (Elapsed < MinDuration)
	{
		return true;
	}
	return !bHoldToSustain || bKeyHeld;
}

bool AGBSpell_Beam::IsInterruptingHit(float DamageTaken, float MaxHealth, float InterruptHitFraction)
{
	if (InterruptHitFraction <= 0.f || MaxHealth <= 0.f)
	{
		return false;
	}
	// Small tolerance so "30% or more" holds in float math (0.3f * 100 is 30.000001, which would miss a hit of 30).
	const float Threshold = InterruptHitFraction * MaxHealth;
	return DamageTaken >= Threshold - KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER * FMath::Max(1.f, Threshold);
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::SubscribeCasterDamage()
{
	if (InterruptFraction <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(GetCaster());
	if (!Attributes)
	{
		return;
	}
	UnsubscribeCasterDamage();
	BoundCasterAttributes = Attributes;
	CasterDamagedHandle = Attributes->OnDamaged.AddUObject(this, &AGBSpell_Beam::HandleCasterDamaged);
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::UnsubscribeCasterDamage()
{
	if (UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = BoundCasterAttributes.Get())
	{
		Attributes->OnDamaged.Remove(CasterDamagedHandle);
	}
	CasterDamagedHandle.Reset();
	BoundCasterAttributes.Reset();
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::HandleCasterDamaged(const FGBDamageInfo& Info, float ActualDamage)
{
	if (bEnded || InterruptFraction <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(GetCaster());
	const float MaxHealth = Attributes ? Attributes->GetMaxHealth() : 0.f;
	if (IsInterruptingHit(ActualDamage, MaxHealth, InterruptFraction))
	{
		HandleInterrupt();
	}
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::HandleInterrupt()
{
	if (bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (AGBHUD* Hud = AGBHUD::Get(this))
	{
		Hud->ShowMessage(TEXT("Interrupted!"), 1.5f);
	}
	if (AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetCaster()))
	{
		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
		const float Duration = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBInterruptShake::Duration, 0.35f) : 0.35f;
		const float Amplitude = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBInterruptShake::Amplitude, 2.5f) : 2.5f;
		const float Frequency = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBInterruptShake::Frequency, 22.f) : 22.f;
		Player->StartViewShake(Duration, Amplitude, Frequency);
	}
	EndSpell();
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::EndSpell()
{
	UnsubscribeCasterDamage();
	Super::EndSpell();
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	UnsubscribeCasterDamage();
	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::CheckSustain()
{
	if (bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}
	const double Now = GetWorld() ? GetWorld()->GetTimeSeconds() : BeamStartTime;
	const float Elapsed = static_cast<float>(Now - BeamStartTime);
	bool bKeyHeld = true;
	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetCaster()))
	{
		bKeyHeld = Player->IsSpellInputHeld(SpellCast.SpellId);
	}
	if (!ShouldBeamContinue(bHoldToSustain, bKeyHeld, Elapsed, BeamMinDuration, BeamMaxDuration))
	{

void AGBSword::InitFromDefinition(const FGBWeaponDefinition& Definition, AGBPlayerCharacter* InOwner)
{
	Super::InitFromDefinition(Definition, InOwner);

	const FName Required[] = { GBStat::Damage, GBStat::FireInterval, GBStat::MeleeRange, GBStat::MeleeArc };
	for (const FName& Stat : Required)
	{
		if (!Definition.Stats.Contains(Stat))
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Weapons.json '%s': missing stat '%s', using a default"), *Definition.Id.ToString(), *Stat.ToString());
		}
	}

	// Placeholder 3D hilt (art rule: the hilt comes from the future). Sizes in cm are purely visual; the blade
	// rises along +Z from the guard and is drawn by the HUD as pixel art.
	Grip = MakeSwordHiltPart(this, Root, TEXT("Grip"), EGBShape::Cylinder, FVector(0.f, 0.f, -5.f), FVector(3.f, 3.f, 10.f), FLinearColor(0.10f, 0.10f, 0.12f), false);
	Guard = MakeSwordHiltPart(this, Root, TEXT("Guard"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(0.f, 0.f, 1.f), FVector(4.f, 14.f, 2.f), FLinearColor(0.35f, 0.35f, 0.40f), false);
	ShownElement = Element;
	Gem = MakeSwordHiltPart(this, Root, TEXT("Gem"), EGBShape::Sphere, FVector(2.5f, 0.f, 1.f), FVector(2.5f, 2.5f, 2.5f),
		GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(ShownElement)), true);
}

void AGBSword::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	if (Gem && ShownElement != Element)
	{
		ShownElement = Element;
		GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(Gem, GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(ShownElement)));
	}
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
}

AActor* AGBEnemyCharacter::FindTarget() const
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(this);
	if (Player && UGBCombatLibrary::IsAlive(Player) && UGBCombatLibrary::AreHostile(this, Player))
	{
		return Player;
	}
	return nullptr;
}

bool AGBEnemyCharacter::IsRanged() const
{
	return AttackModeName.Equals(GBEnemyCharacterLocal::AttackModeRanged.ToString(), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase);
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::SetState(EGBEnemyState NewState)
{
	if (State == EGBEnemyState::Windup && NewState != EGBEnemyState::Windup)
	{
		SetTelegraph(false);
	}
	State = NewState;
	StateTime = 0.f;
	if (NewState == EGBEnemyState::Windup)
	{
		SetTelegraph(true);
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::SetTelegraph(bool bOn)
{
	if (bPixelArtLook)
	{
		Sprite->SetRelativeScale3D(bOn ? SpriteBaseScale * GBEnemyCharacterLocal::TelegraphSpriteScale : SpriteBaseScale);
	}
	else
	{
		const FLinearColor Color = bOn ? BodyColor + (FLinearColor::White - BodyColor) * GBEnemyCharacterLocal::TelegraphTintBlend : BodyColor;
		GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(BodyMesh, Color);
	}
}

#include "Components/PoseableMeshComponent.h"
#include "Components/SkeletalMeshComponent.h"
#include "Animation/AnimStats.h"

#include UE_INLINE_GENERATED_CPP_BY_NAME(PoseableMeshComponent)

UPoseableMeshComponent::UPoseableMeshComponent(const FObjectInitializer& ObjectInitializer)
	: Super(ObjectInitializer)
	, bNeedsRefreshTransform (false)
{

}

bool UPoseableMeshComponent::AllocateTransformData()
{
	// Allocate transforms if not present.
	if ( Super::AllocateTransformData() )
	{
		if(BoneSpaceTransforms.Num() != GetSkinnedAsset()->GetRefSkeleton().GetNum() )
		{
			BoneSpaceTransforms = GetSkinnedAsset()->GetRefSkeleton().GetRefBonePose();

			TArray<FBoneIndexType> RequiredBoneIndexArray;
			RequiredBoneIndexArray.AddUninitialized(BoneSpaceTransforms.Num());
			for(int32 BoneIndex = 0; BoneIndex < BoneSpaceTransforms.Num(); ++BoneIndex)
			{
				RequiredBoneIndexArray[BoneIndex] = BoneIndex;
			}

			RequiredBones.InitializeTo(RequiredBoneIndexArray, UE::Anim::FCurveFilterSettings(UE::Anim::ECurveFilterMode::DisallowAll), *GetSkinnedAsset());
		}

		FillComponentSpaceTransforms();
		FinalizeBoneTransform();

		return true;
	}

	BoneSpaceTransforms.Empty();

	return false;
}

void UPoseableMeshComponent::RefreshBoneTransforms(FActorComponentTickFunction* TickFunction)
{
	SCOPE_CYCLE_COUNTER(STAT_RefreshBoneTransforms);

	// Can't do anything without a SkinnedAsset
	if( !GetSkinnedAsset())
	{
		return;
	}

	// Do nothing more if no bones in skeleton.
	if( GetNumComponentSpaceTransforms() == 0 )
	{
		return;
	}

	// We need the mesh space bone transforms now for renderer to get delta from ref pose:
	FillComponentSpaceTransforms();
	FinalizeBoneTransform();

	UpdateChildTransforms();
	UpdateBounds();
	MarkRenderTransformDirty();
	MarkRenderDynamicDataDirty();

	bNeedsRefreshTransform = false;
}

void UPoseableMeshComponent::FillComponentSpaceTransforms()
{
	ANIM_MT_SCOPE_CYCLE_COUNTER(FillComponentSpaceTransforms, IsRunningParallelEvaluation());

	if( !GetSkinnedAsset())
	{
		return;
	}

	// If skeleton has changed then attempt to reallocate internal transform data
	if (BoneSpaceTransforms.Num() != GetSkinnedAsset()->GetRefSkeleton().GetNum())
	{
		verify(AllocateTransformData());
	}

	// right now all this does is to convert to SpaceBases
	check( GetSkinnedAsset()->GetRefSkeleton().GetNum() == BoneSpaceTransforms.Num() );
	check( GetSkinnedAsset()->GetRefSkeleton().GetNum() == GetNumComponentSpaceTransforms());
	check( GetSkinnedAsset()->GetRefSkeleton().GetNum() == GetBoneVisibilityStates().Num() );

	const int32 NumBones = BoneSpaceTransforms.Num();

#if DO_GUARD_SLOW
	/** Keep track of which bones have been processed for fast look up */
	TArray<uint8, TInlineAllocator<256>> BoneProcessed;
	BoneProcessed.AddZeroed(NumBones);
#endif
	// Build in 3 passes.
	FTransform* LocalTransformsData = BoneSpaceTransforms.GetData();
	FTransform* SpaceBasesData = GetEditableComponentSpaceTransforms().GetData();
	
	GetEditableComponentSpaceTransforms()[0] = BoneSpaceTransforms[0];
#if DO_GUARD_SLOW
	BoneProcessed[0] = 1;
#endif

	for(int32 BoneIndex=1; BoneIndex<BoneSpaceTransforms.Num(); BoneIndex++)
	{
		FPlatformMisc::Prefetch(SpaceBasesData + BoneIndex);

#if DO_GUARD_SLOW
		// Mark bone as processed
		BoneProcessed[BoneIndex] = 1;
#endif
		// For all bones below the root, final component-space transform is relative transform * component-space transform of parent.
		const int32 ParentIndex = GetSkinnedAsset()->GetRefSkeleton().GetParentIndex(BoneIndex);
		FPlatformMisc::Prefetch(SpaceBasesData + ParentIndex);

#if DO_GUARD_SLOW
		// Check the precondition that Parents occur before Children in the RequiredBones array.
		checkSlow(BoneProcessed[ParentIndex] == 1);
#endif
		FTransform::Multiply(SpaceBasesData + BoneIndex, LocalTransformsData + BoneIndex, SpaceBasesData + ParentIndex);

		checkSlow(GetEditableComponentSpaceTransforms()[BoneIndex].IsRotationNormalized());
		checkSlow(!GetEditableComponentSpaceTransforms()[BoneIndex].ContainsNaN());
	}
	bNeedToFlipSpaceBaseBuffers = true;
}

void UPoseableMeshComponent::SetBoneTransformByName(FName BoneName, const FTransform& InTransform, EBoneSpaces::Type BoneSpace)

namespace GBPlaceholderPrivate
{
	const FName ColorParam(TEXT("Color"));

	// Parameters of M_PixelSurface (Tools/Jam/make_materials.py builds it; keep the names in sync).
	const FName TextureParam(TEXT("Texture"));
	const FName TintParam(TEXT("Tint"));
	const FName TileSizeParam(TEXT("TileSize"));
	const FName GlowParam(TEXT("Glow"));
	const TCHAR* const PixelSurfacePath = TEXT("/Game/Glyphbound/Materials/M_PixelSurface.M_PixelSurface");
	const TCHAR* const PixelDecalPath = TEXT("/Game/Glyphbound/Materials/M_PixelDecal.M_PixelDecal");

	/** Smallest tile accepted from data, so a 0 in the JSON cannot divide by zero in the shader. */
	constexpr float MinTileSizeCm = 1.f;

	/** M_PixelSurface, or null when the asset was not generated (then the caller falls back to SetupShape). */
	UMaterialInterface* GetPixelSurfaceMaterial()
	{
		static TWeakObjectPtr<UMaterialInterface> Cached;
		static bool bKnownMissing = false;
		if (!Cached.IsValid() && !bKnownMissing)
		{
			Cached = LoadObject<UMaterialInterface>(nullptr, PixelSurfacePath, FStringView(), LOAD_NoWarn | LOAD_Quiet);
			if (!Cached.IsValid())
			{
				// Logged once: run Tools/Jam/Make-Materials.ps1 to create it.
				bKnownMissing = true;
				UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Placeholder: material %s is missing (run Tools/Jam/Make-Materials.ps1); textured shapes use plain tinted shapes."), PixelSurfacePath);
			}
		}
		return Cached.Get();
	}

	/** M_PixelDecal, or null when the asset was not generated (then the caller falls back to SetupShape). */
	UMaterialInterface* GetPixelDecalMaterial()
	{
		static TWeakObjectPtr<UMaterialInterface> Cached;
		static bool bKnownMissing = false;
		if (!Cached.IsValid() && !bKnownMissing)
		{
			Cached = LoadObject<UMaterialInterface>(nullptr, PixelDecalPath, FStringView(), LOAD_NoWarn | LOAD_Quiet);
			if (!Cached.IsValid())
			{
				// Logged once: run Tools/Jam/Make-Materials.ps1 to create it.
				bKnownMissing = true;
				UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Placeholder: material %s is missing (run Tools/Jam/Make-Materials.ps1); ground spells use plain tinted shapes."), PixelDecalPath);
			}
		}
		return Cached.Get();
	}

	// The engine shapes are 100 cm across.
	constexpr float ShapeSizeCm = 100.f;

	UMaterialInterface* GetMaterial(bool bGlow)
	{
		static TWeakObjectPtr<UMaterialInterface> LitMaterial;
		static TWeakObjectPtr<UMaterialInterface> GlowMaterial;

		TWeakObjectPtr<UMaterialInterface>& Cached = bGlow ? GlowMaterial : LitMaterial;
		if (!Cached.IsValid())
		{
			const TCHAR* Path = bGlow
				? TEXT("/Engine/EngineMaterials/EmissiveMeshMaterial.EmissiveMeshMaterial")
				: TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/BasicShapeMaterial.BasicShapeMaterial");
			Cached = LoadObject<UMaterialInterface>(nullptr, Path);
			if (!Cached.IsValid())
			{
				UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Placeholder: cannot load material %s"), Path);
			}
		}
		return Cached.Get();
	}
}

UStaticMesh* GBPlaceholder::GetShapeMesh(EGBShape Shape)
{
	static TWeakObjectPtr<UStaticMesh> Cache[5];

	const int32 Index = FMath::Clamp(static_cast<int32>(Shape), 0, 4);
	if (!Cache[Index].IsValid())
	{
		static const TCHAR* const Paths[5] =
		{
			TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Cube.Cube"),
			TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Sphere.Sphere"),
			TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Cylinder.Cylinder"),
			TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Cone.Cone"),
			TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Plane.Plane"),
		};
		Cache[Index] = LoadObject<UStaticMesh>(nullptr, Paths[Index]);
		if (!Cache[Index].IsValid())
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Placeholder: cannot load mesh %s"), Paths[Index]);
		}
	}
	return Cache[Index].Get();
}

UMaterialInstanceDynamic* GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(UStaticMeshComponent* Component, EGBShape Shape, const FVector& SizeCm, const FLinearColor& Color, bool bGlow)
{
	if (!Component)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	Component->SetStaticMesh(GetShapeMesh(Shape));
	Component->SetRelativeScale3D(SizeCm / GBPlaceholderPrivate::ShapeSizeCm);

	UMaterialInterface* BaseMaterial = GBPlaceholderPrivate::GetMaterial(bGlow);
	if (!BaseMaterial)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	UMaterialInstanceDynamic* Material = UMaterialInstanceDynamic::Create(BaseMaterial, Component);
	Material->SetVectorParameterValue(GBPlaceholderPrivate::ColorParam, Color);
	Component->SetMaterial(0, Material);
	return Material;
}

UMaterialInstanceDynamic* GBPlaceholder::SetupTexturedShape(UStaticMeshComponent* Component, EGBShape Shape, const FVector& SizeCm, FName TextureId,
	float TileSizeCm, const FLinearColor& Tint, bool bGlow)
{
	if (!Component)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	UMaterialInterface* BaseMaterial = GBPlaceholderPrivate::GetPixelSurfaceMaterial();
	if (!BaseMaterial)
	{
		return SetupShape(Component, Shape, SizeCm, Tint);
	}

	Component->SetStaticMesh(GetShapeMesh(Shape));
	Component->SetRelativeScale3D(SizeCm / GBPlaceholderPrivate::ShapeSizeCm);

	UMaterialInstanceDynamic* Material = UMaterialInstanceDynamic::Create(BaseMaterial, Component);
	if (UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* Library = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(Component))
	{
		Material->SetTextureParameterValue(GBPlaceholderPrivate::TextureParam, Library->GetTileTexture(TextureId));
	}
	else
	{
		// Not in a game world yet (the library lives on the game instance): the material's default texture shows.
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Placeholder: no pixel sprite library for '%s' on %s; using the default texture."),
			*TextureId.ToString(), *GetNameSafe(Component->GetOwner()));
	}
	Material->SetVectorParameterValue(GBPlaceholderPrivate::TintParam, Tint);
	Material->SetScalarParameterValue(GBPlaceholderPrivate::TileSizeParam, FMath::Max(TileSizeCm, GBPlaceholderPrivate::MinTileSizeCm));
	Material->SetScalarParameterValue(GBPlaceholderPrivate::GlowParam, bGlow ? 1.f : 0.f);
	Component->SetMaterial(0, Material);
	return Material;
}

void GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(UStaticMeshComponent* Component, const FLinearColor& Color)
{
	if (!Component)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (UMaterialInstanceDynamic* Material = Cast<UMaterialInstanceDynamic>(Component->GetMaterial(0)))
	{
		// "Color" on SetupShape materials, "Tint" on SetupTexturedShape ones; the other name is ignored.
		Material->SetVectorParameterValue(GBPlaceholderPrivate::ColorParam, Color);
		Material->SetVectorParameterValue(GBPlaceholderPrivate::TintParam, Color);
	}
}

UMaterialInstanceDynamic* GBPlaceholder::SetupFlatSprite(UStaticMeshComponent* Component, FName SpriteId, float SizeCm, const FLinearColor& Tint, float Glow)
{
	if (!Component)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	const float SafeSize = FMath::Max(1.f, SizeCm);
	UMaterialInterface* BaseMaterial = GBPlaceholderPrivate::GetPixelDecalMaterial();
	if (!BaseMaterial)
	{
		return SetupShape(Component, EGBShape::Plane, FVector(SafeSize, SafeSize, 100.f), Tint, true);
	}

	Component->SetStaticMesh(GetShapeMesh(EGBShape::Plane));
	const float Scale = SafeSize / GBPlaceholderPrivate::ShapeSizeCm;
	Component->SetRelativeScale3D(FVector(Scale, Scale, 1.f));

	UMaterialInstanceDynamic* Material = UMaterialInstanceDynamic::Create(BaseMaterial, Component);
	if (UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* Library = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(Component))
	TArray<FQuat> RefLocal;
	/** Reference component-space rotations, one per bone. */
	TArray<FQuat> RefComp;
	/** Reference bone direction in the bone's own frame (dominant axis), for wave bends. */
	TArray<FVector> LocalDirs;
	/** Which two local axes a bone bends about (both perpendicular to its dominant axis). */
	TArray<int32> BendAxisA;
	TArray<int32> BendAxisB;
	/** Current joint positions in component space, updated every tick (for FABRIK and tip queries). */
	TArray<FVector> FKJoints;
	/** Random wave phase of this chain, in cycles. */
	float Phase = 0.f;
};

void FA2CSPose::AllocateLocalPoses(const FBoneContainer& InBoneContainer, const FTransformArrayA2& LocalBones)
{
	check( InBoneContainer.IsValid() );
	BoneContainer = &InBoneContainer;

	Bones = LocalBones;
	ComponentSpaceFlags.Init(0, Bones.Num());

	// root is same, so set root first
	check(ComponentSpaceFlags.Num() > 0);
	ComponentSpaceFlags[0] = 1;
}

bool FA2CSPose::IsValid() const
{
	return (BoneContainer && BoneContainer->IsValid());
}

int32 FA2CSPose::GetParentBoneIndex(const int32 BoneIndex) const
{
	checkSlow( IsValid() );
	return BoneContainer->GetParentBoneIndex(BoneIndex);
}

// Named (not anonymous) so unity builds cannot collide with another file's helpers.
namespace GBEnemyCharacterLocal
{
	const FName AttackModeMelee(TEXT("Melee"));
	const FName AttackModeRanged(TEXT("Ranged"));
	const TCHAR* const DefaultEnemyProjectileSprite = TEXT("enemy_bolt");

	// Pure layout / technical constants (not gameplay tuning).
	constexpr float GroundProbeDepth = 1000.f;     // cm below the requested spawn point searched for a floor
	constexpr float SpawnLift = 2.f;               // cm above the floor, so the capsule does not start inside it
	constexpr int32 MaxSteerCandidatesPerSide = 12;
	constexpr float TelegraphSpriteScale = 1.12f;
	constexpr float TelegraphTintBlend = 0.55f;    // towards white
}

AGBEnemyCharacter::AGBEnemyCharacter()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;

	Stats = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBStatsComponent>(TEXT("Stats"));
	Attributes = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBAttributeComponent>(TEXT("Attributes"));
	Attributes->Team = EGBTeam::Enemy;
	StatusEffects = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBStatusEffectComponent>(TEXT("StatusEffects"));
	ElementalStatus = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBElementalStatusComponent>(TEXT("ElementalStatus"));
	EraComponent = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBEraComponent>(TEXT("Era"));

	Sprite = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBillboardComponent>(TEXT("Sprite"));
	Sprite->SetupAttachment(GetCapsuleComponent());
	Sprite->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Sprite->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);

	BodyMesh = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("BodyMesh"));
	BodyMesh->SetupAttachment(GetCapsuleComponent());
	BodyMesh->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	BodyMesh->SetCollisionProfileName(UCollisionProfile::NoCollision_ProfileName);
	BodyMesh->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
	BodyMesh->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);

	// Pawn profile ignores Visibility; line-of-sight traces use it, so block it. Guns trace ECC_Camera: keep blocking it too.
	UCapsuleComponent* Capsule = GetCapsuleComponent();
	Capsule->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Pawn, ECR_Block);
	Capsule->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Visibility, ECR_Block);
	Capsule->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Camera, ECR_Block);

	AIControllerClass = AAIController::StaticClass();
	AutoPossessAI = EAutoPossessAI::PlacedInWorldOrSpawned;

	bUseControllerRotationYaw = false;
	bUseControllerRotationPitch = false;
	bUseControllerRotationRoll = false;
	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		Move->bOrientRotationToMovement = true;
	}
}

AGBEnemyCharacter* AGBEnemyCharacter::SpawnEnemy(UWorld* World, FName InEnemyId, const FVector& Location, const FRotator& Rotation, EGBEraPresence Presence)
{
	if (!World)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(World);
	const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindEnemy(InEnemyId) : nullptr;
	if (!Def)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("SpawnEnemy: unknown enemy id '%s'"), *InEnemyId.ToString());
		return nullptr;
	}

	UClass* Class = AGBEnemyCharacter::StaticClass();
	if (!Def->ActorClass.IsEmpty())
	{
		if (UClass* Loaded = LoadClass<AGBEnemyCharacter>(nullptr, *Def->ActorClass))
		{
			Class = Loaded;
		}
		else
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("SpawnEnemy: class '%s' of '%s' not found, using AGBEnemyCharacter"), *Def->ActorClass, *InEnemyId.ToString());
		}
	}

	const AGBEnemyCharacter* CDO = Class->GetDefaultObject<AGBEnemyCharacter>();
	const float DefaultHalfHeight = (CDO && CDO->GetCapsuleComponent()) ? CDO->GetCapsuleComponent()->GetUnscaledCapsuleHalfHeight() : 0.f;
	const float* DefHalfHeight = Def->Stats.Find(GBStat::CapsuleHalfHeight);
	const float HalfHeight = DefHalfHeight ? FMath::Max(*DefHalfHeight, 1.f) : DefaultHalfHeight;

	// Stand on the floor under the requested point, if there is one.
	FVector Ground = Location;
	FHitResult Hit;
	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBEnemySpawnGround), false);
	if (World->LineTraceSingleByObjectType(Hit, Location + FVector::UpVector * HalfHeight, Location - FVector::UpVector * GBEnemyCharacterLocal::GroundProbeDepth,
		FCollisionObjectQueryParams(ECC_WorldStatic), Params))
	{
		Ground = Hit.ImpactPoint;
	}

	const FTransform SpawnTransform(FRotator(0.f, Rotation.Yaw, 0.f), Ground + FVector::UpVector * (HalfHeight + GBEnemyCharacterLocal::SpawnLift));
	AGBEnemyCharacter* Enemy = World->SpawnActorDeferred<AGBEnemyCharacter>(Class, SpawnTransform, nullptr, nullptr,
		ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AdjustIfPossibleButAlwaysSpawn);
	if (!Enemy)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("SpawnEnemy: spawn of '%s' failed"), *InEnemyId.ToString());
		return nullptr;
	}

	Enemy->EnemyId = InEnemyId;
	Enemy->InitFromDefinition(*Def);
	Enemy->EraComponent->Presence = Presence;
	Enemy->FinishSpawning(SpawnTransform);
	return Enemy;
}

void UPoseableMeshComponent::ResetBoneTransformByName(FName BoneName)
{
	if( !GetSkinnedAsset())
	{
		return;
	}

	const int32 BoneIndex = GetBoneIndex(BoneName);
	if( BoneIndex != INDEX_NONE )
	{
		BoneSpaceTransforms[BoneIndex] = GetSkinnedAsset()->GetRefSkeleton().GetRefBonePose()[BoneIndex];
	}
	else
	{
		FString Message = FString::Printf(TEXT("Invalid Bone Name '%s'"), *BoneName.ToString());
		FFrame::KismetExecutionMessage(*Message, ELogVerbosity::Warning);
	}
}

void UPoseableMeshComponent::CopyPoseFromSkeletalComponent(USkeletalMeshComponent* InComponentToCopy)
{
	if(RequiredBones.IsValid())
	{
		TArray<FTransform> LocalTransforms = InComponentToCopy->GetBoneSpaceTransforms();
		if (LocalTransforms.IsEmpty()) // during incremental unregistering this might be empty
		{
			return;
		}

		if(this->GetSkinnedAsset() == InComponentToCopy->GetSkinnedAsset()
			&& LocalTransforms.Num() == BoneSpaceTransforms.Num())
		{
			
			Exchange(BoneSpaceTransforms, LocalTransforms);
		}
		else
		{
			// The meshes don't match, search bone-by-bone (slow path)

			// first set the localatoms to ref pose from our current mesh
			BoneSpaceTransforms = GetSkinnedAsset()->GetRefSkeleton().GetRefBonePose();

			// Now overwrite any matching bones
			const int32 NumSourceBones = InComponentToCopy->GetSkinnedAsset()->GetRefSkeleton().GetNum();

			for(int32 SourceBoneIndex = 0 ; SourceBoneIndex < NumSourceBones ; ++SourceBoneIndex)
			{
				const FName SourceBoneName = InComponentToCopy->GetBoneName(SourceBoneIndex);
				const int32 TargetBoneIndex = GetBoneIndex(SourceBoneName);

				if(TargetBoneIndex != INDEX_NONE)
				{
					BoneSpaceTransforms[TargetBoneIndex] = LocalTransforms[SourceBoneIndex];
				}
			}
		}

		MarkRefreshTransformDirty();
	}
}

bool UPoseableMeshComponent::ShouldUpdateTransform(bool bLODHasChanged) const
{
	// we don't always update transform - each function when they changed will update
	return Super::ShouldUpdateTransform(bLODHasChanged) || bNeedsRefreshTransform;
}

void UPoseableMeshComponent::MarkRefreshTransformDirty()
{
	bNeedsRefreshTransform = true;
}

void AGBSpellActor::QueryHostiles(const FVector& Center, const FQuat& Rotation, const FCollisionShape& Shape, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return;
	}
	FCollisionObjectQueryParams ObjectParams;
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_Pawn);
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_WorldDynamic);
	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellArea), false, this);

	TArray<FOverlapResult> Overlaps;
	World->OverlapMultiByObjectType(Overlaps, Center, Rotation, ObjectParams, Shape, Params);
	for (const FOverlapResult& Overlap : Overlaps)
	{
		AActor* Actor = Overlap.GetActor();
		if (IsHostileTarget(Actor))
		{
			OutActors.AddUnique(Actor);
		}
	}
}

void AGBSpellActor::GatherHostilesInSphere(const FVector& Center, float Radius, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	if (Radius <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	QueryHostiles(Center, FQuat::Identity, FCollisionShape::MakeSphere(Radius), OutActors);
}

void AGBSpellActor::GatherHostilesOnDisc(const FVector& GroundCenter, float Radius, float Height, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	if (Radius <= 0.f || Height <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const FVector BoxCenter = GroundCenter + FVector(0.f, 0.f, Height * 0.5f);
	QueryHostiles(BoxCenter, FQuat::Identity, FCollisionShape::MakeBox(FVector(Radius, Radius, Height * 0.5f)), OutActors);
	// The box is only the broad phase: keep actors whose collision touches the disc horizontally.
	OutActors.RemoveAll([&GroundCenter, Radius](const AActor* Actor)
	{
		return FVector::Dist2D(Actor->GetActorLocation(), GroundCenter) > Radius + Actor->GetSimpleCollisionRadius();
	});
}

void AGBSpellActor::GatherHostilesInBox(const FVector& Center, const FQuat& Rotation, const FVector& Extent, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	if (Extent.GetMin() <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	QueryHostiles(Center, Rotation, FCollisionShape::MakeBox(Extent), OutActors);
}

UGBStatsComponent::UGBStatsComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = false;
}

UGBStatsComponent* UGBStatsComponent::Find(const AActor* Actor)
{
	return Actor ? Actor->FindComponentByClass<UGBStatsComponent>() : nullptr;
}

void UGBStatsComponent::SetBaseStats(const TMap<FName, float>& InBaseStats)
{
	BaseStats = InBaseStats;
	OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBStatsComponent::SetBaseStat(FName Stat, float Value)
{
	BaseStats.Add(Stat, Value);
	OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
}

bool UGBStatsComponent::HasBaseStat(FName Stat) const
{
	return BaseStats.Contains(Stat);
}

float UGBStatsComponent::GetStat(FName Stat, float DefaultValue) const
{
	const float* Base = BaseStats.Find(Stat);
	return Evaluate(Stat, Base ? *Base : DefaultValue);
}

float UGBStatsComponent::Evaluate(FName Key, float Base) const
{
	return EvaluateKeys(MakeArrayView(&Key, 1), Base);
}

float UGBStatsComponent::EvaluateKeys(TConstArrayView<FName> Keys, float Base) const
{
	TArray<FGBModifierDef, TInlineAllocator<8>> Matching;
	for (const FSourcedModifier& Entry : ActiveModifiers)
	{
		if (Keys.Contains(Entry.Modifier.Stat))
		{
			Matching.Add(Entry.Modifier);
		}
	}
	return ApplyModifiers(Base, Matching);
}

void UGBStatsComponent::AddModifiers(FName Source, TConstArrayView<FGBModifierDef> Modifiers, float ValueScale)
{
	ActiveModifiers.RemoveAll([Source](const FSourcedModifier& Entry) { return Entry.Source == Source; });

	for (const FGBModifierDef& Modifier : Modifiers)
	{
		FSourcedModifier& Entry = ActiveModifiers.AddDefaulted_GetRef();
		Entry.Source = Source;
		Entry.Modifier = Modifier;
		switch (Modifier.Op)
		{
		case EGBModOp::Add:
			Entry.Modifier.Value = Modifier.Value * ValueScale;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Multiply:
			// Scaled around 1, so a x1.5 buff at half strength is x1.25, not x0.75.
			Entry.Modifier.Value = 1.f + (Modifier.Value - 1.f) * ValueScale;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::MultiplyMin:
			// Same scaling as Multiply: the strongest slow at partial strength stays a slow.
			Entry.Modifier.Value = 1.f + (Modifier.Value - 1.f) * ValueScale;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Override:
			break;
		}
	}

	OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBStatsComponent::RemoveModifiers(FName Source)
{
	if (ActiveModifiers.RemoveAll([Source](const FSourcedModifier& Entry) { return Entry.Source == Source; }) > 0)
	{
		OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
	}
}

bool UGBStatsComponent::HasModifierSource(FName Source) const
{
	return ActiveModifiers.ContainsByPredicate([Source](const FSourcedModifier& Entry) { return Entry.Source == Source; });
}

float UGBStatsComponent::ApplyModifiers(float Base, TConstArrayView<FGBModifierDef> Modifiers)
{
	float Sum = Base;
	float Product = 1.f;
	float LowestMin = 1.f;
	bool bHasMin = false;
	const FGBModifierDef* LastOverride = nullptr;

	for (const FGBModifierDef& Modifier : Modifiers)
	{
		switch (Modifier.Op)
		{
		case EGBModOp::Add:
			Sum += Modifier.Value;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Multiply:
			Product *= Modifier.Value;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::MultiplyMin:
			if (!bHasMin || Modifier.Value < LowestMin)
			{
				LowestMin = Modifier.Value;
				bHasMin = true;
			}
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Override:
			LastOverride = &Modifier;
			break;
		}
	}

	return LastOverride ? LastOverride->Value : Sum * Product * (bHasMin ? LowestMin : 1.f);
}

codex
The review is nearly complete. I reproduced a FABRIK collapse when the target coincides with an existing joint. I also confirmed that each bone rotation setter copies the full pose twice. The dormant projectile light is registered, but it does not tick or add a visible scene light when unused.
codex
1. **[P1] `AI/GBEntity.cpp:100` — Explosive hits can deal zero damage at the shell.** `DealRadialDamage` measures distance to `GetActorLocation()`, still at the ground capsule. A fireball exploding against the elevated shell can exclude the Entity from its own blast. Fix damage distance, falloff, and obstruction checks to use the damageable body’s location.

2. **[P1] `AI/GBEntity.cpp:139` — The invisible capsule remains damageable by melee and area spells.** Ignoring Camera and WorldDynamic does not exclude it from Pawn object queries. Sword swings and ground spells beneath the floating boss still damage it; Visibility also still blocks against empty space. Separate movement collision from damage queries and update melee/spell targeting to use the shell.

3. **[P2] `AI/GBTentacleMath.cpp:55` — Coincident joints collapse FABRIK segments.** For joints at X = `0,40,80,120,160`, targeting `(120,0,0)` produces `0,40,80,120,120`: the last segment becomes zero length. Preserve a fallback direction for coincident points and handle collinear solver singularities without losing segment lengths.

4. **[P2] `AI/GBEntity.cpp:839` — Reach survives cancelled attacks and era changes.** Reach clears on firing or death, but losing LOS, interruption, or switching eras can leave `ReachDesired=1`; the tentacle keeps aiming at an obsolete world position after returning to Chase. Clear reach whenever the Entity leaves an active windup.

5. **[P2] `AI/GBEntity.cpp:732` — Flight bypasses world collision.** The visual moves without a sweep, and `ShellHit` ignores WorldStatic. The ground capsule can walk beneath an overhead beam while the shell passes through it, or altitude drift can lift the boss into a ceiling. Sweep the shell’s intended movement and constrain altitude against surrounding geometry.

6. **[P2] `AI/GBEntity.cpp:780` — Motion slows below 20 FPS.** Clamping elapsed time to 0.05 discards simulation time: at 10 FPS, turning, flight, knockback, and light fades run at half speed while attack timing uses full elapsed time. Substep the full frame duration instead of truncating it.

7. **[P2] `AI/GBTentacleRigComponent.cpp:381` — Root limits exclude lag and FABRIK.** Only wave/ripple bends are clamped; the subsequent lag and solved rotations can exceed the limit arbitrarily. Reaching towards a target above or across the shell can pull the first two bones through it. Apply anatomical limits to the final composed pose and constrain the IK solution accordingly.

8. **[P2] `AI/GBEntity.cpp:244` — Missing-art fallback has invisible, displaced hit collision.** `bVisualBuilt` becomes true before asset validation, so failure still runs flight and leaves the shell sphere elevated while the visible placeholder stays at the capsule. Mark construction successful only after validation, and retain matching placeholder collision on failure.

9. **[P2] `AI/GBTentacleRigComponent.cpp:150` — Tip queries use an invented endpoint.** Setup extrapolates another segment beyond `_07`, and `GetChainTipPosition` returns that point. Shots, muzzle flashes, and smoke measurements therefore use a different location from the specified `_07` tip. Use the actual terminal bone, or explicitly validate an authored mesh endpoint.

10. **[P2] `Core/GBDevCommands.cpp:1813` — Smoke sampling depends on wall time, not simulation progress.** The scheduler at line 2064 executes every overdue step in one callback. A hitch can record and compare tips without an intervening tick, or evaluate reach before it has simulated 0.6 seconds. Advance these checks through simulation-time stages with guaranteed intervening ticks.

11. **[P2] `Core/GBDevCommands.cpp:1972` — The turn smoke can pass without turning.** It reads the implementation’s capped `YawRateDeg` statistic rather than measuring actor rotation; zero movement also passes. Sample actual yaw changes over elapsed simulation time and require meaningful progress towards the player as well as the rate limit.

12. **[P3] `AI/GBTentacleRigComponent.cpp:419` — Pose submission copies the whole skeleton per bone.** UE 5.9’s rotation setter builds full-pose copies in both its getter and setter, causing roughly 160 full-pose copies per Entity tick. Batch local bone transforms and mark the pose dirty once to keep submission proportional to bone count.

13. **[P3] `Tests/GBEntityTests.cpp:39,57,82` — Each test admits incorrect implementations.** Reach tests only equal-length, origin-rooted XY geometry; TooFar accepts reversed, folded collinear chains and never asserts a fixed root; SpringSettles accepts instantly snapping to the target. Add translated, unequal-length 3D and coincident-joint cases, assert forward segment projections, and verify transient spring position/velocity and timestep consistency.

No findings for crashes/null dereferences, post-`Destroy` timer/light cleanup, duplicate damage, other-enemy hooks, encounter/pickup/nav integration, or the JSON changes. Existing non-energy projectile behavior remains unchanged: the two extra components register, but the unused light does not tick or illuminate the scene. No loaded-object scans were found in the reviewed tick paths.

Read-only review; no files changed or builds run.
